function sanitize(str: unknown): string {
  return String(str ?? "")
    // eslint-disable-next-line no-control-regex
    .replace(/[\u0000-\u001F\u007F]/g, "")
    .trim();
}

function validate(options: Partial<DirectorEnhanceOptions>): string[] {
  const errors: string[] = [];

  if (!options.prompt || !String(options.prompt).trim()) {
    errors.push("Prompt is required and cannot be empty.");
  }

  if (options.duration !== undefined) {
    const duration = Number(options.duration);
    if (Number.isNaN(duration)) {
      errors.push("Duration must be a number.");
    } else if (duration < CONFIG.minDuration || duration > CONFIG.maxDuration) {
      errors.push(
        `Duration must be between ${CONFIG.minDuration} and ${CONFIG.maxDuration} seconds.`
      );
    }
  }

  if (
    options.aspectRatio &&
    !(CONFIG.supportedAspectRatios as readonly string[]).includes(options.aspectRatio)
  ) {
    errors.push(
      `Unsupported aspect ratio. Use one of: ${CONFIG.supportedAspectRatios.join(", ")}`
    );
  }

  if (
    options.fps !== undefined &&
    !(CONFIG.supportedFPS as readonly number[]).includes(Number(options.fps))
  ) {
    errors.push(`Unsupported FPS. Use one of: ${CONFIG.supportedFPS.join(", ")}`);
  }

  if (
    options.engine &&
    !ENGINES[String(options.engine).toLowerCase() as DirectorEngineId]
  ) {
    errors.push(`Unknown engine. Available: ${Object.keys(ENGINES).join(", ")}`);
  }

  return errors;
}

function buildTimeline(duration: number, beats?: TimelineBeat[] | null): string {
  const timelineBeats =
    Array.isArray(beats) && beats.length > 0 ? beats : DEFAULT_TIMELINE_BEATS;

  return timelineBeats
    .map((part) => {
      const start = Math.round(duration * part.start * 10) / 10;
      const end = Math.round(duration * part.end * 10) / 10;
      return `${start}s → ${end}s: ${sanitize(part.role)}`;
    })
    .join("\n");
}

function buildCameraModule(opts: {
  shotType?: DirectorShotTypeId | null;
  cameraMovements?: DirectorCameraMovementId[];
  lens?: string | null;
}): string {
  const { shotType, cameraMovements, lens } = opts;
  const lines = ["CAMERA DIRECTION:"];

  if (shotType && SHOT_TYPES[shotType]) {
    lines.push(`- Primary shot: ${SHOT_TYPES[shotType]}`);
  }

  if (Array.isArray(cameraMovements) && cameraMovements.length > 0) {
    const moves = cameraMovements
      .filter((m) => CAMERA_MOVEMENTS[m])
      .map((m) => CAMERA_MOVEMENTS[m]);
    if (moves.length) lines.push(`- Movement: ${moves.join("; ")}`);
  }

  if (lens && sanitize(lens)) {
    lines.push(`- Lens: ${sanitize(lens)}`);
  }

  lines.push(
    "- Professional cinematic camera language.",
    "- Smooth intentional camera movement with natural acceleration/deceleration.",
    "- Consistent framing and spatial orientation.",
    "- No random camera movement."
  );

  return lines.join("\n");
}

function buildPhysicsModule(): string {
  return [
    "PHYSICAL REALISM:",
    "- Physically plausible object movement.",
    "- Realistic gravity, water, wind, smoke, and particles.",
    "- Natural interaction between characters and environment.",
    "- No impossible transformations.",
  ].join("\n");
}

function buildLightingModule(lightingStyle?: string | null): string {
  const lines = [
    "CINEMATIC LIGHTING:",
    "- Physically coherent lighting.",
    "- Natural shadows and reflections.",
    "- Consistent exposure and color temperature.",
    "- Professional cinematic color grading.",
  ];
  if (lightingStyle && sanitize(lightingStyle)) {
    lines.push(`- Style: ${sanitize(lightingStyle)}`);
  }
  return lines.join("\n");
}

function buildContinuityModule(): string {
  return [
    "CONTINUITY CONTROL:",
    "- Maintain same characters, clothing, objects, environment, architecture, and spatial relationships.",
    "- Preserve visual identity across every frame.",
    "- No duplicated subjects.",
    "- No object morphing.",
    "- No unexplained scene changes.",
    "- No flickering or temporal inconsistency.",
  ].join("\n");
}

function buildAudioModule(audioStyle?: string | null): string {
  const lines = [
    "AUDIO DIRECTION:",
    "- Natural synchronized environmental sound.",
    "- Realistic ambience, weather, and environmental effects.",
    "- No unnecessary dialogue or music.",
  ];
  if (audioStyle && sanitize(audioStyle)) {
    lines.push(`- Style: ${sanitize(audioStyle)}`);
  }
  return lines.join("\n");
}

function buildGenreModule(genre?: DirectorGenreId | null): string {
  if (!genre || !GENRES[genre]) return "";
  const g = GENRES[genre];
  return [
    `GENRE STYLING (${g.name}):`,
    `- ${g.instructions}`,
    `- Color grading: ${g.grading}`,
  ].join("\n");
}

function buildMoodModule(mood?: DirectorMoodId | null): string {
  if (!mood || !MOODS[mood]) return "";
  return [
    "MOOD & ATMOSPHERE:",
    `- ${MOODS[mood]}`,
    "- Emotional tone must be consistent throughout.",
  ].join("\n");
}

function buildGradingModule(grading?: DirectorGradingId | null): string {
  if (!grading || !GRADING_PRESETS[grading]) return "";
  return ["COLOR GRADING:", `- ${GRADING_PRESETS[grading]}`].join("\n");
}

function buildNegativeModule(negatives?: string[]): string {
  const custom = Array.isArray(negatives)
    ? negatives.map(sanitize).filter(Boolean)
    : [];
  const all = [...new Set([...DEFAULT_DIRECTOR_NEGATIVES, ...custom])];

  return ["NEGATIVE PROMPT (avoid):", ...all.map((n) => `- ${n}`)].join("\n");
}

function buildReferenceModule(refs?: string[]): string {
  if (!Array.isArray(refs)) return "";
  const cleaned = refs.map(sanitize).filter(Boolean);
  if (cleaned.length === 0) return "";
  return [
    "REFERENCE FRAMES:",
    ...cleaned.map((r, i) => `- Ref ${i + 1}: ${r}`),
  ].join("\n");
}

function formatOutput(
  text: string,
  format: DirectorOutputFormat | string,
  meta: {
    engine: string;
    duration: number;
    fps: number;
    aspect: string;
    modules: string[];
    prompt: string;
  }
): string {
  switch (String(format).toLowerCase()) {
    case "json":
      return JSON.stringify(
        {
          version: "2.0",
          engine: meta.engine,
          duration: meta.duration,
          fps: meta.fps,
          aspectRatio: meta.aspect,
          prompt: meta.prompt,
          modules: meta.modules,
          fullPrompt: text,
        },
        null,
        2
      );

    case "plain":
      return text.replace(/\[([^\]]+)\]/g, "== $1 ==");

    case "markdown":
    default:
      return text;
  }
}

function enhance(options: DirectorEnhanceOptions): string {
  const errors = validate(options);
  if (errors.length) {
    throw new Error("Validation failed:\n- " + errors.join("\n- "));
  }

  const {
    prompt,
    duration = CONFIG.defaultDuration,
    engine = "generic",
    aspectRatio = CONFIG.defaultAspectRatio,
    fps = CONFIG.defaultFPS,
    genre = null,
    shotType = null,
    cameraMovements = [],
    lens = null,
    mood = null,
    grading = null,
    lightingStyle = null,
    audioStyle = null,
    camera = true,
    physics = true,
    lighting = true,
    sound = true,
    continuity = true,
    timeline = true,
    beats = null,
    negatives = [],
    references = [],
    language = "en",
    format = "markdown",
  } = options;

  const safeDuration = Math.min(
    CONFIG.maxDuration,
    Math.max(CONFIG.minDuration, Number(duration) || CONFIG.defaultDuration)
  );

  const selectedEngine =
    ENGINES[String(engine).toLowerCase() as DirectorEngineId] || ENGINES.generic;

  const safeAspect = (CONFIG.supportedAspectRatios as readonly string[]).includes(
    aspectRatio
  )
    ? aspectRatio
    : CONFIG.defaultAspectRatio;

  const safeFps = (CONFIG.supportedFPS as readonly number[]).includes(Number(fps))
    ? Number(fps)
    : CONFIG.defaultFPS;

  const cleanPrompt = sanitize(prompt);

  const modules: string[] = [];

  if (genre) {
    const m = buildGenreModule(genre);
    if (m) modules.push(m);
  }

  if (mood) {
    const m = buildMoodModule(mood);
    if (m) modules.push(m);
  }

  if (camera) {
    modules.push(buildCameraModule({ shotType, cameraMovements, lens }));
  }

  if (physics) modules.push(buildPhysicsModule());
  if (lighting) modules.push(buildLightingModule(lightingStyle));
  if (continuity) modules.push(buildContinuityModule());
  if (sound) modules.push(buildAudioModule(audioStyle));

  if (grading) {
    const m = buildGradingModule(grading);
    if (m) modules.push(m);
  }

  const timelineBlock = timeline
    ? [
        "TEMPORAL DIRECTING:",
        buildTimeline(safeDuration, beats),
        "",
        "The timeline describes one coherent scene.",
        "Preserve the original idea and action.",
        "Do not introduce unrelated events.",
        "Transitions must be physically and visually coherent.",
      ].join("\n")
    : "";

  const negativesBlock = buildNegativeModule(negatives);
  const referencesBlock = buildReferenceModule(references);

  const output = [
    "[ARABIPROMPT CINEMATIC DIRECTOR v2.0]",
    "",
    "VIDEO ENGINE: " + selectedEngine,
    "DURATION: " + safeDuration + " seconds",
    "FRAME RATE: " + safeFps + " FPS",
    "ASPECT RATIO: " + safeAspect,
    "LANGUAGE: " + (LANGUAGES[language]?.name || "English"),
    "",
    "[ORIGINAL CREATIVE IDEA]",
    cleanPrompt,
    "",
    "[DIRECTOR'S INSTRUCTIONS]",
    modules.filter(Boolean).join("\n\n"),
    "",
    timelineBlock,
    "",
    referencesBlock,
    "",
    "[VISUAL QUALITY]",
    "Ultra-realistic cinematic image.",
    "Professional composition.",
    "Natural motion blur.",
    "Detailed environmental textures.",
    "Consistent depth and perspective.",
    "High-quality temporal coherence.",
    "",
    negativesBlock,
    "",
    "[END OF CINEMATIC DIRECTOR]",
  ]
    .filter((block, idx, arr) => {
      // collapse consecutive empty blocks when optional modules like referencesBlock are ""
      if (block === "" && arr[idx - 1] === "") return false;
      return true;
    })
    .join("\n")
    .trim();

  return formatOutput(output, format, {
    engine: selectedEngine,
    duration: safeDuration,
    fps: safeFps,
    aspect: safeAspect,
    modules,
    prompt: cleanPrompt,
  });
}

export const ArabiCinematicDirector = {
  version: "2.0",
  enhance,
  buildTimeline,
  validate,
  sanitize,
  formatOutput,
  engines: ENGINES,
  genres: GENRES,
  shotTypes: SHOT_TYPES,
  cameraMovements: CAMERA_MOVEMENTS,
  gradingPresets: GRADING_PRESETS,
  moods: MOODS,
  languages: LANGUAGES,
  config: CONFIG,
};

export default ArabiCinematicDirector;


####################################################################################################
FILE: src/utils/promptAnalyzer.ts
####################################################################################################

import type { StudioFormState } from "../types/prompt";
import { SmartSceneGuard } from "./smartSceneGuard";

export interface PromptCheckItem {
  id: string;
  passed: boolean;
  labelEn: string;
  labelAr: string;
}

export interface PromptAnalysisReport {
  wordCount: number;
  checks: PromptCheckItem[];
  passedCount: number;
  totalCount: number;
}

export function analyzePromptQuality(
  form: StudioFormState,
  compiledText: string
): PromptAnalysisReport {
  const clean = SmartSceneGuard.strip(compiledText).trim();
  const wordCount = clean ? clean.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean).length : 0;

  const checks: PromptCheckItem[] = [
    {
      id: "subject",
      passed: form.subject.trim().length >= 3,
      labelEn: "Clear subject",
      labelAr: "تحديد الموضوع",
    },
    {
      id: "motion",
      passed:
        form.action.trim().length >= 3 ||
        /\b(walks|drifts|sweeps|rotates|glides|moves|flows|swirls)\b/i.test(clean),
      labelEn: "Motion & physics",
      labelAr: "الحركة والفيزياء",
    },
    {
      id: "camera",
      passed: Boolean(form.cameraMovement || form.cameraLens || form.camera.trim()),
      labelEn: "Camera & lens",
      labelAr: "الكاميرا والعدسة",
    },
    {
      id: "lighting",
      passed: Boolean(form.light.trim()),
      labelEn: "Lighting & mood",
      labelAr: "الإضاءة والمزاج",
    },
    {
      id: "length",
      passed: wordCount >= 35 && wordCount <= 160,
      labelEn: "Optimal length (35–160w)",
      labelAr: "طول متوازن",
    },
  ];

  const passedCount = checks.filter((c) => c.passed).length;

  return {
    wordCount,
    checks,
    passedCount,
    totalCount: checks.length,
  };
}


####################################################################################################
FILE: src/utils/cinematicPromptRules.ts
####################################################################################################

import type { EngineId } from "../types/prompt";

export type CinematicProvider = "wan" | "minimax" | "veo" | "universal";

const RULE_MARKER = "[CINEMATIC PRODUCTION RULES]";

/**
 * Engine-aware directing rules. Keep each rule concise and avoid forcing cuts,
 * audio, lenses, or timelines that the user did not request.
 */
const PROVIDER_RULES: Record<CinematicProvider, string> = {
  wan: `MODEL PROFILE — WAN 2.2:
- Prefer concrete visual descriptions and one clearly readable primary action.
- Describe motion as a simple cause-and-effect sequence; avoid stacking many simultaneous actions.
- For a single-shot request, keep one continuous camera move and one coherent environment.
- For storyboard requests, make each shot visually distinct and specify only the transition needed.
- Keep prompts signal-rich and concise; avoid contradictory camera, motion, and lighting instructions.
- Do not assume native audio generation; include sound direction only when the selected workflow supports audio.`,
  minimax: `MODEL PROFILE — MINIMAX / HAILUO:
- Prioritize the main subject, its physical action, and the camera movement.
- Use direct, observable motion verbs and a clear beginning, development, and end.
- Avoid excessive micro-actions, multiple competing camera commands, and unnecessary technical jargon.
- Keep one coherent shot unless the user explicitly requests a sequence of shots.
- Do not assume native audio generation; include sound direction only when the selected workflow supports audio.`,
  veo: `MODEL PROFILE — GOOGLE VEO:
- Use natural-language cinematic direction with clear subject, action, setting, and camera intent.
- Describe physical interactions and synchronized sound only when requested and supported by the selected Veo model.
- Keep dialogue exact and assign it to the correct speaker when dialogue is provided.
- For fast generation, prioritize one subject, one primary action, and a simple camera instruction.
- Respect the selected model's duration, aspect ratio, resolution, and audio capabilities; never imply unsupported settings.`,
  universal: `MODEL PROFILE — UNIVERSAL VIDEO:
- Make the subject, setting, primary action, and camera intent unambiguous.
- Prefer observable details over abstract adjectives.
- Keep actions physically plausible and temporally ordered.
- Do not invent dialogue, sound, props, characters, or scene changes that the user did not request.`,
};

export const CinematicPromptRules = {
  version: "1.0.0",

  resolveProvider(engine: EngineId | string): CinematicProvider {
    const value = String(engine || "").toLowerCase();
    if (value.includes("wan")) return "wan";
    if (value.includes("minimax") || value.includes("hailuo")) return "minimax";
    if (value.includes("veo") || value.includes("google")) return "veo";
    return "universal";
  },

  build(engine: EngineId | string): string {
    const provider = this.resolveProvider(engine);
    return `${RULE_MARKER}

${PROVIDER_RULES[provider]}

GLOBAL FILM-DIRECTING RULES:
- SUBJECT: establish the primary subject and preserve its visible identity and defining attributes.
- ENVIRONMENT: establish a coherent location, spatial layout, foreground, background, weather, and important objects only as relevant.
- ACTION: describe one primary action with a readable progression; secondary actions must support, not compete with, it.
- CAMERA: specify framing and movement only when useful; do not combine incompatible movements or angles.
- LIGHT: keep light direction, color, exposure, and reflections coherent throughout the shot.
- PHYSICS: preserve believable gravity, momentum, collisions, fluid motion, cloth, hair, smoke, and environmental interaction when present.
- TEMPORAL ORDER: events must happen in a clear order; use timestamps only when the user requests timed beats or a storyboard needs them.
- CONTINUITY: preserve identity, wardrobe, props, geography, screen direction, and lighting within a continuous shot.
- EDITING: one continuous take by default; use cuts or transitions only when multiple shots are explicitly requested.
- ENDING: finish the requested action naturally; do not append an unrelated event or a new scene.
- FIDELITY: preserve the user's idea and do not add decorative details that change its meaning.

PROMPT CONFLICT CHECK:
Resolve contradictions by prioritizing the user's explicit creative instructions, then the selected model's supported capabilities, then these general production rules.

[END CINEMATIC PRODUCTION RULES]`;
  },

  seriesBibleRules: `SERIES / FILM CONTINUITY BIBLE:
- Treat the supplied base context as the canonical visual bible for the project.
- Preserve recurring character identity, age appearance, face, hairstyle, wardrobe, accessories, and distinctive traits unless the script explicitly changes them.
- Preserve established locations, architecture, geography, color palette, lens language, lighting mood, and recurring props.
- Track each scene's starting state and ending state: character position, pose, gaze, prop placement, screen direction, time of day, and unresolved action.
- The next scene must follow the requested story logic. Continue an action seamlessly only when it is the same moment; otherwise use a motivated time or location transition.
- Do not repeat the previous scene's action, reset character positions without reason, or carry transient effects into a new scene.
- Keep each generated scene independently renderable while preserving the series-level visual identity.
- Do not change established facts or invent backstory, dialogue, characters, or props that conflict with the user's material.`,

  strip(prompt: string): string {
    if (!prompt || typeof prompt !== "string") return "";
    return prompt
      .replace(/\n*\[CINEMATIC PRODUCTION RULES\][\s\S]*?\[END CINEMATIC PRODUCTION RULES\]/g, "")
      .replace(/\n{3,}/g, "\n\n")
      .trim();
  },
};

import {
  AUDIO_DESIGN_TAGS,
  ENGINES,
  HAILUO_CAMERA_BRACKETS,
  PHYSICS_HINTS,
  WAN_VARIANTS,
} from "../data/engines";
import type {
  EngineId,
  GenerationMode,
  ShotItem,
  StudioFormState,
  WanVariant,
} from "../types/prompt";
import {
  compileGoogleVeoFromStudio,
  type GoogleVideoPromptResult,
} from "./googleVeoPrompt";
import { SmartSceneGuard } from "./smartSceneGuard";

const DEFAULT_SHOT_ROLES = [
  {
    role: "Establishing Wide / Reveal",
    camera: "Slow cinematic push-in",
    description: "Establish subject and atmospheric scale with smooth initial motion.",
  },
  {
    role: "Macro Texture & Detail",
    camera: "Extreme macro tracking glide",
    description: "Cut to close-up highlighting intricate material textures and light reflections.",
  },
  {
    role: "Dynamic Orbit / Action Peak",
    camera: "Smooth orbital arc movement",
    description: "Capture the peak physical motion from a dramatic angle with shallow depth of field.",
  },
  {
    role: "Hero Final Pose & Lighting",
    camera: "Locked-off hero frame with subtle pull-back",
    description: "Settle into the iconic final composition as rim light sweeps across the silhouette.",
  },
];

export function getExactFrameCount(
  engine: EngineId,
  durationSec: number,
  wanVariant: WanVariant = "a14b"
): number {
  if (engine === "wan22") {
    const variantSpec = WAN_VARIANTS[wanVariant];
    const match = variantSpec.durationOptions.find((d) => d.value === durationSec);
    if (match) return match.frames;
    return wanVariant === "ti2v_5b" ? 193 : 129;
  }
  if (engine === "minimax") {
    return durationSec <= 6 ? 151 : 251;
  }
  if (engine === "hunyuan") {
    return durationSec <= 5 ? 129 : 193;
  }
  if (engine === "ltx") {
    if (durationSec <= 4) return 97;
    if (durationSec <= 5) return 121;
    return 161;
  }
  if (engine === "veo") {
    return Math.max(24, Math.round((durationSec || 8) * 24));
  }
  return 1;
}

export function getEngineFps(engine: EngineId, wanVariant: WanVariant = "a14b"): number {
  if (engine === "wan22") {
    return WAN_VARIANTS[wanVariant].fps;
  }
  if (engine === "minimax") {
    return 25;
  }
  return 24;
}

export function buildDefaultShots(count: number, existing: ShotItem[] = []): ShotItem[] {
  const safeCount = Math.max(1, Math.min(10, count));
  const shots: ShotItem[] = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < safeCount; i++) {
    const template = DEFAULT_SHOT_ROLES[i % DEFAULT_SHOT_ROLES.length];
    const prev = existing[i];
    shots.push({
      id: i + 1,
      role: template.role,
      camera: prev?.camera ?? "",
      description: prev?.description ?? "",
    });
  }
  return shots;
}

export function buildSmartNegative(
  engine: EngineId,
  mode: GenerationMode,
  category: string
): string {
  if (engine === "flux1" || engine === "minimax") {
    return "";
  }

  const baseVideo = [
    "blurry",
    "low resolution",
    "temporal flickering",
    "frame jitter",
    "morphing geometry",
    "inconsistent object volume",
    "deformed textures",
    "unwanted camera shake",
    "oversaturated neon artifacts",
    "plastic skin",
    "watermark",
    "text overlay",
    "subtitles",
    "MPEG compression blocks",
  ];

  const baseImage = [
    "lowres",
    "bad anatomy",
    "bad proportions",
    "extra fingers",
    "mutated hands",
    "blurry background",
    "plastic skin",
    "oversharpened",
    "chromatic aberration artifacts",
    "watermark",
    "signature",
    "text overlay",
    "JPEG artifacts",
  ];

  const byCategory: Record<string, string[]> = {
    "Luxury Product Advertisement": [
      "cheap plastic material",
      "dull specular highlights",
      "asymmetrical bottle",
    ],
    "Technology Commercial": [
      "melting ports",
      "warped screen bezels",
      "flickering LEDs",
    ],
    "Perfume Advertisement": [
      "cloudy glass",
      "distorted liquid refraction",
      "warped cap",
    ],
    "Automotive Commercial": [
      "oval wheels",
      "rubber car body",
      "warped road lines",
    ],
    "Fashion Editorial Film": [
      "extra fingers",
      "mutated hands",
      "garment clipping",
      "uncanny face",
    ],
    "Real Estate & Luxury Hospitality": [
      "warped architectural lines",
      "slanted walls",
      "unnatural water ripples",
    ],
    "Cyberpunk & Sci-Fi Cinema": [
      "flat lighting",
      "muddy shadows",
      "broken perspective",
    ],
    "Moroccan Architectural & Documentary": [
      "warped zellige geometry",
      "broken arches",
      "unnatural water waves",
    ],
    "Anime & Stylized Makoto Shinkai": [
      "3D CGI uncanny look",
      "muddy lineart",
      "inconsistent cel shading",
    ],
    "Food & Macro Beverage": [
      "unappetizing grey tones",
      "unnatural fluid gravity",
      "static steam",
    ],
    "Vocal & Music Performance": [
      "lip desync",
      "static mouth",
      "warped teeth",
      "uncanny eyes",
      "frozen expression",
    ],
    "Editorial Portrait Photography": [
      "airbrushed wax skin",
      "asymmetric eyes",
      "artificial studio glare",
    ],
  };

  const isVideo = ENGINES[engine].kind === "video";
  const base = isVideo ? baseVideo : baseImage;

  const modeExtra =
    mode === "i2v" || mode === "flf2v" || mode === "img2img" || mode === "s2v"
      ? ["identity drift", "subject redesign", "background hallucination", "sudden cut"]
      : ["empty background", "missing hero subject"];

  return [...base, ...(byCategory[category] || []), ...modeExtra].join(", ");
}

function cleanSegment(text: string): string {
  return (text || "").trim().replace(/[.,;\s]+$/, "");
}

function capitalizeFirst(text: string): string {
  const s = (text || "").trim();
  return s ? s[0].toUpperCase() + s.slice(1) : s;
}

function normalizeProse(text: string): string {
  return text
    .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
    .replace(/\.\s*\./g, ".")
    .replace(/,\s*,/g, ",")
    .replace(/\s+([.,;:])/g, "$1")
    .trim();
}

/**
 * دمج مراحل حركة الكاميرا في مسار سينمائي متصل بدلاً من تكرار كلمة "then"
 */
function buildSmoothMiniMaxSequence(
  action: string,
  camera: string,
  shots: ShotItem[],
  effectiveShotsCount: number
): string {
  if (effectiveShotsCount <= 1) {
    return action ? capitalizeFirst(action) : "";
  }

  const activeShots = shots.slice(0, effectiveShotsCount);
  const stages: string[] = [];

  activeShots.forEach((s, idx) => {
    const sCam = cleanSegment(s.camera);
    const sDesc = cleanSegment(s.description);
    if (idx === 0) {
      const firstText = capitalizeFirst(action || sDesc);
      if (firstText) stages.push(firstText);
    } else if (sDesc) {
      const framingNote =
        sCam && sCam.toLowerCase() !== camera.toLowerCase()
          ? ` into a ${sCam.toLowerCase()}`
          : "";
      if (idx === 1) {
        stages.push(
          `transitioning smoothly${framingNote} as ${sDesc.charAt(0).toLowerCase() + sDesc.slice(1)}`
        );
      } else {
        stages.push(
          `and settling intimately${framingNote} to ${sDesc.charAt(0).toLowerCase() + sDesc.slice(1)}`
        );
      }
    }
  });

  return stages.join(", ");
}

export interface CompiledPromptResult {
  fullPrompt: string;
  positiveOnly: string;
  negativeOnly: string;
  audioBlock: string;
  guardBlock?: string;
  words: number;
  positiveWords: number;
  chars: number;
  tokens: number;
  veoMeta?: GoogleVideoPromptResult["meta"];
  veoSettings?: GoogleVideoPromptResult["settings"];
}

export function compileEnginePrompt(state: StudioFormState): CompiledPromptResult {
  const guardEnabled = state.smartSceneGuard !== false;
  const providerKey = SmartSceneGuard.resolveProvider(state.engine);

  if (state.engine === "veo") {
    const veoRes = compileGoogleVeoFromStudio(state);
    const cleanVeoPrompt = guardEnabled
      ? SmartSceneGuard.apply(veoRes.prompt, providerKey, false)
      : SmartSceneGuard.strip(veoRes.prompt);
    const veoNegative = guardEnabled
      ? SmartSceneGuard.mergeNegativePrompt(state.negative.trim())
      : state.negative.trim();
    const words = cleanVeoPrompt.trim().split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean).length;

    return {
      fullPrompt: cleanVeoPrompt,
      positiveOnly: cleanVeoPrompt,
      negativeOnly: veoNegative,
      audioBlock: "",
      guardBlock: guardEnabled ? SmartSceneGuard.getGuardMetadataBlock(providerKey) : "",
      words,
      positiveWords: words,
      chars: cleanVeoPrompt.length,
      tokens: Math.round(words * 1.32),
      veoMeta: {
        ...veoRes.meta,
        promptChars: cleanVeoPrompt.length,
      },
      veoSettings: veoRes.settings,
    };
  }

  const spec = ENGINES[state.engine];
  const promptFormat = state.promptFormat || "official";
  const subject = cleanSegment(state.subject);
  const environment = cleanSegment(state.environment);
  const action = cleanSegment(state.action);
  const extra = cleanSegment(state.extra);
  const camera = cleanSegment(state.camera);
  const light = cleanSegment(state.light);
  const style = cleanSegment(state.style);
  const category = cleanSegment(state.category);
  const lora = state.loraTags.trim();

  // دمج الكلمات السلبية الخاصة بـ SmartSceneGuard في حقل الـ Negative فقط وليس داخل البرومت البصري
  const rawNegative = spec.supportsNegative ? state.negative.trim() : "";
  const negative =
    spec.supportsNegative && guardEnabled && spec.kind === "video"
      ? SmartSceneGuard.mergeNegativePrompt(rawNegative)
      : rawNegative;

  const physicsPhrases = PHYSICS_HINTS.filter((h) =>
    state.physicsHints.includes(h.id)
  ).map((h) => h.phrase);
  const combinedMicro = [extra, ...physicsPhrases].filter(Boolean).join(", ");

  const audioPhrases = AUDIO_DESIGN_TAGS.filter((t) =>
    state.audioTags.includes(t.id)
  ).map((t) => t.phrase);
  const audioLine = [cleanSegment(state.audioDescription), ...audioPhrases]
    .filter(Boolean)
    .join("; ");

  // فصل الصوت ليظهر في بطاقته المستقلة أو للمحركات الداعمة للصوت دون تلويث برومت MiniMax
  const audioBlock =
    spec.kind === "video" && state.includeAudio && audioLine
      ? `AUDIO & SOUND DESIGN:\n${audioLine}.`
      : "";

  const motionDesc =
    state.motionScale <= 3
      ? "subtle, gentle physical movement"
      : state.motionScale <= 7
        ? "smooth, natural motion with realistic inertia"
        : "dynamic, high-energy cinematic motion";

  const exactFrames = getExactFrameCount(state.engine, state.duration, state.wanVariant);
  const fps = getEngineFps(state.engine, state.wanVariant);
  const hasShotContent = state.shots
    .slice(0, state.shotsCount)
    .some((s) => cleanSegment(s?.camera || "") || cleanSegment(s?.description || ""));
  const effectiveShotsCount =
    state.shotsCount > 1 && hasShotContent ? state.shotsCount : 1;

  const lead = [category ? `${capitalizeFirst(category)} style` : "", style]
    .filter(Boolean)
    .join(", ");
  const camSentence = camera ? `Camera: ${camera}.` : "";
  const lightSentence = light ? `Lighting: ${light}.` : "";
  const subjEnv = [capitalizeFirst(subject), environment].filter(Boolean).join(", ");

  let positive = "";

  if (promptFormat === "official") {
    if (state.engine === "wan22") {
      const microTail = combinedMicro ? ` ${capitalizeFirst(combinedMicro)}.` : "";

      if (state.mode === "i2v") {
        positive = [
          `Keeping the subject, composition and lighting of the reference image${subject ? ` (${subject})` : ""}${action ? `, ${action}` : ""}, ${motionDesc}.`,
          camSentence,
          lightSentence,
          microTail.trim(),
          lead ? `${lead}.` : "",
        ]
          .filter(Boolean)
          .join(" ");
      } else if (state.mode === "s2v") {
        positive = [
          `Audio-driven vocal performance keeping the character identity from the reference image${subject ? ` (${subject})` : ""}${environment ? `, set ${environment}` : ""}.`,
          `${action ? `${capitalizeFirst(action)}, ` : ""}synchronized lip-sync and facial micro-expressions, ${motionDesc}.`,
          camSentence,
          lightSentence,
          microTail.trim(),
          lead ? `${lead}.` : "",
        ]
          .filter(Boolean)
          .join(" ");
      } else if (state.mode === "flf2v") {
        positive = [
          `A seamless transition from the first frame to the last frame${subject ? `: ${subject}` : ""}.`,
          action ? `${capitalizeFirst(action)}, ${motionDesc}.` : `${capitalizeFirst(motionDesc)}.`,
          camSentence,
          lightSentence,
          microTail.trim(),
          lead ? `${lead}.` : "",
        ]
          .filter(Boolean)
          .join(" ");
      } else if (effectiveShotsCount <= 1) {
        positive = [
          lead ? `${lead}.` : "",
          subjEnv ? `${subjEnv}.` : "",
          action ? `${capitalizeFirst(action)}, ${motionDesc}.` : "",
          camSentence,
          lightSentence,
          microTail.trim(),
        ]
          .filter(Boolean)
          .join(" ");
      } else {
        const parts: string[] = [];
        for (let i = 0; i < effectiveShotsCount; i++) {
          const shot = state.shots[i];
          const shotCam = cleanSegment(shot?.camera || "") || camera;
          const shotDesc = cleanSegment(shot?.description || "");
          if (i === 0) {
            parts.push(
              [
                `Shot 1: ${subjEnv}.`,
                action ? `${capitalizeFirst(action)}, ${motionDesc}.` : "",
                shotDesc ? `${capitalizeFirst(shotDesc)}.` : "",
                shotCam ? `Camera: ${shotCam}.` : "",
              ]
                .filter(Boolean)
                .join(" ")
            );
          } else {
            parts.push(
              [
                `Cut to shot ${i + 1}: ${shotDesc ? `${capitalizeFirst(shotDesc)}.` : ""}`,
                shotCam ? `Camera: ${shotCam}.` : "",
              ]
                .filter(Boolean)
                .join(" ")
            );
          }
        }
        positive = [
          lead ? `${lead}.` : "",
          parts.join(" "),
          light ? `Consistent lighting across all shots: ${light}.` : "",
          microTail.trim(),
        ]
          .filter(Boolean)
          .join(" ");
      }
      if (lora) positive = `${lora} ${positive}`;
      positive = normalizeProse(positive);
    } else if (state.engine === "minimax") {
      const customCmds = (state.minimaxCameraCommands || []).filter(Boolean).slice(0, 3);
      const bracketCam =
        customCmds.length > 0
          ? `[${customCmds.join(", ")}]`
          : HAILUO_CAMERA_BRACKETS[state.cameraMovement] ||
            (camera ? `[${camera}]` : "");
      const customDirective = cleanSegment(state.minimaxCustomDirective || "");
      const modePrefix =
        state.mode === "i2v"
          ? "Preserving the exact subject identity and lighting of the first reference frame, "
          : state.mode === "flf2v"
            ? "Smoothly bridging from the first reference frame to the target last frame, "
            : "";

      const sequenceLine = buildSmoothMiniMaxSequence(
        action,
        camera,
        state.shots,
        effectiveShotsCount
      );

      // Avoid repeating camera movement if bracketCam or sequenceLine already handles cinematography
      const shouldAppendCamera = Boolean(camera) && effectiveShotsCount <= 1 && !bracketCam;
      const tailDetails = [customDirective, combinedMicro].filter(Boolean).join(", ");
      positive = normalizeProse(
        [
          `${bracketCam ? `${bracketCam} ` : ""}${modePrefix}${subjEnv}.`,
          sequenceLine ? `${sequenceLine}, with ${motionDesc}.` : "",
          shouldAppendCamera ? `${camera}.` : "",
          light ? `${light}.` : "",
          style ? `${style}.` : "",
          tailDetails ? `${capitalizeFirst(tailDetails)}.` : "",
        ]
          .filter(Boolean)
          .join(" ")
      );
    } else if (state.engine === "hunyuan") {
      const prefix =
        state.mode === "i2v"
          ? "Anchored to the reference frame, preserving subject identity and scene lighting, "
          : "";
      const seq =
        effectiveShotsCount > 1
          ? state.shots
              .slice(0, effectiveShotsCount)
              .map((s, idx) => {
                const sCam = cleanSegment(s.camera) || camera;
                const sDesc = cleanSegment(s.description) || action;
                const body = idx === 0 ? capitalizeFirst(action || sDesc) : sDesc;
                return sCam ? `${body} (${sCam})` : body;
              })
              .filter(Boolean)
              .join(", transitioning to ")
          : [action, camera ? `Camera: ${camera}` : ""].filter(Boolean).join(". ");
      positive = normalizeProse(
        [
          lora,
          `${prefix}${[capitalizeFirst(category), style].filter(Boolean).join(", ")}${category || style ? "." : ""}`,
          subject
            ? `${capitalizeFirst(subject)}${environment ? ` set in ${environment}` : ""}.`
            : environment
              ? `${capitalizeFirst(environment)}.`
              : "",
          seq ? `${capitalizeFirst(seq)}, ${motionDesc}.` : "",
          lightSentence,
          combinedMicro ? `${capitalizeFirst(combinedMicro)}.` : "",
        ]
          .filter(Boolean)
          .join(" ")
      );
    } else if (state.engine === "ltx") {
      const modeIntro =
        state.mode === "i2v"
          ? "Animate the reference image smoothly while preserving exact composition."
          : state.mode === "flf2v"
            ? "Smoothly interpolate motion between the first frame and last frame."
            : "";
      positive = normalizeProse(
        [
          modeIntro,
          camera ? `${camera}.` : "",
          subject
            ? `${capitalizeFirst(subject)}${environment ? ` in ${environment}` : ""}.`
            : environment
              ? `${capitalizeFirst(environment)}.`
              : "",
          action ? `${capitalizeFirst(action)}, ${motionDesc}.` : "",
          light ? `${light}.` : "",
          style ? `${style}.` : "",
          combinedMicro ? `${capitalizeFirst(combinedMicro)}.` : "",
        ]
          .filter(Boolean)
          .join(" ")
      );
    } else if (state.engine === "flux1") {
      const parts: string[] = [];
      if (lora) parts.push(lora);
      if (category) parts.push(`${category} photograph.`);
      if (subjEnv) parts.push(`${subjEnv}.`);
      if (action) parts.push(`${capitalizeFirst(action)}.`);
      if (camera) parts.push(`Framed with ${camera}.`);
      if (light) parts.push(`Illuminated by ${light}.`);
      if (style) parts.push(`${style}.`);
      if (combinedMicro) parts.push(`Fine material and optical details: ${combinedMicro}.`);
      positive = normalizeProse(parts.join(" "));
    } else {
      const tags: string[] = [];
      if (lora) tags.push(lora);
      tags.push("masterpiece", "best quality", "highly detailed");
      if (subject) tags.push(`(${subject}:1.2)`);
      if (environment) tags.push(environment);
      if (action) tags.push(`(${action}:1.1)`);
      if (camera) tags.push(camera);
      if (light) tags.push(light);
      if (style) tags.push(style);
      if (combinedMicro) tags.push(combinedMicro);
      positive = tags.filter(Boolean).join(", ");
    }
  } else {
    // Structured studio production brief format
    if (state.engine === "wan22") {
      const variantLabel = state.wanVariant === "ti2v_5b" ? "WAN 2.2 TI2V-5B" : "WAN 2.2 A14B";
      positive += `[${variantLabel} · ${state.aspect} · ${state.duration}s (${exactFrames} frames @ ${fps}fps) · Seed ${state.seed}]\n`;
      if (style || category) {
        positive += `${[style, category].filter(Boolean).join(". ")}.\n\n`;
      }

      if (state.mode === "t2v" || state.mode === "film") {
        positive += `HERO SUBJECT & SCENE ARCHITECTURE:\n`;
        positive += `${[subject, environment ? `Set within ${environment}` : ""].filter(Boolean).join(". ")}.\n`;
        if (light || combinedMicro) {
          positive += `${light ? `Lighting & Atmosphere: ${light}.` : ""}${combinedMicro ? ` Micro-physics: ${combinedMicro}.` : ""}\n\n`;
        } else {
          positive += `\n`;
        }
      } else if (state.mode === "i2v") {
        positive += `IMAGE-TO-VIDEO CONTINUITY LOCK:\n`;
        positive += `Anchor strictly to the input reference frame. Preserve exact subject geometry, surface shaders, facial/product identity, and lighting direction.${subject ? ` Subject focus: ${subject}.` : ""}\n\n`;
      } else if (state.mode === "s2v") {
        positive += `AUDIO-DRIVEN SPEECH / VOCAL PERFORMANCE (Wan2.2-S2V-14B):\n`;
        positive += `Synchronize lip articulation, jaw cadence, and emotional facial expressions directly to the reference audio stem while preserving character identity.${subject ? ` Performer: ${subject}.` : ""}\n\n`;
      } else {
        positive += `FIRST-TO-LAST FRAME TEMPORAL BRIDGE:\n`;
        positive += `Interpolate seamlessly from the starting reference frame to the target end frame with coherent 3D spatial transformation.${subject ? ` ${subject}.` : ""}\n\n`;
      }

      if (effectiveShotsCount <= 1) {
        positive += `CONTINUOUS TEMPORAL SEQUENCE (0.0s - ${state.duration}.0s):\n`;
        if (action) positive += `Action: ${action}.\n`;
        positive += `${camera ? `Cinematography: ${camera}. ` : ""}Motion dynamics (${state.motionScale}/10): ${motionDesc}.\n`;
        if (light || combinedMicro) {
          positive += `${light ? `Lighting behavior: ${light}.` : ""}${combinedMicro ? ` ${combinedMicro}.` : ""}\n\n`;
        } else {
          positive += `\n`;
        }
      } else {
        positive += `MULTI-SHOT STORYBOARD BREAKDOWN (${effectiveShotsCount} Cuts across ${state.duration}s):\n`;
        const seg = state.duration / effectiveShotsCount;
        for (let i = 0; i < effectiveShotsCount; i++) {
          const s = (i * seg).toFixed(1);
          const e = ((i + 1) * seg).toFixed(1);
          const shot = state.shots[i];
          const customCam = cleanSegment(shot?.camera || "") || camera;
          const customDesc = cleanSegment(shot?.description || "") || action;
          positive += `• SHOT ${i + 1} [${s}s - ${e}s]: `;
          if (i === 0) {
            positive += `${[action, customDesc, camera ? `Camera: ${camera}` : ""].filter(Boolean).join(". ")}.\n`;
          } else {
            positive += `${[customDesc, customCam ? `Camera: ${customCam}` : ""].filter(Boolean).join(". ")}.\n`;
          }
        }
        if (light || combinedMicro) {
          positive += `${light ? `Global Lighting & Micro-Physics: Maintain ${light} across all cuts.` : "Micro-Physics:"}${combinedMicro ? ` ${combinedMicro}.` : ""}\n\n`;
        } else {
          positive += `\n`;
        }
      }

      positive += `MOTION & TEMPORAL QUALITY:\n`;
      positive += `${capitalizeFirst(motionDesc)}, natural acceleration and deceleration curves, consistent 3D volume, zero temporal flickering, realistic depth of field.`;
    } else if (state.engine === "minimax") {
      const customCmds = (state.minimaxCameraCommands || []).filter(Boolean).slice(0, 3);
      const bracketCam =
        customCmds.length > 0
          ? `[${customCmds.join(", ")}]`
          : HAILUO_CAMERA_BRACKETS[state.cameraMovement] ||
            (camera ? `[${camera}]` : "");
      const resLabel =
        state.minimaxResolution || (state.aspect.includes("1080p") ? "1080P" : "768P");
      const customDirective = cleanSegment(state.minimaxCustomDirective || "");
      positive += `[MINIMAX HAILUO 02 · ${resLabel} · ${state.duration}s (${exactFrames} frames @ 25fps) · Optimizer ${state.minimaxPromptOptimizer ? "ON" : "OFF"}]\n`;
      if (bracketCam || camera) {
        positive += `Camera Directive: ${[bracketCam, camera ? `(${camera})` : ""].filter(Boolean).join(" ")}\n`;
      }
      if (subjEnv) {
        positive += `Subject & Environment: ${subjEnv}.\n`;
      }
      if (state.mode === "i2v") {
        positive += `First-Frame Anchor: Maintain exact visual continuity from the uploaded first frame.\n`;
      } else if (state.mode === "flf2v") {
        positive += `First-Last-Frame Bridge: Transition coherently between the uploaded first and last frames.\n`;
      }
      if (effectiveShotsCount > 1) {
        positive += `Temporal Beats:\n`;
        for (let i = 0; i < effectiveShotsCount; i++) {
          const shot = state.shots[i];
          const sCam = cleanSegment(shot?.camera || "") || camera;
          const sDesc = cleanSegment(shot?.description || "") || action;
          positive += `• Beat ${i + 1}: ${i === 0 ? action || sDesc : sDesc}${sCam ? ` [${sCam}]` : ""}\n`;
        }
      } else if (action) {
        positive += `Physical Motion: ${action}, ${motionDesc}.\n`;
      }
      if (customDirective) {
        positive += `Hailuo Physics & Nuance: ${customDirective}.\n`;
      }
      if (light || style || combinedMicro) {
        positive += `Lighting & Grade: ${[light, style, combinedMicro].filter(Boolean).join(". ")}.`;
      }
    } else if (state.engine === "hunyuan") {
      positive += `[HUNYUANVIDEO · ${state.aspect} · ${state.duration}s (${exactFrames} frames) · Guidance ${state.cfgScale} · Seed ${state.seed}]\n`;
      if (category || style) {
        positive += `${[category, style ? `captured in ${style}` : ""].filter(Boolean).join(", ")}. `;
      }
      if (state.mode === "i2v") {
        positive += `Anchored directly to the reference frame, maintaining subject identity and background continuity. `;
      }
      positive += `${subject}${environment ? ` positioned within ${environment}` : ""}.\n\n`;

      if (effectiveShotsCount > 1) {
        const seg = state.duration / effectiveShotsCount;
        positive += `Sequence Progression:\n`;
        for (let i = 0; i < effectiveShotsCount; i++) {
          const s = (i * seg).toFixed(1);
          const e = ((i + 1) * seg).toFixed(1);
          const shot = state.shots[i];
          const sCam = cleanSegment(shot?.camera || "") || camera;
          const sDesc = cleanSegment(shot?.description || "") || action;
          positive += `Segment ${i + 1} (${s}s-${e}s): ${i === 0 ? action || sDesc : sDesc}${sCam ? ` Captured with ${sCam}` : ""}.\n`;
        }
        positive += "\n";
      } else if (action || camera) {
        positive += `${action ? `Temporal Action: ${action}. ` : ""}${camera ? `Camera Trajectory: ${camera}.` : ""}\n\n`;
      }

      positive += `Lighting & Physics: ${[light, motionDesc].filter(Boolean).join(". ")}.${combinedMicro ? ` Physical nuances: ${combinedMicro}.` : ""}`;
    } else if (state.engine === "ltx") {
      positive += `[LTX-VIDEO 0.9.5 · ${state.aspect} · ${state.duration}s (${exactFrames} frames) · CFG ${state.cfgScale} · Seed ${state.seed}]\n`;
      if (state.mode === "i2v") {
        positive += `Animate the reference image smoothly while preserving exact composition. `;
      } else if (state.mode === "flf2v") {
        positive += `Smoothly interpolate motion between start frame and end frame. `;
      }
      positive += [
        camera ? `${camera}.` : "",
        `${subject}${environment ? ` in ${environment}` : ""}.`,
        action ? `${action}.` : "",
        light ? `${light}.` : "",
        style ? `${style}.` : "",
        combinedMicro ? `${combinedMicro}.` : "",
        `${motionDesc}.`,
      ]
        .filter(Boolean)
        .join(" ");
    } else if (state.engine === "flux1") {
      const parts: string[] = [];
      if (lora) parts.push(lora);
      if (category) parts.push(`${category} photograph.`);
      if (subjEnv) parts.push(`${subjEnv}.`);
      if (action) parts.push(`${action}.`);
      if (camera) parts.push(`Framed with ${camera}.`);
      if (light) parts.push(`Illuminated by ${light}.`);
      if (style) parts.push(`${style}.`);
      if (combinedMicro) parts.push(`Fine material and optical details: ${combinedMicro}.`);
      positive = parts.join(" ");
    } else {
      const tags: string[] = [];
      if (lora) tags.push(lora);
      tags.push("masterpiece", "best quality", "highly detailed");
      if (subject) tags.push(`(${subject}:1.2)`);
      if (environment) tags.push(environment);
      if (action) tags.push(`(${action}:1.1)`);
      if (camera) tags.push(camera);
      if (light) tags.push(light);
      if (style) tags.push(style);
      if (combinedMicro) tags.push(combinedMicro);
      positive = `[SDXL · ${state.aspect} · CFG ${state.cfgScale} · ${state.steps} Steps · Seed ${state.seed}]\n${tags.filter(Boolean).join(", ")}`;
    }
  }

  const basePositive = positive.trim();
  const isVideo = spec.kind === "video";
  const positiveOnly =
    isVideo && guardEnabled && basePositive
      ? SmartSceneGuard.apply(basePositive, providerKey, false)
      : basePositive;

  // لا ندمج الصوت داخل fullPrompt إذا كان المحرك MiniMax أو محركاً صامتاً، مع إبقاء audioBlock متاحاً للنسخ المنفصل
  const shouldAppendAudioToFull =
    Boolean(audioBlock) && state.engine === "wan22" && state.mode === "s2v";

  const sections = [positiveOnly];
  if (shouldAppendAudioToFull) sections.push(audioBlock);
  if (spec.supportsNegative && negative) sections.push(`NEGATIVE PROMPT:\n${negative}`);
  const fullPrompt = sections.join("\n\n");

  const words = fullPrompt.trim().split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean).length;
  const positiveWords = positiveOnly
    ? positiveOnly.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean).length
    : 0;

  return {
    fullPrompt,
    positiveOnly,
    negativeOnly: negative,
    audioBlock,
    guardBlock:
      isVideo && guardEnabled ? SmartSceneGuard.getGuardMetadataBlock(providerKey) : "",
    words,
    positiveWords,
    chars: fullPrompt.length,
    tokens: Math.round(words * 1.32),
  };
}

export function buildComfyExportPayload(
  state: StudioFormState,
  positiveOnly: string,
  negativeOnly: string
) {
  const spec = ENGINES[state.engine];
  const resMatch = state.aspect.match(/\((\d+)x(\d+)\)/);
  const width = resMatch ? Number(resMatch[1]) : 1280;
  const height = resMatch ? Number(resMatch[2]) : 720;
  const numFrames = getExactFrameCount(state.engine, state.duration, state.wanVariant);
  const fps = getEngineFps(state.engine, state.wanVariant);

  return {
    generator: "VideoPrompts Studio",
    timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
    target_tool: {
      id: spec.id,
      name: spec.name,
      ...(state.engine === "wan22"
        ? {
            variant: state.wanVariant,
            checkpoint:
              state.mode === "s2v"
                ? "Wan-AI/Wan2.2-S2V-14B"
                : WAN_VARIANTS[state.wanVariant].modelRepo,
          }
        : state.engine === "minimax"
          ? {
              model: "MiniMax-Hailuo-02",
              resolution:
                state.minimaxResolution ||
                (state.aspect.includes("1080p") ? "1080P" : "768P"),
              prompt_optimizer: Boolean(state.minimaxPromptOptimizer),
              camera_commands: state.minimaxCameraCommands || [],
            }
          : state.engine === "veo"
            ? {
                model:
                  state.veoModel === "veo-3.1-fast"
                    ? "veo-3.1-fast-generate-preview"
                    : "veo-3.1-generate-preview",
                resolution: state.veoResolution || "1080p",
                audio: Boolean(state.includeAudio),
                detail: state.veoDetail || "professional",
                language: state.veoLanguage || "English",
              }
            : {}),
      vendor: spec.vendor,
      encoder: spec.encoder,
      kind: spec.kind,
    },
    engine_params: {
      mode: state.mode,
      aspect_ratio: state.aspect,
      width,
      height,
      ...(spec.kind === "video"
        ? {
            duration_sec: state.duration,
            fps,
            num_frames: numFrames,
            motion_scale: state.motionScale,
            shots_count: state.shotsCount,
          }
        : {}),
      cfg_scale: state.cfgScale,
      steps: state.steps,
      seed: state.seed,
      seed_locked: state.seedLocked,
    },
    prompts: {
      positive: SmartSceneGuard.strip(positiveOnly),
      ...(spec.supportsNegative ? { negative: negativeOnly } : {}),
      ...(spec.kind === "video" && state.includeAudio
        ? {
            audio_design: [
              state.audioDescription.trim(),
              ...AUDIO_DESIGN_TAGS.filter((t) =>
                state.audioTags.includes(t.id)
              ).map((t) => t.phrase),
            ]
              .filter(Boolean)
              .join("; "),
          }
        : {}),
    },
  };
}

export function buildPythonPipelineScript(
  state: StudioFormState,
  positiveOnly: string,
  negativeOnly: string
): string {
  const spec = ENGINES[state.engine];
  const resMatch = state.aspect.match(/\((\d+)x(\d+)\)/);
  const width = resMatch ? Number(resMatch[1]) : 1280;
  const height = resMatch ? Number(resMatch[2]) : 720;
  const numFrames = getExactFrameCount(state.engine, state.duration, state.wanVariant);
  const fps = getEngineFps(state.engine, state.wanVariant);

  const safePos = JSON.stringify(SmartSceneGuard.strip(positiveOnly));
  const safeNeg = JSON.stringify(negativeOnly);

  if (state.engine === "wan22") {
    const modelId =
      state.mode === "s2v"
        ? "Wan-AI/Wan2.2-S2V-14B"
        : state.wanVariant === "ti2v_5b"
          ? "Wan-AI/Wan2.2-TI2V-5B-Diffusers"
          : state.mode === "i2v"
            ? "Wan-AI/Wan2.2-I2V-A14B-Diffusers"
            : "Wan-AI/Wan2.2-T2V-A14B-Diffusers";
    return `import torch
from diffusers import WanPipeline
from diffusers.utils import export_to_video

# Variant: ${WAN_VARIANTS[state.wanVariant].name}
pipe = WanPipeline.from_pretrained("${modelId}", torch_dtype=torch.bfloat16)
pipe.to("cuda")

generator = torch.Generator(device="cuda").manual_seed(${state.seed})

prompt = ${safePos}
negative_prompt = ${safeNeg}

frames = pipe(
    prompt=prompt,
    negative_prompt=negative_prompt,
    height=${height},
    width=${width},
    num_frames=${numFrames},
    guidance_scale=${state.cfgScale.toFixed(1)},
    num_inference_steps=${state.steps},
    generator=generator,
).frames[0]

export_to_video(frames, "wan22_${state.wanVariant}_${state.seed}.mp4", fps=${fps})
`;
  }

  if (state.engine === "minimax") {
    const resolution =
      state.duration >= 10
        ? "768P"
        : state.minimaxResolution || (state.aspect.includes("1080p") ? "1080P" : "768P");
    const firstFrameLine =
      state.mode === "i2v" || state.mode === "flf2v"
        ? `    "first_frame_image": "path/to/first_frame.jpg",\n`
        : "";
    const lastFrameLine =
      state.mode === "flf2v"
        ? `    "last_frame_image": "path/to/last_frame.jpg",\n`
        : "";
    return `import os
import requests

# MiniMax Hailuo 02 Video Generation API (${state.mode.toUpperCase()})
API_KEY = os.environ["MINIMAX_API_KEY"]

payload = {
    "model": "MiniMax-Hailuo-02",
    "prompt": ${safePos},
    "duration": ${state.duration},
    "resolution": "${resolution}",
    "prompt_optimizer": ${state.minimaxPromptOptimizer ? "True" : "False"},
${firstFrameLine}${lastFrameLine}}

response = requests.post(
    "https://api.minimax.io/v1/video_generation",
    headers={"Authorization": f"Bearer {API_KEY}", "Content-Type": "application/json"},
    json=payload,
    timeout=60,
)
print(response.json())
`;
  }

  if (state.engine === "veo") {
    const veoModelId =
      state.veoModel === "veo-3.1-fast"
        ? "veo-3.1-fast-generate-preview"
        : "veo-3.1-generate-preview";
    const veoRes = state.veoResolution || "1080p";
    const veoAspect = state.aspect.includes("9:16") ? "9:16" : "16:9";
    const veoDuration =
      state.duration === 4 || state.duration === 6 || state.duration === 8
        ? state.duration
        : 8;
    return `from google import genai
from google.genai import types

# Google Veo 3.1 SDK (${veoModelId})
client = genai.Client()

prompt = ${safePos}
negative_prompt = ${safeNeg}

operation = client.models.generate_videos(
    model="${veoModelId}",
    prompt=prompt,
    config=types.GenerateVideosConfig(
        aspect_ratio="${veoAspect}",
        resolution="${veoRes}",
        duration_seconds=${veoDuration},
        negative_prompt=negative_prompt or None,
    ),
)
print("Started Veo generation:", operation.name)
`;
  }

  if (state.engine === "hunyuan") {
    return `import torch
from diffusers import HunyuanVideoPipeline
from diffusers.utils import export_to_video

pipe = HunyuanVideoPipeline.from_pretrained("hunyuanvideo-community/HunyuanVideo", torch_dtype=torch.bfloat16)
pipe.vae.enable_tiling()
pipe.to("cuda")

generator = torch.Generator(device="cuda").manual_seed(${state.seed})

prompt = ${safePos}

frames = pipe(
    prompt=prompt,
    height=${height},
    width=${width},
    num_frames=${numFrames},
    num_inference_steps=${state.steps},
    generator=generator,
).frames[0]

export_to_video(frames, "hunyuan_output_${state.seed}.mp4", fps=24)
`;
  }

  if (state.engine === "ltx") {
    return `import torch
from diffusers import LTXPipeline
from diffusers.utils import export_to_video

pipe = LTXPipeline.from_pretrained("Lightricks/LTX-Video-0.9.5", torch_dtype=torch.bfloat16)
pipe.to("cuda")

generator = torch.Generator(device="cuda").manual_seed(${state.seed})

prompt = ${safePos}
negative_prompt = ${safeNeg}

frames = pipe(
    prompt=prompt,
    negative_prompt=negative_prompt,
    width=${width},
    height=${height},
    num_frames=${numFrames},
    guidance_scale=${state.cfgScale.toFixed(1)},
    num_inference_steps=${state.steps},
    generator=generator,
).frames[0]

export_to_video(frames, "ltx_output_${state.seed}.mp4", fps=24)
`;
  }

  if (state.engine === "flux1") {
    return `import torch
from diffusers import FluxPipeline

pipe = FluxPipeline.from_pretrained("black-forest-labs/FLUX.1-dev", torch_dtype=torch.bfloat16)
pipe.enable_model_cpu_offload()

generator = torch.Generator("cpu").manual_seed(${state.seed})

prompt = ${safePos}

image = pipe(
    prompt=prompt,
    height=${height},
    width=${width},
    guidance_scale=${state.cfgScale.toFixed(1)},
    num_inference_steps=${state.steps},
    max_sequence_length=512,
    generator=generator,
).images[0]

image.save("flux1_output_${state.seed}.png")
`;
  }

  return `import torch
from diffusers import StableDiffusionXLPipeline

pipe = StableDiffusionXLPipeline.from_pretrained(
    "stabilityai/stable-diffusion-xl-base-1.0",
    torch_dtype=torch.float16,
    variant="fp16",
    use_safetensors=True,
)
pipe.to("cuda")

generator = torch.Generator(device="cuda").manual_seed(${state.seed})

prompt = ${safePos}
negative_prompt = ${safeNeg}

image = pipe(
    prompt=prompt,
    negative_prompt=negative_prompt,
    height=${height},
    width=${width},
    guidance_scale=${state.cfgScale.toFixed(1)},
    num_inference_steps=${state.steps},
    generator=generator,
).images[0]

image.save("${spec.id}_output_${state.seed}.png")
`;
}


####################################################################################################
FILE: src/utils/smartSceneGuard.ts
####################################################################################################

import type { EngineId } from "../types/prompt";

export type SmartSceneGuardProvider = "wan" | "minimax" | "veo" | "universal";

export interface IsolatedSceneItem {
  sceneId: string;
  jobId: string;
  prompt: string;
  independent: true;
}

export const SmartSceneGuard = {
  version: "2.0",

  /** القالب الكامل يُستخدم كـ System Instruction لنموذج LLM أو للعرض المنفصل فقط */
  isolationRules: `[SMART SCENE ISOLATION — STRICT MODE]

SCENE INDEPENDENCE:
Treat this generation as an independent cinematic scene.
Never import, reuse, or blend unrelated previous scenes.
Do not carry over previous backgrounds, characters, objects,
lighting setups, or camera compositions.

ENVIRONMENT LOCK:
Maintain one coherent environment throughout this shot.
No unexpected location changes or background replacement.

CHARACTER LOCK:
Preserve the identity, face, clothing, body proportions,
and appearance of every reference character.
No duplication, replacement, or identity drift.

TEMPORAL STABILITY:
No ghosting, residual frames, double exposure,
frame blending, flickering, visual trails, or unwanted morphing.

CAMERA CONTINUITY:
Follow only the camera movements requested by the user.
Maintain realistic camera physics and spatial continuity.
No unintended cuts or sudden camera jumps.

OBJECT CONSISTENCY:
Keep objects in their correct positions.
Do not introduce unrelated objects or duplicate existing objects.

SCENE BOUNDARIES:
One coherent shot unless multiple scenes are explicitly requested.
Keep intentional camera movements and transitions.
Do not blend unrelated scenes or environments.

FINAL FRAME:
End the requested scene naturally.
Do not introduce another scene at the end.

PRIORITY:
Preserve the user's original creative idea.
These instructions must not override the requested action.

[END SMART SCENE ISOLATION]`,

  negativeRules: `Avoid: scene blending, previous scene residue,
unrelated backgrounds, ghosting, double exposure,
duplicated characters, duplicated objects, flickering,
temporal artifacts, unwanted transitions, visual trails,
identity drift, environment morphing, camera jumps,
random scene changes, residual frames.`,

  /** كلمات سلبية صافية تُدمج تلقائياً في حقل Negative Prompt بدلاً من البرومت الإيجابي */
  negativeTokens: [
    "scene blending",
    "previous scene residue",
    "unrelated backgrounds",
    "ghosting",
    "double exposure",
    "duplicated characters",
    "duplicated objects",
    "flickering",
    "temporal artifacts",
    "unwanted transitions",
    "visual trails",
    "identity drift",
    "environment morphing",
    "camera jumps",
    "random scene changes",
    "residual frames",
  ],

  providerRules: {
    wan: `[WAN TEMPORAL CONTROL]
Maintain stable frame-to-frame composition.
Preserve reference image identity.
Use smooth continuous motion.
Avoid unintended temporal blending.`,

    minimax: `[MINIMAX SHOT CONTROL]
Maintain one coherent cinematic shot.
Preserve subject and environment consistency.
No unexpected scene transitions.
Maintain natural camera and object motion.`,

    veo: `[VEO SCENE CONTROL]
Maintain a single continuous cinematic scene with consistent subject identity, natural lighting continuity, and coherent camera motion.`,

    universal: `[UNIVERSAL VIDEO CONSISTENCY]
Maintain visual and temporal consistency.`,
  } satisfies Record<SmartSceneGuardProvider, string>,

  /** عبارات فيزيائية وبصرية موجزة تفهمها مشفرات الفيديو (T5 / UMT5 / Hailuo) دون تشتيت */
  organicVisualDirectives: {
    wan: "Single continuous shot with stable frame-to-frame geometry and consistent subject identity.",
    minimax: "One continuous single-take cinematic shot preserving subject identity and spatial continuity.",
    veo: "Single continuous cinematic take with consistent subject identity and coherent camera motion.",
    universal: "Continuous single-take shot with consistent environment and temporal stability.",
  } satisfies Record<SmartSceneGuardProvider, string>,

  resolveProvider(engine: EngineId | string): SmartSceneGuardProvider {
    const lower = String(engine || "").toLowerCase();
    if (lower.includes("wan")) return "wan";
    if (lower.includes("minimax") || lower.includes("hailuo")) return "minimax";
    if (lower.includes("veo") || lower.includes("google")) return "veo";
    return "universal";
  },

  /**
   * يدمج التوجيه البصري العضوي فقط داخل برومت الفيديو لمنع تشويش المحرك بالقوالب الإدارية.
   * إذا تم تمرير includeRawBlocks = true، يعيد القالب الكامل (لأغراض LLM System Prompt).
   */
  apply(
    prompt: string,
    provider: string = "universal",
    includeRawBlocks: boolean = false
  ): string {
    if (!prompt || typeof prompt !== "string" || !prompt.trim()) {
      throw new Error("A valid video prompt is required.");
    }

    const cleaned = this.strip(prompt);
    const providerKey = this.resolveProvider(provider);

    if (includeRawBlocks) {
      return [
        cleaned,
        this.providerRules[providerKey],
        this.isolationRules,
        this.negativeRules,
      ].join("\n\n");
    }

    const directive = this.organicVisualDirectives[providerKey];
    if (
      cleaned.toLowerCase().includes("single-take") ||
      cleaned.toLowerCase().includes("continuous shot") ||
      cleaned.toLowerCase().includes("continuous cinematic take")
    ) {
      return cleaned;
    }

    return `${cleaned} ${directive}`.trim();
  },

  applyWithinLimit(
    prompt: string,
    provider: string = "universal",
    maxChars: number = 2200
  ): string {
    const applied = this.apply(prompt, provider, false);
    if (applied.length <= maxChars) return applied;
    return `${applied.slice(0, Math.max(0, maxChars - 1)).trimEnd()}…`;
  },

  /** يعيد قالب العزل الكامل لإرساله كـ System Instruction في الخلفية أو عرضه في تبويب مستقل */
  getGuardMetadataBlock(provider: string = "universal"): string {
    const providerKey = this.resolveProvider(provider);
    return [this.providerRules[providerKey], this.isolationRules].join("\n\n");
  },

  /** يدمج الكلمات السلبية الخاصة بالعزل مع البرومت السلبي للمحرك بدون تكرار */
  mergeNegativePrompt(baseNegative: string): string {
    const existing = (baseNegative || "")
      .split(",")
      .map((s) => s.trim())
      .filter(Boolean);
    const existingLower = new Set(existing.map((s) => s.toLowerCase()));

    for (const token of this.negativeTokens) {
      if (!existingLower.has(token.toLowerCase())) {
        existing.push(token);
      }
    }
    return existing.join(", ");
  },

  strip(prompt: string): string {
    if (!prompt || typeof prompt !== "string") return "";
    return prompt
      .replace(
        /\n*\[(?:WAN TEMPORAL CONTROL|MINIMAX SHOT CONTROL|VEO SCENE CONTROL|UNIVERSAL VIDEO CONSISTENCY)\][\s\S]*?(?=\n+\[SMART SCENE ISOLATION — STRICT MODE\]|\n+AUDIO & SOUND DESIGN:|\n+NEGATIVE PROMPT:|$)/g,
        ""
      )
      .replace(
        /\n*\[SMART SCENE ISOLATION — STRICT MODE\][\s\S]*?\[END SMART SCENE ISOLATION\](?:\s*\n+Avoid:\s*scene blending,[\s\S]*?residual frames\.)?/g,
        ""
      )
      .replace(/\n*\[SMART SCENE ISOLATION — STRICT MODE\][\s\S]*$/g, "")
      .replace(/\n*Avoid:\s*scene blending,[\s\S]*?residual frames\./g, "")
      .replace(/\n{3,}/g, "\n\n")
      .trim();
  },

  separateScenes(
    scenes: string[],
    provider: string = "universal"
  ): IsolatedSceneItem[] {
    if (!Array.isArray(scenes)) {
      throw new Error("Scenes must be an array.");
    }

    const baseTs = Date.now();
    return scenes.map((scene, index) => ({
      sceneId: `scene_${index + 1}`,
      jobId: `sg_${baseTs}_${index + 1}_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 7)}`,
      prompt: this.apply(scene, provider, false),
      independent: true,
    }));
  },
};


####################################################################################################
FILE: src/utils/googleVeoPrompt.ts
####################################################################################################

import type { StudioFormState } from "../types/prompt";

export const GOOGLE_VEO_LANGUAGES = ["English", "Arabic", "French", "Spanish"] as const;

export interface GoogleVideoPromptResult {
  prompt: string;
  negative: string;
  meta: {
    model: string;
    resolution: string;
    aspect: string;
    duration: number;
    hasAudio: boolean;
    wordCount: number;
    promptChars: number;
  };
  settings: {
    model: string;
    resolution: string;
    aspectRatio: string;
    durationSeconds: number;
    language: string;
  };
}

export function compileGoogleVeoFromStudio(form: StudioFormState): GoogleVideoPromptResult {
  const parts: string[] = [];

  if (form.mode === "film") {
    parts.push("Maintain: same character, same palette, same lens.");
  }

  const cameraLine = [form.cameraLens, form.cameraMovement].filter(Boolean).join(", ");
  const coreScene = [
    cameraLine ? `A ${cameraLine} shot` : "A cinematic shot",
    form.subject.trim() ? `of ${form.subject.trim()}` : "",
    form.environment.trim() ? `in ${form.environment.trim()}` : "",
  ]
    .filter(Boolean)
    .join(" ");

  if (coreScene) parts.push(`${coreScene}.`);
  if (form.action.trim()) parts.push(form.action.trim().replace(/\.+$/, "") + ".");
  if (form.light.trim()) parts.push(`Lighting: ${form.light.trim().replace(/\.+$/, "")}.`);
  if (form.style.trim()) parts.push(`Mood and aesthetic: ${form.style.trim().replace(/\.+$/, "")}.`);
  if (form.extra.trim()) parts.push(form.extra.trim().replace(/\.+$/, "") + ".");

  const audioPieces: string[] = [];
  if (form.veoSoundEffects.trim()) {
    audioPieces.push(`SFX: ${form.veoSoundEffects.trim()}`);
  }
  if (form.veoDialogue.trim()) {
    audioPieces.push(`Dialogue (${form.veoLanguage}): "${form.veoDialogue.trim()}"`);
  }
  if (audioPieces.length > 0) {
    parts.push(audioPieces.join(". ") + ".");
  }

  const prompt = parts.join(" ").replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
  const wordCount = prompt.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean).length;

  return {
    prompt,
    negative: form.negative.trim(),
    meta: {
      model: form.veoModel,
      resolution: form.veoResolution,
      aspect: form.aspect,
      duration: form.duration || 8,
      hasAudio: audioPieces.length > 0 || form.includeAudio,
      wordCount,
      promptChars: prompt.length,
    },
    settings: {
      model: form.veoModel,
      resolution: form.veoResolution,
      aspectRatio: form.aspect,
      durationSeconds: form.duration || 8,
      language: form.veoLanguage,
    },
  };
}


####################################################################################################
FILE: src/App.tsx
