import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { Moon, Sun } from "lucide-react";
import { DirectorEngineView } from "./components/DirectorEngineView";
import { EngineGuideView } from "./components/EngineGuideView";
import { HistoryView } from "./components/HistoryView";
import { PortalView } from "./components/PortalView";
import { ProStudioView } from "./components/ProStudioView";
import { VideoPromptGeneratorView } from "./components/VideoPromptGeneratorView";
import { ENGINES, WAN_VARIANTS } from "./data/engines";
import { I18N } from "./data/i18n";
import type { DirectorEnhanceOptions } from "./utils/arabiCinematicDirector";
import type {
  EngineId,
  HistoryEntry,
  Language,
  PresetItem,
  SavedSequenceEntry,
  StudioFormState,
  ThemeMode,
  VideoGeneratorRequest,
  VideoGeneratorResult,
  WorkspaceTab,
} from "./types/prompt";
import { buildDefaultShots, compileEnginePrompt } from "./utils/promptEngine";

const STORAGE_PREFS_KEY = "videoprompts_prefs_v1";
const STORAGE_HISTORY_KEY = "videoprompts_history_v1";
const STORAGE_SEQUENCES_KEY = "videoprompts_saved_sequences_v1";

function createInitialForm(): StudioFormState {
  const engine: EngineId = "wan22";
  const spec = ENGINES[engine];
  const wan = WAN_VARIANTS.a14b;
  return {
    engine,
    wanVariant: "a14b",
    promptFormat: "official",
    mode: spec.defaultMode,
    category: "",
    subject: "",
    environment: "",
    action: "",
    camera: "",
    cameraMovement: "",
    cameraLens: "",
    light: "",
    style: "",
    extra: "",
    aspect: wan.aspectOptions[0],
    duration: spec.defaultDuration,
    motionScale: 6,
    shotsCount: 3,
    shots: buildDefaultShots(3),
    cfgScale: wan.defaultCfg,
    steps: wan.defaultSteps,
    loraTags: "",
    seed: Math.floor(100_000 + Math.random() * 8_999_999_999),
    seedLocked: false,
    physicsHints: [],
    includeAudio: true,
    audioTags: [],
    audioDescription: "",
    negative: "",
    negativeEdited: false,
    minimaxResolution: "1080P",
    minimaxCameraCommands: [],
    minimaxCustomDirective: "",
    minimaxPromptOptimizer: true,
    veoModel: "veo-3.1",
    veoResolution: "1080p",
    veoDialogue: "",
    veoSoundEffects: "",
    veoDetail: "professional",
    veoLanguage: "English",
    smartSceneGuard: true,
  };
}

export default function App() {
  const [lang, setLang] = useState<Language>(() => {
    try {
      const saved = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_PREFS_KEY) || "{}");
      if (saved.lang === "ar" || saved.lang === "fr" || saved.lang === "en") {
        return saved.lang;
      }
    } catch {
      // ignore
    }
    return "ar";
  });

  const [theme, setTheme] = useState<ThemeMode>(() => {
    try {
      const saved = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_PREFS_KEY) || "{}");
      if (saved.theme === "dark" || saved.theme === "light") {
        return saved.theme;
      }
    } catch {
      // ignore
    }
    return "dark";
  });

  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<WorkspaceTab>("portal");
  const [form, setForm] = useState<StudioFormState>(createInitialForm);
  const [outputText, setOutputText] = useState("");
  const [activeGenResult, setActiveGenResult] =
    useState<VideoGeneratorResult | null>(null);
  const [activeGenIdea, setActiveGenIdea] = useState("");
  const [genPresetProps, setGenPresetProps] = useState<
    (Partial<VideoGeneratorRequest> & { nonce: number }) | null
  >(null);
  const [activeDirectorSnapshot, setActiveDirectorSnapshot] = useState<{
    options: DirectorEnhanceOptions;
    output: string;
  } | null>(null);
  const [directorPresetProps, setDirectorPresetProps] = useState<
    (Partial<DirectorEnhanceOptions> & { nonce: number }) | null
  >(null);
  const [history, setHistory] = useState<HistoryEntry[]>(() => {
    try {
      const raw = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_HISTORY_KEY) || "[]");
      return Array.isArray(raw) ? raw : [];
    } catch {
      return [];
    }
  });
  const [savedSequences, setSavedSequences] = useState<SavedSequenceEntry[]>(() => {
    try {
      const raw = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_SEQUENCES_KEY) || "[]");
      return Array.isArray(raw) ? raw : [];
    } catch {
      return [];
    }
  });
  const [status, setStatus] = useState<{ msg: string; type: "ok" | "err" } | null>(
    null
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    const root = document.documentElement;
    root.lang = lang;
    root.dir = lang === "ar" ? "rtl" : "ltr";
    root.classList.toggle("dark", theme === "dark");
    try {
      localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_PREFS_KEY, JSON.stringify({ lang, theme }));
    } catch {
      // ignore
    }
  }, [lang, theme]);

  useEffect(() => {
    try {
      localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_HISTORY_KEY, JSON.stringify(history));
    } catch {
      // ignore
    }
  }, [history]);

  useEffect(() => {
    try {
      localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_SEQUENCES_KEY, JSON.stringify(savedSequences));
    } catch {
      // ignore
    }
  }, [savedSequences]);

  const notify = (msg: string, type: "ok" | "err") => {
    setStatus({ msg, type });
    setTimeout(() => {
      setStatus((prev) => (prev?.msg === msg ? null : prev));
    }, 4000);
  };

  const copy = I18N[lang];

  const handleSaveStudioEntry = (
    compiledFull: string,
    positiveOnly: string,
    negativeOnly: string,
    options?: { silent?: boolean }
  ) => {
    const spec = ENGINES[form.engine];
    setHistory((prev) => {
      const existingIndex = prev.findIndex((item) => item.prompt === compiledFull);
      if (existingIndex === 0) return prev;
      const entry: HistoryEntry = {
        id: `${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 7)}`,
        engine: form.engine,
        wanVariant: form.engine === "wan22" ? form.wanVariant : undefined,
        mode: form.mode,
        category: form.category,
        shotsCount: form.shotsCount,
        title: `${spec.name} — ${(form.subject || form.action || "Prompt").slice(0, 48)}`,
        time: new Date().toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: "2-digit", minute: "2-digit" }),
        timestamp: Date.now(),
        isFavorite: existingIndex > -1 ? prev[existingIndex].isFavorite : false,
        prompt: compiledFull,
        positiveOnly,
        negativeOnly,
        source: "studio",
        stateSnapshot: { ...form },
      };
      const filtered = prev.filter((item) => item.prompt !== compiledFull);
      return [entry, ...filtered].slice(0, 60);
    });
    if (!options?.silent) {
      notify(copy.genPromptSaved, "ok");
    }
  };

  const handleSaveGeneratorResult = (
    result: VideoGeneratorResult,
    idea: string,
    options?: { silent?: boolean; favorite?: boolean }
  ) => {
    const engineMap: Record<VideoGeneratorResult["model"], EngineId> = {
      wan: "wan22",
      minimax: "minimax",
      google: "veo",
    };
    const isFilm = result.mode === "film" || result.mode === "film_10";
    const mappedEngine = engineMap[result.model];
    const cleanIdea = idea.trim() || "Prompt";

    setHistory((prev) => {
      const existing = prev.find((item) => item.prompt === result.formattedOutput);
      const entry: HistoryEntry = {
        id: existing?.id || `${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 7)}`,
        engine: mappedEngine,
        mode: isFilm ? "film" : "t2v",
        category: result.style,
        shotsCount: isFilm ? result.scenes.length : 1,
        title: `${result.model.toUpperCase()} — ${cleanIdea.slice(0, 48)}`,
        time: new Date().toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: "2-digit", minute: "2-digit" }),
        timestamp: Date.now(),
        isFavorite: options?.favorite ?? existing?.isFavorite ?? false,
        prompt: result.formattedOutput,
        positiveOnly: !isFilm
          ? result.singlePrompt
          : result.scenes.map((s) => s.prompt).join("\n\n"),
        negativeOnly: "",
        source: "generator_v1",
        generatorSnapshot: {
          result,
          idea: cleanIdea,
        },
      };
      const remaining = prev.filter((item) => item.prompt !== result.formattedOutput);
      return [entry, ...remaining].slice(0, 60);
    });

    if (!options?.silent) {
      notify(copy.genPromptSaved, "ok");
    }
  };

  const handleSaveDirectorResult = (
    options: DirectorEnhanceOptions,
    output: string
  ) => {
    const dirEngine = String(options.engine || "generic").toLowerCase();
    const mappedEngine: EngineId =
      dirEngine === "wan"
        ? "wan22"
        : dirEngine === "minimax" || dirEngine === "hailuo"
          ? "minimax"
          : dirEngine === "veo"
            ? "veo"
            : "veo";
    const cleanIdea = options.prompt.trim() || "Director Prompt";

    setHistory((prev) => {
      const existing = prev.find((item) => item.prompt === output);
      const entry: HistoryEntry = {
        id: existing?.id || `${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 7)}`,
        engine: mappedEngine,
        mode: "t2v",
        category: options.genre || "cinematic",
        shotsCount: 1,
        title: `Director v2 · ${dirEngine.toUpperCase()} — ${cleanIdea.slice(0, 42)}`,
        time: new Date().toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: "2-digit", minute: "2-digit" }),
        timestamp: Date.now(),
        isFavorite: existing?.isFavorite ?? false,
        prompt: output,
        positiveOnly: output,
        negativeOnly: (options.negatives || []).join(", "),
        source: "director_v2",
        directorSnapshot: {
          options,
          output,
        },
      };
      const remaining = prev.filter((item) => item.prompt !== output);
      return [entry, ...remaining].slice(0, 60);
    });
  };

  const handleLoadHistoryEntry = (entry: HistoryEntry) => {
    if (entry.directorSnapshot) {
      setActiveDirectorSnapshot(entry.directorSnapshot);
      setActiveTab("director");
      return;
    }
    if (entry.generatorSnapshot) {
      setActiveGenIdea(entry.generatorSnapshot.idea);
      setActiveGenResult(entry.generatorSnapshot.result);
      setActiveTab("generator");
      return;
    }
    if (entry.stateSnapshot) {
      setForm((prev) => ({ ...prev, ...entry.stateSnapshot }));
    }
    setOutputText(entry.prompt);
    setActiveTab("studio");
  };

  const handleSendGeneratorToStudio = (
    result: VideoGeneratorResult,
    idea: string
  ) => {
    const engineMap: Record<VideoGeneratorResult["model"], EngineId> = {
      wan: "wan22",
      minimax: "minimax",
      google: "veo",
    };
    const isFilm = result.mode === "film" || result.mode === "film_10";
    const nextEngine = engineMap[result.model];
    const firstScenePrompt = !isFilm
      ? result.singlePrompt
      : result.scenes[0]?.prompt || result.formattedOutput;

    setForm((prev) => ({
      ...prev,
      engine: nextEngine,
      mode: isFilm ? "film" : "t2v",
      duration: result.shotLen,
      subject: idea,
      style: result.style,
      shotsCount: isFilm ? Math.min(12, Math.max(1, result.scenes.length)) : 1,
      shots: isFilm
        ? result.scenes.slice(0, 12).map((s, idx) => ({
            id: idx + 1,
            camera: s.narrativeBeat || `Scene ${idx + 1}`,
            description: s.prompt.split("\n")[0] || s.prompt,
          }))
        : prev.shots,
    }));
    setOutputText(firstScenePrompt);
    setActiveTab("studio");
  };

  const handleSaveSequence = (
    result: VideoGeneratorResult,
    ideaText: string
  ): boolean => {
    if (!result.scenes || result.scenes.length === 0) {
      return false;
    }
    const cleanIdea = ideaText.trim() || "Untitled Sequence";
    const entry: SavedSequenceEntry = {
      id: `seq-${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 7)}`,
      title: `${cleanIdea.slice(0, 56)}`,
      idea: cleanIdea,
      model: result.model,
      mode: result.mode,
      style: result.style,
      lang: result.lang,
      shotLen: result.shotLen,
      scenes: result.scenes,
      consistencyNotes: result.consistencyNotes,
      formattedOutput: result.formattedOutput,
      createdAt: Date.now(),
      isFavorite: false,
    };
    setSavedSequences((prev) => {
      // Re-saving the same project updates its existing series instead of creating duplicates.
      const existingIndex = prev.findIndex(
        (item) => item.idea.trim() === cleanIdea && item.model === result.model
      );
      if (existingIndex >= 0) {
        const updated = [...prev];
        updated[existingIndex] = {
          ...entry,
          id: prev[existingIndex].id,
          createdAt: prev[existingIndex].createdAt,
          isFavorite: prev[existingIndex].isFavorite,
        };
        return updated;
      }
      return [entry, ...prev].slice(0, 50);
    });
    notify(copy.genSequenceSaved, "ok");
    return true;
  };

  const handleLoadSequence = (seq: SavedSequenceEntry) => {
    setActiveGenIdea(seq.idea);
    setActiveGenResult({
      version: "1.0",
      model: seq.model,
      mode: seq.mode,
      style: seq.style,
      lang: seq.lang,
      shotLen: seq.shotLen,
      singlePrompt: "",
      scenes: seq.scenes,
      consistencyNotes: seq.consistencyNotes,
      formattedOutput: seq.formattedOutput,
    });
    setActiveTab("generator");
  };

  const handleApplySequenceProps = (seq: SavedSequenceEntry) => {
    setActiveGenResult(null);
    setActiveGenIdea("");
    setGenPresetProps({
      model: seq.model,
      mode: seq.mode,
      scenes: seq.scenes.length || 10,
      shotLen: seq.shotLen,
      style: seq.style,
      lang: seq.lang,
      nonce: Date.now(),
    });
    setActiveTab("generator");
    notify(copy.historyPropsApplied, "ok");
  };

  const handleApplyHistoryEntryProps = (entry: HistoryEntry) => {
    if (entry.directorSnapshot) {
      const { options } = entry.directorSnapshot;
      const { prompt: _ignoredPrompt, beats: _ignoredBeats, ...restProps } = options;
      setActiveDirectorSnapshot(null);
      setDirectorPresetProps({
        ...restProps,
        nonce: Date.now(),
      });
      setActiveTab("director");
      notify(copy.historyPropsApplied, "ok");
      return;
    }

    if (entry.generatorSnapshot) {
      const { result } = entry.generatorSnapshot;
      setActiveGenResult(null);
      setActiveGenIdea("");
      setGenPresetProps({
        model: result.model,
        mode: result.mode,
        scenes: result.scenes.length || 10,
        shotLen: result.shotLen,
        style: result.style,
        lang: result.lang,
        nonce: Date.now(),
      });
      setActiveTab("generator");
      notify(copy.historyPropsApplied, "ok");
      return;
    }

    if (entry.stateSnapshot) {
      const snap = entry.stateSnapshot;
      setForm((prev) => ({
        ...prev,
        engine: snap.engine ?? entry.engine,
        wanVariant: snap.wanVariant ?? prev.wanVariant,
        mode: snap.mode ?? entry.mode,
        category: snap.category ?? entry.category,
        aspect: snap.aspect ?? prev.aspect,
        duration: snap.duration ?? prev.duration,
        cameraMovement: snap.cameraMovement ?? prev.cameraMovement,
        cameraLens: snap.cameraLens ?? prev.cameraLens,
        camera: snap.camera ?? prev.camera,
        light: snap.light ?? prev.light,
        style: snap.style ?? prev.style,
        physicsHints: snap.physicsHints ?? prev.physicsHints,
        audioTags: snap.audioTags ?? prev.audioTags,
      }));
      setActiveTab("studio");
      notify(copy.historyPropsApplied, "ok");
      return;
    }

    handleLaunchEngine(entry.engine);
    notify(copy.historyPropsApplied, "ok");
  };

  const handleLoadPreset = (preset: PresetItem) => {
    const nextForm: StudioFormState = {
      ...createInitialForm(),
      engine: preset.engine,
      mode: preset.mode,
      category: preset.category,
      subject: preset.subject,
      environment: preset.environment,
      action: preset.action,
      cameraMovement: preset.cameraMovement,
      cameraLens: preset.cameraLens,
      camera: [preset.cameraMovement, preset.cameraLens].filter(Boolean).join(", "),
      light: preset.light,
      style: preset.style,
      extra: preset.extra,
      duration: preset.duration,
      aspect: preset.aspect,
    };
    setForm(nextForm);
    setOutputText(compileEnginePrompt(nextForm).fullPrompt);
    setActiveTab("studio");
  };

  const handleLaunchEngine = (engineId: EngineId) => {
    const spec = ENGINES[engineId];
    setForm((prev) => ({
      ...prev,
      engine: engineId,
      mode: spec.defaultMode,
      aspect: spec.aspectOptions[0],
      duration: spec.defaultDuration,
      ...(engineId === "wan22" ? { wanVariant: "a14b" as const } : {}),
    }));
    setActiveTab("studio");
  };

  const navItems: { id: WorkspaceTab; label: string }[] = [
    { id: "portal", label: copy.navPortal },
    { id: "generator", label: copy.navGenerator },
    { id: "director", label: copy.navDirector },
    { id: "studio", label: copy.navStudio },
    { id: "guide", label: copy.navGuide },
    { id: "history", label: copy.navHistory },
  ];

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-zinc-50 text-zinc-900 dark:bg-[#090A0D] dark:text-zinc-100">
      {/* 3-Zone Top Bar Contract */}
      <header className="border-b border-zinc-200 bg-white/90 backdrop-blur dark:border-zinc-800/80 dark:bg-[#090A0D]/90">
        <div className="mx-auto flex max-w-7xl items-center justify-between gap-8 px-4 py-3.5 sm:px-6">
          {/* Zone 1: Single-element Brand wordmark */}
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setActiveTab("portal")}
            className="whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 text-base font-semibold tracking-tight text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-50"
          >
            {copy.brand}
          </button>

          {/* Zone 2: Clean single-line text navigation links */}
          <nav className="hidden items-center gap-6 md:flex">
            {navItems.map((item) => {
              const active = activeTab === item.id;
              return (
                <button
                  key={item.id}
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setActiveTab(item.id)}
                  className={`whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 border-b-2 pb-0.5 text-sm font-medium transition-colors ${
                    active
                      ? "border-amber-500 text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-50"
                      : "border-transparent text-zinc-500 hover:text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-400 dark:hover:text-zinc-100"
                  }`}
                >
                  {item.label}
                </button>
              );
            })}
          </nav>

          {/* Zone 3: 1 primary action */}
          <div className="shrink-0">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => {
                setForm(createInitialForm());
                setOutputText("");
                setActiveGenResult(null);
                setActiveGenIdea("");
                setActiveTab("generator");
              }}
              className="whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 rounded-lg bg-zinc-900 px-3.5 py-2 text-xs font-medium text-white transition-colors hover:bg-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-100 dark:text-zinc-900 dark:hover:bg-zinc-200"
            >
              {copy.ctaNewPrompt}
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Mobile navigation row */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-4 overflow-x-auto border-t border-zinc-200 px-4 py-2 md:hidden dark:border-zinc-800">
          {navItems.map((item) => {
            const active = activeTab === item.id;
            return (
              <button
                key={item.id}
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setActiveTab(item.id)}
                className={`whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 text-xs font-medium ${
                  active
                    ? "text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400"
                    : "text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400"
                }`}
              >
                {item.label}
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      </header>

      {/* Workspace Utility Strip (Language & Theme Switch) */}
      <div className="border-b border-zinc-200 bg-zinc-100/70 dark:border-zinc-800/80 dark:bg-zinc-900/40">
        <div className="mx-auto flex max-w-7xl items-center justify-between px-4 py-2 sm:px-6">
          <div className="text-xs text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400">
            {status ? (
              <span
                className={
                  status.type === "err"
                    ? "text-red-600 dark:text-red-400"
                    : "text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400"
                }
              >
                {status.msg}
              </span>
            ) : (
              <span>Wan 2.2 · MiniMax Hailuo · Google Veo</span>
            )}
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <div className="flex items-center rounded-md border border-zinc-200 bg-white p-0.5 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900">
              {(["ar", "fr", "en"] as Language[]).map((l) => (
                <button
                  key={l}
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setLang(l)}
                  className={`rounded px-2 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-medium uppercase ${
                    lang === l
                      ? "bg-zinc-900 text-white dark:bg-zinc-100 dark:text-zinc-900"
                      : "text-zinc-500 hover:text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-400 dark:hover:text-zinc-100"
                  }`}
                >
                  {l}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setTheme((prev) => (prev === "dark" ? "light" : "dark"))}
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-md border border-zinc-200 bg-white px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-medium text-zinc-700 hover:bg-zinc-100 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-200 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800"
            >
              {theme === "dark" ? (
                <>
                  <Sun className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                  <span>{copy.lightMode}</span>
                </>
              ) : (
                <>
                  <Moon className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                  <span>{copy.darkMode}</span>
                </>
              )}
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Main Content */}
      <main className="mx-auto max-w-7xl px-4 py-8 sm:px-6">
        {activeTab === "generator" && (
          <VideoPromptGeneratorView
            lang={lang}
            activeResult={activeGenResult}
            activeIdea={activeGenIdea}
            appliedProps={genPresetProps}
            onResultChange={(nextResult, nextIdea) => {
              setActiveGenResult(nextResult);
              setActiveGenIdea(nextIdea);
            }}
            savedPrompts={history}
            onLoadSavedPrompt={handleLoadHistoryEntry}
            onToggleFavoritePrompt={(id) =>
              setHistory((prev) =>
                prev.map((item) =>
                  item.id === id ? { ...item, isFavorite: !item.isFavorite } : item
                )
              )
            }
            onDeleteSavedPrompt={(id) =>
              setHistory((prev) => prev.filter((item) => item.id !== id))
            }
            savedSequences={savedSequences}
            onSaveSequence={handleSaveSequence}
            onLoadSequence={handleLoadSequence}
            onDeleteSequence={(id) =>
              setSavedSequences((prev) => prev.filter((item) => item.id !== id))
            }
            onSaveToHistory={handleSaveGeneratorResult}
            onSendToStudio={handleSendGeneratorToStudio}
            onNotify={notify}
          />
        )}

        {activeTab === "portal" && (
          <PortalView
            lang={lang}
            onOpenGenerator={() => setActiveTab("generator")}
            onOpenDirector={() => setActiveTab("director")}
            onOpenStudio={() => setActiveTab("studio")}
            onLaunchEngine={handleLaunchEngine}
            onLoadPreset={handleLoadPreset}
          />
        )}

        {activeTab === "director" && (
          <DirectorEngineView
            lang={lang}
            activeSnapshot={activeDirectorSnapshot}
            appliedProps={directorPresetProps}
            onSaveToHistory={handleSaveDirectorResult}
            onNotify={notify}
          />
        )}

        {activeTab === "studio" && (
          <ProStudioView
            lang={lang}
            form={form}
            setForm={setForm}
            outputText={outputText}
            setOutputText={setOutputText}
            onSaveEntry={handleSaveStudioEntry}
            onNotify={notify}
          />
        )}

        {activeTab === "guide" && (
          <EngineGuideView
            lang={lang}
            onUseEngine={handleLaunchEngine}
          />
        )}

        {activeTab === "history" && (
          <HistoryView
            lang={lang}
            history={history}
            savedSequences={savedSequences}
            onToggleFavorite={(id) =>
              setHistory((prev) =>
                prev.map((item) =>
                  item.id === id ? { ...item, isFavorite: !item.isFavorite } : item
                )
              )
            }
            onDeleteEntry={(id) =>
              setHistory((prev) => prev.filter((item) => item.id !== id))
            }
            onClearAll={() => setHistory([])}
            onLoadEntry={handleLoadHistoryEntry}
            onToggleFavoriteSequence={(id) =>
              setSavedSequences((prev) =>
                prev.map((item) =>
                  item.id === id ? { ...item, isFavorite: !item.isFavorite } : item
                )
              )
            }
            onDeleteSequence={(id) =>
              setSavedSequences((prev) => prev.filter((item) => item.id !== id))
            }
            onClearSequences={() => setSavedSequences([])}
            onLoadSequence={handleLoadSequence}
            onApplySequenceProps={handleApplySequenceProps}
            onApplyEntryProps={handleApplyHistoryEntryProps}
          />
        )}
      </main>
    </div>
  );
}

import {createRoot} from 'react-dom/client';
import App from './App.tsx';
import './index.css';

createRoot(document.getElementById('root')!).render(<App />);


####################################################################################################
FILE: src/components/VideoPromptGeneratorView.tsx
####################################################################################################

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import {
  BookmarkCheck,
  BookmarkPlus,
  Check,
  Clapperboard,
  Copy,
  Download,
  Film,
  Loader2,
  Save,
  SlidersHorizontal,
  Sparkles,
  Star,
  Trash2,
  Wand2,
} from "lucide-react";
import { VIDEO_GENERATOR_STYLES } from "../data/engines";
import { I18N } from "../data/i18n";
import type {
  HistoryEntry,
  Language,
  SavedSequenceEntry,
  VideoGeneratorLang,
  VideoGeneratorMode,
  VideoGeneratorModel,
  VideoGeneratorRequest,
  VideoGeneratorResult,
} from "../types/prompt";
import { safeFetchJson } from "../utils/safeFetchJson";

interface VideoPromptGeneratorViewProps {
  lang: Language;
  activeResult: VideoGeneratorResult | null;
  activeIdea: string;
  appliedProps?: (Partial<VideoGeneratorRequest> & { nonce: number }) | null;
  onResultChange: (result: VideoGeneratorResult | null, idea: string) => void;
  savedPrompts: HistoryEntry[];
  onLoadSavedPrompt: (entry: HistoryEntry) => void;
  onToggleFavoritePrompt: (id: string) => void;
  onDeleteSavedPrompt: (id: string) => void;
  savedSequences: SavedSequenceEntry[];
  onSaveSequence: (result: VideoGeneratorResult, idea: string) => boolean;
  onLoadSequence: (seq: SavedSequenceEntry) => void;
  onDeleteSequence: (id: string) => void;
  onSaveToHistory: (
    result: VideoGeneratorResult,
    idea: string,
    options?: { silent?: boolean; favorite?: boolean }
  ) => void;
  onSendToStudio: (result: VideoGeneratorResult, idea: string) => void;
  onNotify: (msg: string, type: "ok" | "err") => void;
}

const MODEL_OPTIONS: {
  id: VideoGeneratorModel;
  label: string;
  spec: string;
}[] = [
  { id: "wan", label: "Wan 2.2", spec: "--duration N · single shot per prompt" },
  { id: "minimax", label: "MiniMax (Hailuo)", spec: "[Shot 1] / [Shot 2] · max 15s" },
  { id: "google", label: "Google Veo", spec: "Natural cinema · 1–3 sentences" },
];

export function VideoPromptGeneratorView({
  lang,
  activeResult,
  activeIdea,
  appliedProps,
  onResultChange,
  savedPrompts,
  onLoadSavedPrompt,
  onToggleFavoritePrompt,
  onDeleteSavedPrompt,
  savedSequences,
  onSaveSequence,
  onLoadSequence,
  onDeleteSequence,
  onSaveToHistory,
  onSendToStudio,
  onNotify,
}: VideoPromptGeneratorViewProps) {
  const copy = I18N[lang];

  const [idea, setIdea] = useState(activeIdea);
  const [model, setModel] = useState<VideoGeneratorModel>(
    activeResult?.model || "wan"
  );
  const [mode, setMode] = useState<VideoGeneratorMode>(
    activeResult?.mode || "normal"
  );
  const [scenes, setScenes] = useState(
    activeResult && activeResult.scenes.length > 0
      ? activeResult.scenes.length
      : 10
  );
  const [shotLen, setShotLen] = useState(activeResult?.shotLen || 5);
  const [style, setStyle] = useState(activeResult?.style || "cinematic");
  const [customStyle, setCustomStyle] = useState("");
  const [outputLang, setOutputLang] = useState<VideoGeneratorLang>(
    activeResult?.lang || (lang === "ar" ? "ar" : "en")
  );
  const [allowMinimaxMerge, setAllowMinimaxMerge] = useState(false);

  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [copiedKey, setCopiedKey] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [justSavedSequence, setJustSavedSequence] = useState(false);
  const [justSavedPrompt, setJustSavedPrompt] = useState(false);
  const [libraryTab, setLibraryTab] = useState<"prompts" | "sequences">("prompts");

  // Keep form synced when loading a saved sequence or resetting to a new prompt
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!activeResult && activeIdea === "") {
      setIdea("");
      setJustSavedSequence(false);
      setJustSavedPrompt(false);
      return;
    }
    if (activeIdea && activeIdea !== idea) {
      setIdea(activeIdea);
    }
    if (activeResult) {
      setModel(activeResult.model);
      setMode(activeResult.mode);
      if (activeResult.scenes.length > 0) {
        setScenes(activeResult.scenes.length);
      }
      setShotLen(activeResult.shotLen);
      const isKnownStyle = VIDEO_GENERATOR_STYLES.some(
        (s) => s.id === activeResult.style
      );
      if (isKnownStyle) {
        setStyle(activeResult.style);
        setCustomStyle("");
      } else if (activeResult.style) {
        setCustomStyle(activeResult.style);
      }
      setOutputLang(activeResult.lang);
    }
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [activeResult, activeIdea]);

  // Apply reused prompt properties from HistoryView without overwriting current idea
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!appliedProps) return;
    if (appliedProps.model) setModel(appliedProps.model);
    if (appliedProps.mode) setMode(appliedProps.mode);
    if (typeof appliedProps.scenes === "number" && appliedProps.scenes > 0) {
      setScenes(appliedProps.scenes);
    }
    if (typeof appliedProps.shotLen === "number" && appliedProps.shotLen > 0) {
      setShotLen(appliedProps.shotLen);
    }
    if (appliedProps.style) {
      const isKnown = VIDEO_GENERATOR_STYLES.some((s) => s.id === appliedProps.style);
      if (isKnown) {
        setStyle(appliedProps.style);
        setCustomStyle("");
      } else {
        setCustomStyle(appliedProps.style);
      }
    }
    if (appliedProps.lang) setOutputLang(appliedProps.lang);
  }, [appliedProps]);

  const effectiveStyle = customStyle.trim() || style;
  const maxShotLen = model === "minimax" ? 15 : 20;
  const isFilmSelection = mode === "film" || mode === "film_10";
  const effectiveScenes = Math.min(30, Math.max(2, scenes));

  const handleModelChange = (nextModel: VideoGeneratorModel) => {
    setModel(nextModel);
    if (nextModel === "minimax" && shotLen > 15) {
      setShotLen(15);
    }
  };

  const handleModeChange = (nextMode: VideoGeneratorMode) => {
    setMode(nextMode);
    if (nextMode === "film_10") {
      setScenes(10);
    }
  };

  const handleLoadExample = (preset: "wan-normal" | "minimax-film") => {
    setError(null);
    if (preset === "wan-normal") {
      setIdea("مهندس يبحث عن مدينة مفقودة في الصحراء");
      setModel("wan");
      setMode("normal");
      setShotLen(5);
      setStyle("cinematic");
      setCustomStyle("");
      setOutputLang("ar");
    } else {
      setIdea("مهندس يبحث عن مدينة مفقودة في الصحراء");
      setModel("minimax");
      setMode("film_10");
      setScenes(10);
      setShotLen(5);
      setStyle("cinematic");
      setCustomStyle("");
      setOutputLang("ar");
    }
  };

  const handleGenerate = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!idea.trim()) {
      setError(
        lang === "ar"
          ? "يرجى إدخال فكرة الفيديو أولاً."
          : lang === "fr"
            ? "Veuillez saisir une idée de vidéo."
            : "Please enter a video concept first."
      );
      return;
    }

    setLoading(true);
    setError(null);
    setJustSavedSequence(false);
    setJustSavedPrompt(false);
    try {
      const data = await safeFetchJson<VideoGeneratorResult>(
        "/api/ai/video-generator",
        {
          method: "POST",
          headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
          body: JSON.stringify({
            idea: idea.trim(),
            model,
            mode,
            scenes: effectiveScenes,
            shotLen: Math.min(maxShotLen, Math.max(2, shotLen)),
            style: effectiveStyle,
            lang: outputLang,
            allowMinimaxMerge,
            previousScenes:
              activeResult &&
              activeIdea.trim() === idea.trim() &&
              activeResult.scenes.length > 0 &&
              activeResult.scenes.length < effectiveScenes
                ? activeResult.scenes
                : [],
            previousConsistencyNotes:
              activeResult && activeIdea.trim() === idea.trim()
                ? activeResult.consistencyNotes
                : null,
          }),
        },
        lang
      );
      onResultChange(data, idea.trim());
      onSaveToHistory(data, idea.trim(), { silent: true });
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Failed to generate prompt.";
      setError(msg);
      onNotify(msg, "err");
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const handleCopy = async (text: string, key: string) => {
    try {
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);
      setCopiedKey(key);
      setTimeout(() => {
        setCopiedKey((prev) => (prev === key ? null : prev));
      }, 1600);
    } catch {
      onNotify("Clipboard copy failed.", "err");
    }
  };

  const handleSaveCurrentPrompt = () => {
    if (!activeResult) return;
    onSaveToHistory(activeResult, idea.trim() || activeIdea, { favorite: true });
    setJustSavedPrompt(true);
    setTimeout(() => setJustSavedPrompt(false), 2000);
  };

  const handleSaveCurrentSequence = () => {
    if (!activeResult) return;
    const saved = onSaveSequence(activeResult, idea.trim() || activeIdea);
    if (saved) {
      setJustSavedSequence(true);
      setTimeout(() => setJustSavedSequence(false), 2000);
    }
  };

  const handleExportSequenceJson = (resToExport: VideoGeneratorResult, titleText: string) => {
    const payload = {
      title: titleText,
      ...resToExport,
      exportedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    };
    const blob = new Blob([JSON.stringify(payload, null, 2)], {
      type: "application/json",
    });
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const a = document.createElement("a");
    a.href = url;
    a.download = `sequence-${resToExport.model}-${resToExport.scenes.length || 1}scenes.json`;
    a.click();
    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
  };

  const result = activeResult;
  const resultIsSequence =
    Boolean(result) &&
    (result?.mode === "film" || result?.mode === "film_10") &&
    (result?.scenes.length ?? 0) > 0;

  return (
    <div className="space-y-8">
      {/* Header summary */}
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 border-b border-zinc-200 pb-5 dark:border-zinc-800 md:flex-row md:items-end md:justify-between">
        <div className="space-y-1.5">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400">
            <span>Video Prompt Generator v1.0</span>
            <span aria-hidden="true">·</span>
            <span>Wan 2.2</span>
            <span aria-hidden="true">·</span>
            <span>MiniMax Hailuo</span>
            <span aria-hidden="true">·</span>
            <span>Google Veo</span>
          </div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-semibold tracking-tight text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-50">
            {copy.genTitle}
          </h1>
          <p className="max-w-3xl text-sm leading-relaxed text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400">
            {copy.genSubtitle}
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 shrink-0">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => handleLoadExample("wan-normal")}
            className="whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 rounded-lg border border-zinc-200 bg-white px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-zinc-700 transition-colors hover:bg-zinc-100 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-200 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800"
          >
            {copy.genSampleNormal}
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => handleLoadExample("minimax-film")}
            className="whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 rounded-lg border border-zinc-200 bg-white px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-zinc-700 transition-colors hover:bg-zinc-100 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-200 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800"
          >
            {copy.genSampleFilm}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Main 2-column workbench */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-6 lg:grid-cols-12">
        {/* Left: Generator Inputs */}
        <form
          onSubmit={handleGenerate}
          className="space-y-5 rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 bg-white p-5 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900/70 lg:col-span-5"
        >
          {/* IDEA */}
          <div className="space-y-2">
            <label
              htmlFor="vpg-idea"
              className="block text-sm font-medium text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100"
            >
              {copy.genIdeaLabel}
            </label>
            <textarea
              id="vpg-idea"
              rows={4}
              value={idea}
              onChange={(e) => setIdea(e.target.value)}
              placeholder={copy.genIdeaPlaceholder}
              className="w-full rounded-lg border border-zinc-200 bg-zinc-50 px-3.5 py-2.5 text-sm text-zinc-900 placeholder:text-zinc-400 focus:border-amber-500 focus:outline-none dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-100 dark:placeholder:text-zinc-600"
            />
          </div>

          {/* MODEL */}
          <div className="space-y-2">
            <span className="block text-sm font-medium text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100">
              {copy.genModelLabel}
            </span>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-2 sm:grid-cols-3">
              {MODEL_OPTIONS.map((opt) => {
                const active = model === opt.id;
                return (
                  <button
                    key={opt.id}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => handleModelChange(opt.id)}
                    className={`rounded-lg border px-3 py-2.5 text-start transition-colors ${
                      active
                        ? "border-amber-500 bg-amber-500/10 text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-50"
                        : "border-zinc-200 bg-zinc-50 text-zinc-600 hover:border-zinc-300 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-400 dark:hover:border-zinc-700"
                    }`}
                  >
                    <div className="text-sm font-medium">{opt.label}</div>
                    <div className="mt-0.5 text-[11px] text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400">
                      {opt.spec}
                    </div>
                  </button>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* MODE */}
          <div className="space-y-2">
            <span className="block text-sm font-medium text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100">
              {copy.genModeLabel}
            </span>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-1.5 rounded-lg bg-zinc-100 p-1 dark:bg-zinc-950">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => handleModeChange("normal")}
                className={`flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-md px-2 py-2 text-xs font-medium transition-colors whitespace-nowrap ${
                  mode === "normal"
                    ? "bg-white text-zinc-900 shadow-xs dark:bg-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-50"
                    : "text-zinc-600 hover:text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-400 dark:hover:text-zinc-200"
                }`}
              >
                <Sparkles className="h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0" />
                <span className="truncate">{copy.genModeNormal}</span>
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => handleModeChange("film_10")}
                className={`flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-md px-2 py-2 text-xs font-medium transition-colors whitespace-nowrap ${
                  mode === "film_10"
                    ? "bg-white text-zinc-900 shadow-xs dark:bg-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-50"
                    : "text-zinc-600 hover:text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-400 dark:hover:text-zinc-200"
                }`}
              >
                <Film className="h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0" />
                <span className="truncate">{copy.genModeFilm10}</span>
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => handleModeChange("film")}
                className={`flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-md px-2 py-2 text-xs font-medium transition-colors whitespace-nowrap ${
                  mode === "film"
                    ? "bg-white text-zinc-900 shadow-xs dark:bg-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-50"
                    : "text-zinc-600 hover:text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-400 dark:hover:text-zinc-200"
                }`}
              >
                <SlidersHorizontal className="h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0" />
                <span className="truncate">{copy.genModeFilm}</span>
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>

          {isFilmSelection && (
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between rounded-lg border border-amber-500/30 bg-amber-500/5 px-3.5 py-2.5 text-xs text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-200">
              <span>
                {lang === "ar"
                  ? scenes >= 30
                    ? "فيلم مكتمل من 30 مشهداً: 10 مشاهد أساسية + 20 مشهداً إضافياً، مع الحفاظ على الحبكة والاستمرارية."
                    : "ابدأ بفيلم من 10 مشاهد، أو وسّعه حتى 30 مشهداً بإضافة 20 مشهداً مع الحفاظ على الحبكة والاستمرارية."
                  : lang === "fr"
                    ? scenes >= 30
                      ? "Film complet de 30 scènes : 10 scènes de base + 20 supplémentaires, avec continuité narrative."
                      : "Commencez avec 10 scènes et développez jusqu'à 30 scènes en préservant la continuité."
                    : scenes >= 30
                      ? "Complete 30-scene film: 10 base scenes + 20 additional scenes, with story continuity preserved."
                      : "Start with 10 scenes and expand up to 30 while preserving story continuity."}
              </span>
              <span className="font-mono font-semibold tabular-nums text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400">
                {effectiveScenes}
              </span>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Numeric controls: SCENES (for film / film_10) & SHOT_LEN */}
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
            {isFilmSelection && (
              <div className="space-y-1.5">
                <label
                  htmlFor="vpg-scenes-select"
                  className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs font-medium text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300"
                >
                  <span>{copy.genScenesLabel}</span>
                  <span className="font-mono tabular-nums text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400">
                    {effectiveScenes}
                  </span>
                </label>
                <select
                  id="vpg-scenes-select"
                  value={effectiveScenes}
                  onChange={(e) => {
                    const count = Number(e.target.value);
                    setScenes(count);
                    setMode(count === 10 ? "film_10" : "film");
                  }}
                  className="w-full rounded-lg border border-zinc-200 bg-zinc-50 px-3 py-2 text-sm text-zinc-900 focus:border-amber-500 focus:outline-none dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-100"
                >
                  {[2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 8, 10, 15, 20, 30].map((count) => (
                    <option key={count} value={count}>
                      {lang === "ar"
                        ? `${count} مشهد${count === 10 ? " (الفيلم الأساسي)" : count === 30 ? " (10 + 20 مشهداً إضافياً)" : ""}`
                        : lang === "fr"
                          ? `${count} scènes${count === 10 ? " (Film de base)" : count === 30 ? " (10 + 20 scènes supplémentaires)" : ""}`
                          : `${count} Scenes${count === 10 ? " (Base Film)" : count === 30 ? " (10 + 20 additional scenes)" : ""}`}
                    </option>
                  ))}
                </select>
                <input
                    id="vpg-scenes"
                    type="range"
                    min={2}
                    max={30}
                    value={scenes}
                    onChange={(e) => {
                      const count = Number(e.target.value);
                      setScenes(count);
                      setMode(count === 10 ? "film_10" : "film");
                    }}
                    className="w-full accent-amber-500"
                />
              </div>
            )}

            <div className={`space-y-1.5 ${isFilmSelection ? "" : "sm:col-span-2"}`}>
              <label
                htmlFor="vpg-shot-len"
                className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs font-medium text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300"
              >
                <span>{copy.genShotLenLabel}</span>
                <span className="font-mono tabular-nums text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400">
                  {shotLen}s
                </span>
              </label>
              <input
                id="vpg-shot-len"
                type="range"
                min={3}
                max={maxShotLen}
                value={Math.min(maxShotLen, shotLen)}
                onChange={(e) => setShotLen(Number(e.target.value))}
                className="w-full accent-amber-500"
              />
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* STYLE & LANG */}
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
            <div className="space-y-1.5">
              <label
                htmlFor="vpg-style"
                className="block text-xs font-medium text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300"
              >
                {copy.genStyleLabel}
              </label>
              <select
                id="vpg-style"
                value={style}
                onChange={(e) => setStyle(e.target.value)}
                className="w-full rounded-lg border border-zinc-200 bg-zinc-50 px-3 py-2 text-sm text-zinc-900 focus:border-amber-500 focus:outline-none dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-100"
              >
                {VIDEO_GENERATOR_STYLES.map((st) => (
                  <option key={st.id} value={st.id}>
                    {st[lang]}
                  </option>
                ))}
              </select>
            </div>

            <div className="space-y-1.5">
              <label
                htmlFor="vpg-lang"
                className="block text-xs font-medium text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300"
              >
                {copy.genLangLabel}
              </label>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-1.5 rounded-lg bg-zinc-100 p-1 dark:bg-zinc-950">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setOutputLang("ar")}
                  className={`rounded-md py-1.5 text-xs font-medium transition-colors ${
                    outputLang === "ar"
                      ? "bg-white text-zinc-900 shadow-xs dark:bg-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-50"
                      : "text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400"
                  }`}
                >
                  العربية (ar)
                </button>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setOutputLang("en")}
                  className={`rounded-md py-1.5 text-xs font-medium transition-colors ${
                    outputLang === "en"
                      ? "bg-white text-zinc-900 shadow-xs dark:bg-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-50"
                      : "text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400"
                  }`}
                >
                  English (en)
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Optional Custom Style */}
          <div className="space-y-1.5">
            <input
              type="text"
              value={customStyle}
              onChange={(e) => setCustomStyle(e.target.value)}
              placeholder={
                lang === "ar"
                  ? "أسلوب مخصص اختياري (مثلاً: 1970s Moroccan documentary, Kodak 16mm)..."
                  : "Optional custom style override..."
              }
              className="w-full rounded-lg border border-zinc-200 bg-zinc-50 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-zinc-900 placeholder:text-zinc-400 focus:border-amber-500 focus:outline-none dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-100"
            />
          </div>

          {model === "minimax" && isFilmSelection && (
            <label className="flex cursor-pointer items-center gap-2.5 rounded-lg border border-zinc-200 bg-zinc-50 px-3 py-2.5 text-xs text-zinc-700 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-300">
              <input
                type="checkbox"
                checked={allowMinimaxMerge}
                onChange={(e) => setAllowMinimaxMerge(e.target.checked)}
                className="h-4 w-4 accent-amber-500"
              />
              <span>{copy.genMinimaxMergeLabel}</span>
            </label>
          )}

          {error && (
            <div className="rounded-lg border border-red-300 bg-red-50 px-3.5 py-2.5 text-xs text-red-700 dark:border-red-900/60 dark:bg-red-950/40 dark:text-red-300">
              {error}
            </div>
          )}

          <button
            type="submit"
            disabled={loading}
            className="flex w-full items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-lg bg-amber-500 px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold text-zinc-950 transition-colors hover:bg-amber-400 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-60"
          >
            {loading ? (
              <>
                <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" />
                <span>{copy.genGenerating}</span>
              </>
            ) : (
              <>
                <Wand2 className="h-4 w-4" />
                <span>{copy.genGenerateBtn}</span>
              </>
            )}
          </button>
        </form>

        {/* Right: Structured Output Panel */}
        <div className="space-y-5 lg:col-span-7">
          {!result && !loading && (
            <div className="flex min-h-[420px] flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-xl border border-dashed border-zinc-300 bg-white/60 p-8 text-center dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900/40">
              <Clapperboard className="mb-3 h-9 w-9 text-zinc-400 dark:text-zinc-600" />
              <h2 className="text-base font-medium text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100">
                {copy.genEmptyTitle}
              </h2>
              <p className="mt-1.5 max-w-md text-sm text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400">
                {copy.genEmptyDesc}
              </p>
              <div className="mt-6 grid w-full max-w-lg grid-cols-3 gap-3 border-t border-zinc-200 pt-5 text-start text-xs dark:border-zinc-800">
                <div>
                  <div className="font-medium text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-200">Wan 2.2</div>
                  <div className="mt-1 text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400">
                    --duration N · single shot
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div>
                  <div className="font-medium text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-200">MiniMax</div>
                  <div className="mt-1 text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400">
                    [Shot 1] / [Shot 2] ≤ 15s
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div>
                  <div className="font-medium text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-200">Google Veo</div>
                  <div className="mt-1 text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400">
                    Maintain: same lens & palette
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {loading && (
            <div className="flex min-h-[420px] flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 bg-white p-8 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900/70">
              <Loader2 className="h-7 w-7 animate-spin text-amber-500" />
              <p className="mt-3 text-sm text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400">
                {copy.genGenerating}
              </p>
            </div>
          )}

          {result && !loading && (
            <div className="space-y-4">
              {/* Header + Actions */}
              <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 bg-white px-4 py-3 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900/70">
                <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 text-xs text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-300">
                  <span className="font-semibold text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100">
                    {copy.genOutputTitle}
                  </span>
                  <span aria-hidden="true">·</span>
                  <span className="font-mono uppercase">{result.model}</span>
                  <span aria-hidden="true">·</span>
                  <span className="font-mono uppercase">{result.mode}</span>
                  {resultIsSequence && (
                    <>
                      <span aria-hidden="true">·</span>
                      <span className="font-mono tabular-nums">
                        {result.scenes.length} scenes
                      </span>
                    </>
                  )}
                </div>

                <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => handleCopy(result.formattedOutput, "all")}
                    className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 whitespace-nowrap rounded-lg bg-zinc-900 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-white transition-colors hover:bg-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-100 dark:text-zinc-900 dark:hover:bg-zinc-200"
                  >
                    {copiedKey === "all" ? (
                      <Check className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                    ) : (
                      <Copy className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                    )}
                    <span>{copiedKey === "all" ? copy.genCopied : copy.genCopyAll}</span>
                  </button>

                  {resultIsSequence && (
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={handleSaveCurrentSequence}
                      className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 whitespace-nowrap rounded-lg bg-amber-500 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-950 transition-colors hover:bg-amber-400"
                    >
                      {justSavedSequence ? (
                        <BookmarkCheck className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                      ) : (
                        <BookmarkPlus className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                      )}
                      <span>
                        {justSavedSequence ? copy.genSequenceSaved : copy.genSaveSequence}
                      </span>
                    </button>
                  )}

                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={handleSaveCurrentPrompt}
                    className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 whitespace-nowrap rounded-lg px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold transition-colors ${
                      resultIsSequence
                        ? "border border-zinc-200 bg-white text-zinc-700 hover:bg-zinc-100 dark:border-zinc-700 dark:bg-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-200 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800"
                        : "bg-amber-500 text-zinc-950 hover:bg-amber-400"
                    }`}
                  >
                    {justSavedPrompt ? (
                      <BookmarkCheck className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                    ) : (
                      <Save className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                    )}
                    <span>{justSavedPrompt ? copy.genPromptSaved : copy.genSavePrompt}</span>
                  </button>

                  {resultIsSequence && (
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() =>
                        handleExportSequenceJson(result, idea.trim() || "film-sequence")
                      }
                      className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 whitespace-nowrap rounded-lg border border-zinc-200 bg-white px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-zinc-700 transition-colors hover:bg-zinc-100 dark:border-zinc-700 dark:bg-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-200 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800"
                    >
                      <Download className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                      <span>{copy.genExportSequence}</span>
                    </button>
                  )}

                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => onSendToStudio(result, idea)}
                    className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 whitespace-nowrap rounded-lg border border-zinc-200 bg-white px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-zinc-700 transition-colors hover:bg-zinc-100 dark:border-zinc-700 dark:bg-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-200 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800"
                  >
                    <SlidersHorizontal className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                    <span>{copy.genOpenInStudio}</span>
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Canonical Section 7 Raw Output Block */}
              <div className="rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 bg-zinc-50 p-4 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-950">
                <pre
                  dir="ltr"
                  className="overflow-x-auto whitespace-pre-wrap text-left font-mono text-xs leading-relaxed text-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-200"
                >
                  {result.formattedOutput}
                </pre>
              </div>

              {/* Per-Scene Quick Copy Cards in Film / Film-10 Mode */}
              {resultIsSequence && (
                <div className="space-y-3">
                  {result.scenes.map((scene) => {
                    const key = `scene-${scene.sceneNumber}`;
                    const cleanSceneCopy = scene.prompt
                      .replace(/\n→.*$/gm, "")
                      .trim();
                    return (
                      <div
                        key={scene.sceneNumber}
                        className="rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 bg-white p-4 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900/70"
                      >
                        <div className="mb-2 flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
                          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs">
                            <span className="font-mono font-semibold tabular-nums text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400">
                              SCENE {scene.sceneNumber}/{result.scenes.length}
                            </span>
                            {scene.narrativeBeat && (
                              <>
                                <span aria-hidden="true">·</span>
                                <span className="text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400">
                                  {scene.narrativeBeat}
                                </span>
                              </>
                            )}
                          </div>
                          <button
                            type="button"
                            onClick={() => handleCopy(cleanSceneCopy, key)}
                            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 whitespace-nowrap rounded-md border border-zinc-200 px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-medium text-zinc-700 transition-colors hover:bg-zinc-100 dark:border-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-200 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800"
                          >
                            {copiedKey === key ? (
                              <Check className="h-3 w-3 text-emerald-500" />
                            ) : (
                              <Copy className="h-3 w-3" />
                            )}
                            <span>
                              {copiedKey === key ? copy.genCopied : copy.genCopyScene}
                            </span>
                          </button>
                        </div>
                        <p
                          dir="ltr"
                          className="whitespace-pre-wrap text-left font-mono text-xs leading-relaxed text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300"
                        >
                          {scene.prompt}
                        </p>
                      </div>
                    );
                  })}

                  {result.consistencyNotes && (
                    <div className="rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 bg-white p-4 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900/70">
                      <h3 className="mb-3 text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400">
                        {copy.genConsistencyTitle}
                      </h3>
                      <dl className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 text-xs sm:grid-cols-3" dir="ltr">
                        <div>
                          <dt className="font-medium text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400">
                            Character
                          </dt>
                          <dd className="mt-1 text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100">
                            {result.consistencyNotes.character}
                          </dd>
                        </div>
                        <div>
                          <dt className="font-medium text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400">
                            Palette
                          </dt>
                          <dd className="mt-1 text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100">
                            {result.consistencyNotes.palette}
                          </dd>
                        </div>
                        <div>
                          <dt className="font-medium text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400">
                            Lens
                          </dt>
                          <dd className="mt-1 text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100">
                            {result.consistencyNotes.lens}
                          </dd>
                        </div>
                      </dl>
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Saved Prompts & Sequences Library directly inside Generator v1.0 */}
      <section className="space-y-4 border-t border-zinc-200 pt-6 dark:border-zinc-800">
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
          <div className="inline-flex rounded-lg bg-zinc-100 p-1 dark:bg-zinc-900">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setLibraryTab("prompts")}
              className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-md px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium transition-colors whitespace-nowrap ${
                libraryTab === "prompts"
                  ? "bg-white text-zinc-900 shadow-xs dark:bg-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-50"
                  : "text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400"
              }`}
            >
              <Save className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
              <span>{copy.genSavedPromptsTitle}</span>
              <span className="font-mono tabular-nums text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400">
                ({savedPrompts.length})
              </span>
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setLibraryTab("sequences")}
              className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-md px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium transition-colors whitespace-nowrap ${
                libraryTab === "sequences"
                  ? "bg-white text-zinc-900 shadow-xs dark:bg-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-50"
                  : "text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400"
              }`}
            >
              <Film className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
              <span>{copy.genSavedSequencesTitle}</span>
              <span className="font-mono tabular-nums text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400">
                ({savedSequences.length})
              </span>
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        {libraryTab === "prompts" ? (
          savedPrompts.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="rounded-xl border border-dashed border-zinc-300 bg-white/50 p-6 text-center text-xs text-zinc-500 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900/30 dark:text-zinc-400">
              {copy.genSavedPromptsEmpty}
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 md:grid-cols-2">
              {savedPrompts.map((item) => (
                <div
                  key={item.id}
                  className="flex flex-col justify-between space-y-3 rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 bg-white p-4 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900/70"
                >
                  <div className="space-y-1.5">
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 text-xs text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400">
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                        <span className="font-mono uppercase">{item.engine}</span>
                        <span aria-hidden="true">·</span>
                        <span className="uppercase">{item.mode}</span>
                        <span aria-hidden="true">·</span>
                        <span className="font-mono tabular-nums">{item.time}</span>
                      </div>
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => onToggleFavoritePrompt(item.id)}
                        aria-label="Toggle favorite prompt"
                        className="rounded p-1 text-zinc-400 hover:text-amber-500"
                      >
                        <Star
                          className={`h-3.5 w-3.5 ${
                            item.isFavorite ? "fill-amber-400 text-amber-400" : ""
                          }`}
                        />
                      </button>
                    </div>
                    <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100">
                      {item.title}
                    </h3>
                    <p
                      dir="ltr"
                      className="line-clamp-2 text-left font-mono text-xs text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400"
                    >
                      {item.positiveOnly || item.prompt}
                    </p>
                  </div>

                  <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 pt-1">
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => onLoadSavedPrompt(item)}
                      className="rounded-md bg-zinc-900 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-white hover:bg-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-100 dark:text-zinc-900 dark:hover:bg-zinc-200"
                    >
                      {copy.genLoadPrompt}
                    </button>
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => handleCopy(item.prompt, `prompt-${item.id}`)}
                      className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-md border border-zinc-200 px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-zinc-700 hover:bg-zinc-100 dark:border-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-200 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800"
                    >
                      {copiedKey === `prompt-${item.id}` ? (
                        <Check className="h-3 w-3 text-emerald-500" />
                      ) : (
                        <Copy className="h-3 w-3" />
                      )}
                      <span>
                        {copiedKey === `prompt-${item.id}` ? copy.genCopied : copy.genCopyScene}
                      </span>
                    </button>
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => onDeleteSavedPrompt(item.id)}
                      aria-label="Delete saved prompt"
                      className="ms-auto rounded-md border border-zinc-200 p-1.5 text-zinc-500 hover:text-red-500 dark:border-zinc-800"
                    >
                      <Trash2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          )
        ) : savedSequences.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="rounded-xl border border-dashed border-zinc-300 bg-white/50 p-6 text-center text-xs text-zinc-500 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900/30 dark:text-zinc-400">
            {copy.genSavedSequencesEmpty}
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 md:grid-cols-2">
            {savedSequences.map((seq) => (
              <div
                key={seq.id}
                className="flex flex-col justify-between space-y-3 rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 bg-white p-4 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900/70"
              >
                <div className="space-y-1">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400">
                    <span className="font-mono uppercase">{seq.model}</span>
                    <span aria-hidden="true">·</span>
                    <span className="font-mono tabular-nums">
                      {seq.scenes.length} scenes
                    </span>
                    <span aria-hidden="true">·</span>
                    <span>{seq.style}</span>
                    <span aria-hidden="true">·</span>
                    <span className="font-mono tabular-nums">
                      {new Date(seq.createdAt).toLocaleTimeString([], {
                        hour: "2-digit",
                        minute: "2-digit",
                      })}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100">
                    {seq.title}
                  </h3>
                </div>

                <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 pt-1">
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => onLoadSequence(seq)}
                    className="rounded-md bg-zinc-900 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-white hover:bg-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-100 dark:text-zinc-900 dark:hover:bg-zinc-200"
                  >
                    {copy.genLoadSequence}
                  </button>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => handleCopy(seq.formattedOutput, `seq-${seq.id}`)}
                    className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-md border border-zinc-200 px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-zinc-700 hover:bg-zinc-100 dark:border-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-200 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800"
                  >
                    {copiedKey === `seq-${seq.id}` ? (
                      <Check className="h-3 w-3 text-emerald-500" />
                    ) : (
                      <Copy className="h-3 w-3" />
                    )}
                    <span>
                      {copiedKey === `seq-${seq.id}` ? copy.genCopied : copy.genCopyAll}
                    </span>
                  </button>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => onDeleteSequence(seq.id)}
                    aria-label="Delete saved sequence"
                    className="ms-auto rounded-md border border-zinc-200 p-1.5 text-zinc-500 hover:text-red-500 dark:border-zinc-800"
                  >
                    <Trash2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}


####################################################################################################
FILE: src/components/PromptOutputPanel.tsx
####################################################################################################

import { useState } from "react";
import {
  Ban,
  Check,
  ChevronDown,
  ChevronUp,
  Code2,
  Copy,
  Download,
  FileText,
  Loader2,
  ShieldCheck,
  Sparkles,
  Trash2,
  Volume2,
} from "lucide-react";
import { I18N } from "../data/i18n";
import type { Language } from "../types/prompt";
import type { CompiledPromptResult } from "../utils/promptEngine";

interface PromptOutputPanelProps {
  lang: Language;
  engineName: string;
  compiled: CompiledPromptResult;
  outputText: string;
  onChangeOutput: (next: string) => void;
  onUnslop: () => void;
  onFitLength: () => void;
  fitting: boolean;
  onConvertFormat: (target: "hailuo02" | "veo31") => void;
  converting: "hailuo02" | "veo31" | null;
  onExportComfy: () => void;
  onExportPython: () => void;
  onNotify: (msg: string, type: "ok" | "err") => void;
}

const PANEL_LABELS: Record<
  Language,
  {
    visualTitle: string;
    visualSubtitle: string;
    copyPositive: string;
    negativeTitle: string;
    negativeSubtitle: string;
    negativeEmpty: string;
    copyNegative: string;
    audioTitle: string;
    audioSubtitle: string;
    audioEmpty: string;
    copyAudio: string;
    toolsTitle: string;
    guardTitle: string;
    copyRules: string;
    copyError: string;
    visualWords: string;
    tokens: string;
  }
> = {
  ar: {
    visualTitle: "البرومت البصري (Positive)",
    visualSubtitle: "النص الأساسي لتوليد المشهد",
    copyPositive: "نسخ البصري",
    negativeTitle: "البرومت السلبي (Negative)",
    negativeSubtitle: "العناصر المستبعدة ورموز العزل",
    negativeEmpty: "لا يوجد برومت سلبي مفعّل لهذا المحرك أو الإعداد.",
    copyNegative: "نسخ السلبي",
    audioTitle: "وصف الصوت (Audio Description)",
    audioSubtitle: "المؤثرات والتصميم الصوتي المنفصل",
    audioEmpty: "لم يتم تحديد وصف صوتي — فعّل خيار الصوت لإدراجه هنا.",
    copyAudio: "نسخ الصوت",
    toolsTitle: "المعايرة والتحويل والتصدير",
    guardTitle: "بيانات الحماية النظامية (مستبعدة من برومت الفيديو)",
    copyRules: "نسخ القواعد",
    copyError: "تعذر النسخ إلى الحافظة.",
    visualWords: "كلمة بصرية",
    tokens: "توكن",
  },
  fr: {
    visualTitle: "Prompt Visuel (Positive)",
    visualSubtitle: "Instruction principale de génération",
    copyPositive: "Copier Positif",
    negativeTitle: "Prompt Négatif (Negative)",
    negativeSubtitle: "Éléments exclus et tokens d'isolation",
    negativeEmpty: "Aucun prompt négatif actif pour ce moteur.",
    copyNegative: "Copier Négatif",
    audioTitle: "Description Audio (Audio Description)",
    audioSubtitle: "Design sonore et effets séparés",
    audioEmpty: "Aucune description audio — activez l'audio pour l'afficher ici.",
    copyAudio: "Copier Audio",
    toolsTitle: "Calibration, Conversion & Export",
    guardTitle: "Métadonnées système (exclues du prompt vidéo)",
    copyRules: "Copier les règles",
    copyError: "Échec de la copie dans le presse-papiers.",
    visualWords: "mots visuels",
    tokens: "tokens",
  },
  en: {
    visualTitle: "Visual Prompt (Positive)",
    visualSubtitle: "Primary scene generation prompt",
    copyPositive: "Copy Positive",
    negativeTitle: "Negative Prompt (Negative)",
    negativeSubtitle: "Excluded artifacts & scene isolation tokens",
    negativeEmpty: "No negative prompt configured for this engine.",
    copyNegative: "Copy Negative",
    audioTitle: "Audio Description",
    audioSubtitle: "Separated sound design & foley cues",
    audioEmpty: "No audio description active — enable audio in the studio to populate.",
    copyAudio: "Copy Audio",
    toolsTitle: "Calibration, Format & Export",
    guardTitle: "System Instruction Metadata (Excluded from video prompt)",
    copyRules: "Copy Rules",
    copyError: "Clipboard copy failed.",
    visualWords: "visual words",
    tokens: "tokens",
  },
};

export function PromptOutputPanel({
  lang,
  engineName,
  compiled,
  outputText,
  onChangeOutput,
  onUnslop,
  onFitLength,
  fitting,
  onConvertFormat,
  converting,
  onExportComfy,
  onExportPython,
  onNotify,
}: PromptOutputPanelProps) {
  const copy = I18N[lang];
  const labels = PANEL_LABELS[lang];
  const [copiedKey, setCopiedKey] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [showGuardDetails, setShowGuardDetails] = useState(false);

  const positiveText = (compiled.positiveOnly || outputText || "").trim();
  const negativeText = (compiled.negativeOnly || "").trim();
  const audioText = (compiled.audioBlock || "")
    .replace(/^AUDIO & SOUND DESIGN:\s*/i, "")
    .trim();
  const fullPromptText = (outputText || compiled.fullPrompt || "").trim();

  const handleCopy = async (text: string, key: string) => {
    if (!text.trim()) return;
    try {
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);
      setCopiedKey(key);
      setTimeout(() => setCopiedKey((prev) => (prev === key ? null : prev)), 1500);
    } catch {
      onNotify(labels.copyError, "err");
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4 rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 bg-white p-5 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900/70">
      {/* Top Engine Summary + Full Prompt Copy */}
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 border-b border-zinc-200 pb-3.5 dark:border-zinc-800">
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 text-xs text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400">
          <span className="text-sm font-semibold text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100">
            {engineName}
          </span>
          <span aria-hidden="true">·</span>
          <span className="font-mono tabular-nums">
            {compiled.positiveWords} {labels.visualWords}
