          </span>
          <span aria-hidden="true">·</span>
          <span className="font-mono tabular-nums">
            {compiled.tokens} {labels.tokens}
          </span>
        </div>

        <button
          type="button"
          disabled={!fullPromptText}
          onClick={() => handleCopy(fullPromptText, "full")}
          className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-lg bg-zinc-900 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-white transition-colors hover:bg-zinc-800 disabled:opacity-40 dark:bg-zinc-100 dark:text-zinc-900 dark:hover:bg-zinc-200"
        >
          {copiedKey === "full" ? (
            <Check className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-emerald-400 dark:text-emerald-600" />
          ) : (
            <FileText className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
          )}
          <span>{copiedKey === "full" ? copy.genCopied : copy.studioCopyFull}</span>
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* Section 1: Positive Visual Prompt */}
      <section className="overflow-hidden rounded-lg border border-zinc-200 bg-zinc-50/70 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-950/70">
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 border-b border-zinc-200 bg-white/80 px-3.5 py-2.5 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900/80">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <Sparkles className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-amber-500" />
            <div>
              <h3 className="text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100">
                {labels.visualTitle}
              </h3>
              <p className="text-[11px] text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400">
                {labels.visualSubtitle}
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>

          <button
            type="button"
            disabled={!positiveText}
            onClick={() => handleCopy(positiveText, "positive")}
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-md bg-amber-500 px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-950 transition-colors hover:bg-amber-400 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-40"
          >
            {copiedKey === "positive" ? (
              <Check className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
            ) : (
              <Copy className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
            )}
            <span>
              {copiedKey === "positive" ? copy.genCopied : labels.copyPositive}
            </span>
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="p-3">
          <textarea
            dir="ltr"
            rows={9}
            value={outputText}
            onChange={(e) => onChangeOutput(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="Compiled prompt will appear here..."
            className="w-full resize-y rounded-md border border-zinc-200 bg-white p-3 text-left font-mono text-xs leading-relaxed text-zinc-800 focus:border-amber-500 focus:outline-none dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-200"
          />
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* Section 2: Negative Prompt */}
      <section className="overflow-hidden rounded-lg border border-zinc-200 bg-zinc-50/70 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-950/70">
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 border-b border-zinc-200 bg-white/80 px-3.5 py-2.5 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900/80">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <Ban className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-rose-500" />
            <div>
              <h3 className="text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100">
                {labels.negativeTitle}
              </h3>
              <p className="text-[11px] text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400">
                {labels.negativeSubtitle}
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>

          <button
            type="button"
            disabled={!negativeText}
            onClick={() => handleCopy(negativeText, "negative")}
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-md border border-zinc-200 bg-white px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-zinc-700 transition-colors hover:bg-zinc-100 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-40 dark:border-zinc-700 dark:bg-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-200 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800"
          >
            {copiedKey === "negative" ? (
              <Check className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-emerald-500" />
            ) : (
              <Copy className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
            )}
            <span>
              {copiedKey === "negative" ? copy.genCopied : labels.copyNegative}
            </span>
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="px-3.5 py-2.5">
          {negativeText ? (
            <p
              dir="ltr"
              className="whitespace-pre-wrap break-words text-left font-mono text-[11px] leading-relaxed text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300"
            >
              {negativeText}
            </p>
          ) : (
            <p className="text-xs text-zinc-400 dark:text-zinc-500">
              {labels.negativeEmpty}
            </p>
          )}
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* Section 3: Audio Description */}
      <section className="overflow-hidden rounded-lg border border-zinc-200 bg-zinc-50/70 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-950/70">
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 border-b border-zinc-200 bg-white/80 px-3.5 py-2.5 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900/80">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <Volume2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-sky-500" />
            <div>
              <h3 className="text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100">
                {labels.audioTitle}
              </h3>
              <p className="text-[11px] text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400">
                {labels.audioSubtitle}
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>

          <button
            type="button"
            disabled={!audioText}
            onClick={() => handleCopy(audioText, "audio")}
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-md border border-zinc-200 bg-white px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-zinc-700 transition-colors hover:bg-zinc-100 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-40 dark:border-zinc-700 dark:bg-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-200 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800"
          >
            {copiedKey === "audio" ? (
              <Check className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-emerald-500" />
            ) : (
              <Copy className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
            )}
            <span>
              {copiedKey === "audio" ? copy.genCopied : labels.copyAudio}
            </span>
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="px-3.5 py-2.5">
          {audioText ? (
            <p
              dir="ltr"
              className="whitespace-pre-wrap break-words text-left font-mono text-[11px] leading-relaxed text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300"
            >
              {audioText}
            </p>
          ) : (
            <p className="text-xs text-zinc-400 dark:text-zinc-500">
              {labels.audioEmpty}
            </p>
          )}
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* Section 4: Calibration, Conversion & Export */}
      <div className="space-y-3 border-t border-zinc-200 pt-3.5 dark:border-zinc-800">
        <div className="text-[11px] font-medium text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400">
          {labels.toolsTitle}
        </div>

        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onUnslop}
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-md border border-zinc-200 px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs hover:bg-zinc-100 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800"
          >
            <Trash2 className="h-3 w-3" />
            <span>{copy.studioUnslop}</span>
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            disabled={fitting}
            onClick={onFitLength}
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-md border border-zinc-200 px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs hover:bg-zinc-100 disabled:opacity-50 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800"
          >
            {fitting && <Loader2 className="h-3 w-3 animate-spin" />}
            <span>{copy.studioFitLength}</span>
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            disabled={converting !== null}
            onClick={() => onConvertFormat("hailuo02")}
            className="rounded-md border border-zinc-200 px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs hover:bg-zinc-100 disabled:opacity-50 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800"
          >
            {converting === "hailuo02" ? "..." : "→ Hailuo 02"}
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            disabled={converting !== null}
            onClick={() => onConvertFormat("veo31")}
            className="rounded-md border border-zinc-200 px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs hover:bg-zinc-100 disabled:opacity-50 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800"
          >
            {converting === "veo31" ? "..." : "→ Veo 3.1"}
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 pt-1">
          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onExportComfy}
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-md border border-zinc-200 px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs hover:bg-zinc-100 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800"
            >
              <Download className="h-3 w-3" />
              <span>{copy.studioExportComfy}</span>
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onExportPython}
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-md border border-zinc-200 px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs hover:bg-zinc-100 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800"
            >
              <Code2 className="h-3 w-3" />
              <span>{copy.studioExportPython}</span>
            </button>
          </div>

          {compiled.guardBlock && (
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setShowGuardDetails((v) => !v)}
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-xs text-zinc-500 hover:text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-400 dark:hover:text-zinc-200"
            >
              <ShieldCheck className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-emerald-500" />
              <span>Scene Guard v2.0</span>
              {showGuardDetails ? (
                <ChevronUp className="h-3 w-3" />
              ) : (
                <ChevronDown className="h-3 w-3" />
              )}
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {showGuardDetails && compiled.guardBlock && (
        <div className="rounded-lg border border-zinc-200 bg-zinc-50 p-3 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-950">
          <div className="mb-1.5 flex items-center justify-between text-[11px] text-zinc-500">
            <span>{labels.guardTitle}</span>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => handleCopy(compiled.guardBlock || "", "guard")}
              className="text-amber-500 hover:underline"
            >
              {copiedKey === "guard" ? copy.genCopied : labels.copyRules}
            </button>
          </div>
          <pre
            dir="ltr"
            className="max-h-36 overflow-y-auto whitespace-pre-wrap text-left font-mono text-[11px] text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400"
          >
            {compiled.guardBlock}
          </pre>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}


####################################################################################################
FILE: src/components/SceneChainStudio.tsx
####################################################################################################

import { useMemo, useRef, useState } from "react";
import {
  AlertCircle,
  Check,
  ChevronDown,
  Copy,
  Download,
  FileJson,
  Film,
  Loader2,
  Lock,
  Trash2,
  Upload,
} from "lucide-react";
import type { I18N } from "../data/i18n";
import type { Language, SceneChainItem, SceneChainState } from "../types/prompt";

interface SceneChainStudioProps {
  copy: (typeof I18N)[Language];
  sceneChain: SceneChainState;
  addingNextScene: boolean;
  chainError?: string | null;
  onAddNextScene: (newIdea: string) => Promise<boolean>;
  onCopyChainScene: (scene: SceneChainItem) => void;
  onLoadChainScene: (scene: SceneChainItem) => void;
  onExportChain: () => void;
  onExportChainJson: () => void;
  onImportChainFile: (file: File) => void;
  onClearChain: () => void;
}

export function SceneChainStudio({
  copy,
  sceneChain,
  addingNextScene,
  chainError,
  onAddNextScene,
  onCopyChainScene,
  onLoadChainScene,
  onExportChain,
  onExportChainJson,
  onImportChainFile,
  onClearChain,
}: SceneChainStudioProps) {
  const fileInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement | null>(null);
  const [chainOpen, setChainOpen] = useState(true);
  const [nextSceneIdea, setNextSceneIdea] = useState("");
  const [copiedSceneId, setCopiedSceneId] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [copiedAll, setCopiedAll] = useState(false);
  const [confirmReset, setConfirmReset] = useState(false);
  const [localError, setLocalError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const totalScenes = sceneChain.scenes.length;
  const activeError = chainError || localError;
  const canAdd = useMemo(
    () => !addingNextScene && nextSceneIdea.trim().length > 0,
    [addingNextScene, nextSceneIdea]
  );

  const handleSubmitNext = async () => {
    if (!canAdd) return;
    setLocalError(null);
    try {
      const ok = await onAddNextScene(nextSceneIdea);
      if (ok) {
        setNextSceneIdea("");
      }
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      setLocalError(
        err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Failed to generate next scene."
      );
    }
  };

  const handleCopyAll = async () => {
    if (totalScenes === 0) return;
    const text = sceneChain.scenes
      .map(
        (s) =>
          `=== ${copy.chainSceneLabel} ${s.order} — ${s.idea} ===\n${s.prompt}${
            s.continuityState
              ? `\n\nCONTINUITY HANDOFF FOR NEXT SCENE:\n${s.continuityState}`
              : ""
          }`
      )
      .join("\n\n");
    try {
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);
    } catch {
      const ta = document.createElement("textarea");
      ta.value = text;
      ta.style.position = "fixed";
      ta.style.opacity = "0";
      document.body.appendChild(ta);
      ta.select();
      document.execCommand("copy");
      ta.remove();
    }
    setCopiedAll(true);
    window.setTimeout(() => setCopiedAll(false), 1800);
  };

  return (
    <div className="rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 bg-white p-4 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900/70">
      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={() => setChainOpen((v) => !v)}
        aria-expanded={chainOpen}
        className="flex min-h-[40px] w-full items-center justify-between gap-2 rounded-lg border border-amber-500/30 bg-amber-500/10 px-3 py-2 text-start text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-900 transition-colors hover:border-amber-500 dark:text-amber-300"
      >
        <span className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <Film className="h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0 text-amber-500" />
          <span>
            {copy.chainSectionTitle}
            {totalScenes > 0 ? (
              <span className="ms-1.5 font-mono tabular-nums">({totalScenes})</span>
            ) : null}
          </span>
        </span>
        <ChevronDown
          className={`h-4 w-4 shrink-0 transition-transform ${
            chainOpen ? "rotate-180" : ""
          }`}
        />
      </button>

      {chainOpen && (
        <div className="mt-3 space-y-3">
          {/* Shared Locked Continuity Context */}
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 text-[11px] text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400">
            <span>{copy.chainHint}</span>
            {sceneChain.baseContext?.seed !== undefined && (
              <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 font-mono font-semibold tabular-nums text-amber-700 dark:text-amber-400">
                <Lock className="h-3 w-3 shrink-0" />
                <span>
                  {copy.chainSeedLocked}: {sceneChain.baseContext.seed}
                </span>
              </span>
            )}
          </div>

          {sceneChain.baseContext && (
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-1.5 rounded-lg border border-zinc-200 bg-zinc-50 p-2.5 text-[11px] text-zinc-600 sm:grid-cols-2 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-300">
              {sceneChain.baseContext.character && (
                <p className="truncate">
                  <strong className="text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100">
                    {copy.chainCharacterLbl}:
                  </strong>{" "}
                  {sceneChain.baseContext.character}
                </p>
              )}
              {sceneChain.baseContext.style && (
                <p className="truncate">
                  <strong className="text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100">
                    {copy.chainStyleLbl}:
                  </strong>{" "}
                  {sceneChain.baseContext.style}
                </p>
              )}
              {sceneChain.baseContext.environment && (
                <p className="truncate">
                  <strong className="text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100">
                    {copy.chainEnvLbl}:
                  </strong>{" "}
                  {sceneChain.baseContext.environment}
                </p>
              )}
              {sceneChain.baseContext.camera && (
                <p className="truncate">
                  <strong className="text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100">
                    {copy.chainCameraLbl}:
                  </strong>{" "}
                  {sceneChain.baseContext.camera}
                </p>
              )}
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Scenes List */}
          {totalScenes > 0 && (
            <div className="max-h-72 space-y-2 overflow-y-auto pe-0.5">
              {sceneChain.scenes.map((scene) => (
                <div
                  key={`${scene.id}-${scene.order}`}
                  className="rounded-lg border border-zinc-200 bg-zinc-50 p-2.5 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-950"
                >
                  <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
                    <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-amber-700 dark:text-amber-400">
                      <span className="font-mono tabular-nums">
                        {copy.chainSceneLabel} {scene.order}
                      </span>{" "}
                      —{" "}
                      <span className="font-medium text-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-200">
                        {scene.idea}
                      </span>
                    </span>
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => onLoadChainScene(scene)}
                        className="whitespace-nowrap rounded-md border border-zinc-200 bg-white px-2 py-1 text-[11px] font-medium text-zinc-700 transition-colors hover:border-amber-500 hover:text-amber-700 dark:border-zinc-700 dark:bg-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-200 dark:hover:text-amber-300"
                      >
                        {copy.chainLoadBtn}
                      </button>
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => {
                          onCopyChainScene(scene);
                          setCopiedSceneId(scene.id);
                          window.setTimeout(() => setCopiedSceneId(null), 1600);
                        }}
                        className="inline-flex whitespace-nowrap items-center gap-1 rounded-md border border-zinc-200 bg-white px-2 py-1 text-[11px] font-medium text-zinc-700 transition-colors hover:border-amber-500 hover:text-amber-700 dark:border-zinc-700 dark:bg-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-200 dark:hover:text-amber-300"
                      >
                        {copiedSceneId === scene.id ? (
                          <Check className="h-3 w-3 text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400" />
                        ) : (
                          <Copy className="h-3 w-3" />
                        )}
                        <span>{copy.chainCopyBtn}</span>
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <p
                    dir="ltr"
                    className="mt-1.5 whitespace-pre-wrap break-words font-mono text-[11.5px] leading-relaxed text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300"
                  >
                    {scene.prompt}
                  </p>
                  {scene.continuityState && (
                    <div className="mt-2 rounded border border-zinc-200 bg-white px-2.5 py-1.5 text-[11px] text-zinc-600 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-400">
                      <span className="font-semibold text-amber-700 dark:text-amber-400">
                        {copy.chainContinuityLabel}:{" "}
                      </span>
                      <span dir="ltr">{scene.continuityState}</span>
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Add Next Scene Input & Actions */}
          <div className="space-y-2 border-t border-zinc-200 pt-2.5 dark:border-zinc-800">
            {activeError && (
              <div
                role="alert"
                className="flex items-start gap-2 rounded-lg border border-red-200 bg-red-50 px-3 py-2 text-[11.5px] leading-relaxed text-red-700 dark:border-red-900/60 dark:bg-red-950/40 dark:text-red-300"
              >
                <AlertCircle className="mt-0.5 h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0" />
                <span className="min-w-0 flex-1 break-words">{activeError}</span>
              </div>
            )}

            <textarea
              rows={2}
              value={nextSceneIdea}
              onChange={(e) => setNextSceneIdea(e.target.value)}
              onKeyDown={(e) => {
                if (e.key === "Enter" && !e.shiftKey && canAdd) {
                  e.preventDefault();
                  void handleSubmitNext();
                }
              }}
              placeholder={copy.chainPlaceholder}
              className="w-full resize-y rounded-lg border border-zinc-200 bg-zinc-50 px-3 py-2 text-xs leading-relaxed text-zinc-900 placeholder:text-zinc-400 focus:border-amber-500 focus:outline-none dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-100"
            />

            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
              <input
                ref={fileInputRef}
                type="file"
                accept=".txt,.json,text/plain,application/json"
                className="hidden"
                onChange={(e) => {
                  const file = e.target.files?.[0];
                  if (file) {
                    onImportChainFile(file);
                    setChainOpen(true);
                  }
                  e.target.value = "";
                }}
              />

              <button
                type="button"
                disabled={!canAdd}
                onClick={() => void handleSubmitNext()}
                className="inline-flex min-h-[38px] flex-1 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 whitespace-nowrap rounded-lg bg-amber-500 px-3 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-950 transition-colors hover:bg-amber-400 disabled:opacity-50"
              >
                {addingNextScene && (
                  <Loader2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0 animate-spin" />
                )}
                <span>
                  {addingNextScene ? copy.chainAddingBtn : copy.chainAddBtn}
                </span>
              </button>

              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => fileInputRef.current?.click()}
                className="inline-flex min-h-[38px] items-center justify-center gap-1 whitespace-nowrap rounded-lg border border-zinc-200 bg-white px-2.5 py-2 text-[11.5px] font-medium text-zinc-700 transition-colors hover:border-amber-500 hover:text-amber-700 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-200"
              >
                <Upload className="h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0" />
                <span>{copy.chainImportBtn}</span>
              </button>

              {totalScenes > 0 && (
                <>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => void handleCopyAll()}
                    className="inline-flex min-h-[38px] items-center justify-center gap-1 whitespace-nowrap rounded-lg border border-zinc-200 bg-white px-2.5 py-2 text-[11.5px] font-medium text-zinc-700 transition-colors hover:border-amber-500 hover:text-amber-700 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-200"
                  >
                    {copiedAll ? (
                      <Check className="h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0 text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400" />
                    ) : (
                      <Copy className="h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0" />
                    )}
                    <span>{copy.chainCopyAllBtn}</span>
                  </button>

                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={onExportChain}
                    className="inline-flex min-h-[38px] items-center justify-center gap-1 whitespace-nowrap rounded-lg border border-zinc-200 bg-white px-2.5 py-2 text-[11.5px] font-medium text-zinc-700 transition-colors hover:border-amber-500 hover:text-amber-700 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-200"
                  >
                    <Download className="h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0" />
                    <span>{copy.chainExportBtn}</span>
                  </button>

                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={onExportChainJson}
                    className="inline-flex min-h-[38px] items-center justify-center gap-1 whitespace-nowrap rounded-lg border border-zinc-200 bg-white px-2.5 py-2 text-[11.5px] font-medium text-zinc-700 transition-colors hover:border-amber-500 hover:text-amber-700 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-200"
                  >
                    <FileJson className="h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0" />
                    <span>{copy.chainExportJsonBtn}</span>
                  </button>

                  {totalScenes > 1 && (
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => {
                        if (!confirmReset) {
                          setConfirmReset(true);
                          window.setTimeout(() => setConfirmReset(false), 3000);
                          return;
                        }
                        setConfirmReset(false);
                        onClearChain();
                      }}
                      className={`inline-flex min-h-[38px] items-center justify-center gap-1 whitespace-nowrap rounded-lg border px-2.5 py-2 text-[11.5px] font-medium transition-colors ${
                        confirmReset
                          ? "border-red-500 bg-red-50 text-red-700 dark:border-red-500/70 dark:bg-red-950/40 dark:text-red-300"
                          : "border-zinc-200 bg-white text-zinc-600 hover:border-red-400 hover:text-red-600 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-300"
                      }`}
                    >
                      <Trash2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0" />
                      <span>
                        {confirmReset ? copy.chainConfirmReset : copy.chainClearBtn}
                      </span>
                    </button>
                  )}
                </>
              )}
            </div>

            {totalScenes > 0 && (
              <p className="text-center font-mono text-[11px] tabular-nums text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400">
                {copy.chainTotalScenes}: {totalScenes}
              </p>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
