import { useState } from "react";
import {
  Camera,
  Check,
  Clapperboard,
  Dices,
  Film,
  ImagePlus,
  Loader2,
  Lock,
  Play,
  Plus,
  ShieldCheck,
  Sparkles,
  Unlock,
  Wand2,
} from "lucide-react";
import {
  AUDIO_DESIGN_TAGS,
  CAMERA_LENSES,
  CAMERA_MOVEMENTS,
  CATEGORIES,
  ENGINE_ORDER,
  ENGINES,
  HAILUO_OFFICIAL_COMMANDS,
  LIGHTING_PALETTES,
  PHYSICS_HINTS,
  SMART_KEYWORDS,
  STYLE_OPTIONS,
  WAN_VARIANTS,
} from "../data/engines";
import { I18N } from "../data/i18n";
import type {
  AudioTagId,
  EngineId,
  GenerationMode,
  GoogleVideoLanguage,
  GoogleVideoModel,
  Language,
  PhysicsHintId,
  ReferenceImageState,
  StudioFormState,
  WanVariant,
} from "../types/prompt";
import { GOOGLE_VEO_LANGUAGES } from "../utils/googleVeoPrompt";
import { analyzePromptQuality } from "../utils/promptAnalyzer";
import {
  buildComfyExportPayload,
  buildDefaultShots,
  buildPythonPipelineScript,
  buildSmartNegative,
  compileEnginePrompt,
} from "../utils/promptEngine";
import { SmartSceneGuard } from "../utils/smartSceneGuard";
import { cleanPromptWithUnslop } from "../utils/unslopUtils";
import { PromptOutputPanel } from "./PromptOutputPanel";

interface ProStudioViewProps {
  lang: Language;
  form: StudioFormState;
  setForm: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<StudioFormState>>;
  outputText: string;
  setOutputText: (val: string) => void;
  onSaveEntry: (compiledFull: string, positiveOnly: string, negativeOnly: string) => void;
  onNotify: (msg: string, type: "ok" | "err") => void;
}

function readFileAsReference(file: File): Promise<ReferenceImageState> {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onload = () => {
      const dataUrl = String(reader.result || "");
      const comma = dataUrl.indexOf(",");
      const base64 = comma >= 0 ? dataUrl.slice(comma + 1) : dataUrl;
      resolve({
        dataUrl,
        base64,
        mimeType: file.type || "image/jpeg",
        name: file.name,
      });
    };
    reader.onerror = () => reject(new Error("Could not read image file."));
    reader.readAsDataURL(file);
  });
}

export function ProStudioView({
  lang,
  form,
  setForm,
  outputText,
  setOutputText,
  onSaveEntry,
  onNotify,
}: ProStudioViewProps) {
  const copy = I18N[lang];
  const engineSpec = ENGINES[form.engine];
  const wanSpec = WAN_VARIANTS[form.wanVariant];

  const activeModes =
    form.engine === "wan22" ? wanSpec.modes : engineSpec.modes;
  const activeAspects =
    form.engine === "wan22" ? wanSpec.aspectOptions : engineSpec.aspectOptions;
  const activeDurations =
    form.engine === "wan22" ? wanSpec.durationOptions : engineSpec.durationOptions;

  const [directorBrief, setDirectorBrief] = useState("");
  const [directing, setDirecting] = useState(false);
  const [enhancing, setEnhancing] = useState(false);
  const [fitting, setFitting] = useState(false);
  const [converting, setConverting] = useState<"hailuo02" | "veo31" | null>(null);
  const [analyzingImg, setAnalyzingImg] = useState(false);
  const [nextSceneIdea, setNextSceneIdea] = useState("");
  const [generatingNextScene, setGeneratingNextScene] = useState(false);
  const [refImage, setRefImage] = useState<ReferenceImageState | null>(null);
  const [endImage, setEndImage] = useState<ReferenceImageState | null>(null);
  const [minimaxLaunch, setMinimaxLaunch] = useState<{
    status: string;
    launchUrl?: string;
    videoUrl?: string | null;
  } | null>(null);

  const compiledPreview = compileEnginePrompt(form);
  const displayedOutput = outputText || compiledPreview.fullPrompt;
  const qualityReport = analyzePromptQuality(form, displayedOutput);

  const updateField = <K extends keyof StudioFormState>(
    key: K,
    value: StudioFormState[K]
  ) => {
    setForm((prev) => ({ ...prev, [key]: value }));
  };

  const handleEngineSelect = (nextEngine: EngineId) => {
    const nextSpec = ENGINES[nextEngine];
    setForm((prev) => {
      const nextModes =
        nextEngine === "wan22" ? WAN_VARIANTS[prev.wanVariant].modes : nextSpec.modes;
      const nextAspects =
        nextEngine === "wan22"
          ? WAN_VARIANTS[prev.wanVariant].aspectOptions
          : nextSpec.aspectOptions;
      const nextDurations =
        nextEngine === "wan22"
          ? WAN_VARIANTS[prev.wanVariant].durationOptions
          : nextSpec.durationOptions;
      const mode = nextModes.includes(prev.mode) ? prev.mode : nextSpec.defaultMode;
      const aspect = nextAspects.includes(prev.aspect) ? prev.aspect : nextAspects[0];
      const duration =
        nextSpec.kind === "video"
          ? nextDurations.some((d) => d.value === prev.duration)
            ? prev.duration
            : nextSpec.defaultDuration
          : 0;
      return {
        ...prev,
        engine: nextEngine,
        mode,
        aspect,
        duration,
        cfgScale: nextSpec.defaultCfg,
        steps: nextSpec.defaultSteps,
        negative: prev.negativeEdited
          ? prev.negative
          : buildSmartNegative(nextEngine, mode, prev.category),
      };
    });
  };

  const handleVariantChange = (nextVariant: WanVariant) => {
    const vSpec = WAN_VARIANTS[nextVariant];
    setForm((prev) => ({
      ...prev,
      wanVariant: nextVariant,
      mode: vSpec.modes.includes(prev.mode) ? prev.mode : "t2v",
      aspect: vSpec.aspectOptions.includes(prev.aspect)
        ? prev.aspect
        : vSpec.aspectOptions[0],
      duration: vSpec.durationOptions.some((d) => d.value === prev.duration)
        ? prev.duration
        : vSpec.durationOptions[0].value,
      cfgScale: vSpec.defaultCfg,
      steps: vSpec.defaultSteps,
    }));
  };

  const handleCompileAndSave = () => {
    if (!form.subject.trim() && !form.action.trim() && !form.environment.trim()) {
      onNotify(copy.errSubject, "err");
      return;
    }
    const nextSeed = form.seedLocked
      ? form.seed
      : Math.floor(100_000 + Math.random() * 8_999_999_999);
    const updatedForm = { ...form, seed: nextSeed };
    if (!form.seedLocked) {
      setForm(updatedForm);
    }
    const compiled = compileEnginePrompt(updatedForm);
    setOutputText(compiled.fullPrompt);
    onSaveEntry(compiled.fullPrompt, compiled.positiveOnly, compiled.negativeOnly);
  };

  const handleDirector = async () => {
    if (!directorBrief.trim()) return;
    setDirecting(true);
    try {
      const res = await fetch("/api/ai/director", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          brief: directorBrief.trim(),
          engine: form.engine,
          wanVariant: form.wanVariant,
        }),
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      if (!res.ok) throw new Error(data?.error || "AI Director failed.");

      const nextForm: StudioFormState = {
        ...form,
        category: data.category || form.category,
        duration:
          typeof data.duration === "number" && activeDurations.some((d) => d.value === data.duration)
            ? data.duration
            : form.duration,
        subject: data.subject || form.subject,
        environment: data.environment || form.environment,
        action: data.action || form.action,
        extra: data.extra || form.extra,
        cameraMovement: data.cameraMovement || form.cameraMovement,
        cameraLens: data.lens || form.cameraLens,
        camera: [data.cameraMovement, data.lens].filter(Boolean).join(", "),
        light: data.lightingMood || form.light,
        style: data.style || form.style,
        physicsHints: Array.isArray(data.physicsHints) ? data.physicsHints : form.physicsHints,
        audioTags: Array.isArray(data.audioTags) ? data.audioTags : form.audioTags,
        audioDescription: data.audioDesign || form.audioDescription,
        veoDialogue: data.dialogue || form.veoDialogue,
        veoSoundEffects: data.soundEffects || form.veoSoundEffects,
        minimaxCameraCommands: Array.isArray(data.minimaxCameraCommands)
          ? data.minimaxCameraCommands
          : form.minimaxCameraCommands,
        shots: Array.isArray(data.shots)
          ? data.shots.map((s: { camera?: string; description?: string }, i: number) => ({
              id: i + 1,
              camera: s.camera || "",
              description: s.description || "",
            }))
          : form.shots,
        negative: data.negative ?? form.negative,
      };
      setForm(nextForm);
      const compiled = compileEnginePrompt(nextForm);
      setOutputText(compiled.fullPrompt);
      onNotify("AI Director populated the production setup.", "ok");
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      onNotify(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "AI Director failed.", "err");
    } finally {
      setDirecting(false);
    }
  };

  const handleEnhance = async () => {
    if (!form.subject.trim() && !form.action.trim()) {
      onNotify(copy.errSubject, "err");
      return;
    }
    setEnhancing(true);
    try {
      const res = await fetch("/api/ai/enhance", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          engine: form.engine,
          mode: form.mode,
          category: form.category,
          subject: form.subject,
          environment: form.environment,
          action: form.action,
          style: form.style,
          light: form.light,
          camera: form.camera || [form.cameraMovement, form.cameraLens].filter(Boolean).join(", "),
          extra: form.extra,
        }),
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      if (!res.ok) throw new Error(data?.error || "Enhance failed.");

      const nextForm: StudioFormState = {
        ...form,
        subject: data.subject || form.subject,
        environment: data.environment ?? form.environment,
        action: data.action || form.action,
        extra: data.extra || form.extra,
        cameraMovement: data.cameraMovement || form.cameraMovement,
        cameraLens: data.lens || form.cameraLens,
        camera: [data.cameraMovement, data.lens].filter(Boolean).join(", "),
        light: data.lightingMood || form.light,
        style: data.style || form.style,
        veoDialogue: data.dialogue || form.veoDialogue,
        veoSoundEffects: data.soundEffects || form.veoSoundEffects,
        negative: data.negative ?? form.negative,
      };
      setForm(nextForm);
      const compiled = compileEnginePrompt(nextForm);
      setOutputText(compiled.fullPrompt);
      onNotify("Prompt enhanced with engine-specific physics and cinematography.", "ok");
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      onNotify(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Enhance failed.", "err");
    } finally {
      setEnhancing(false);
    }
  };

  const handleFitLength = async () => {
    if (!displayedOutput.trim()) return;
    setFitting(true);
    try {
      const res = await fetch("/api/ai/fit-length", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ prompt: compiledPreview.positiveOnly || displayedOutput }),
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      if (!res.ok) throw new Error(data?.error || "Length calibration failed.");
      if (data.prompt) {
        setOutputText(data.prompt);
        onNotify("Calibrated to 90–110 words.", "ok");
      }
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      onNotify(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Length calibration failed.", "err");
    } finally {
      setFitting(false);
    }
  };

  const handleConvertFormat = async (target: "hailuo02" | "veo31") => {
    if (!displayedOutput.trim()) return;
    setConverting(target);
    try {
      const res = await fetch("/api/ai/convert-format", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          prompt: compiledPreview.positiveOnly || displayedOutput,
          target,
          isSinger: form.mode === "s2v",
        }),
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      if (!res.ok) throw new Error(data?.error || "Conversion failed.");
      if (data.prompt) {
        setOutputText(data.prompt);
        onNotify(`Converted to ${target === "veo31" ? "Google Veo 3.1" : "MiniMax Hailuo 02"}.`, "ok");
      }
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      onNotify(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Conversion failed.", "err");
    } finally {
      setConverting(null);
    }
  };

  const handleAnalyzeImage = async (file: File, target: "first" | "last") => {
    setAnalyzingImg(true);
    try {
      const ref = await readFileAsReference(file);
      if (target === "first") setRefImage(ref);
      else setEndImage(ref);

      if (target === "first") {
        const res = await fetch("/api/ai/analyze-image", {
          method: "POST",
          headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
          body: JSON.stringify({
            imageBase64: ref.base64,
            mimeType: ref.mimeType,
            engine: form.engine,
          }),
        });
        const data = await res.json();
        if (!res.ok) throw new Error(data?.error || "Image analysis failed.");
        setForm((prev) => ({
          ...prev,
          subject: data.subject || prev.subject,
          environment: data.environment || prev.environment,
          action: data.action || prev.action,
          extra: data.extra || prev.extra,
        }));
        onNotify("Reference frame analyzed and fields populated.", "ok");
      }
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      onNotify(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Image analysis failed.", "err");
    } finally {
      setAnalyzingImg(false);
    }
  };

  const handleAnalyzeTransition = async () => {
    if (!refImage || !endImage) return;
    setAnalyzingImg(true);
    try {
      const res = await fetch("/api/ai/analyze-transition", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          firstImageBase64: refImage.base64,
          firstMimeType: refImage.mimeType,
          lastImageBase64: endImage.base64,
          lastMimeType: endImage.mimeType,
        }),
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      if (!res.ok) throw new Error(data?.error || "Transition analysis failed.");
      setForm((prev) => ({
        ...prev,
        subject: data.subject || prev.subject,
        action: data.action || prev.action,
        extra: data.extra || prev.extra,
      }));
      onNotify("First-to-last frame transition engineered.", "ok");
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      onNotify(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Transition analysis failed.", "err");
    } finally {
      setAnalyzingImg(false);
    }
  };

  const handleNextScene = async () => {
    if (!nextSceneIdea.trim()) return;
    setGeneratingNextScene(true);
    try {
      const res = await fetch("/api/ai/next-scene", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          engine: form.engine,
          wanVariant: form.wanVariant,
          mode: form.mode,
          sceneOrder: 2,
          baseContext: {
            character: form.subject,
            style: form.style,
            environment: form.environment,
            camera: form.camera,
            lighting: form.light,
          },
          previousPrompt: displayedOutput,
          previousIdea: form.subject,
          newIdea: nextSceneIdea.trim(),
          sceneGuardEnabled: form.smartSceneGuard,
          sceneGuardProvider: SmartSceneGuard.resolveProvider(form.engine),
          sceneGuardRules: SmartSceneGuard.getGuardMetadataBlock(form.engine),
        }),
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      if (!res.ok) throw new Error(data?.error || "Next scene failed.");
      const nextForm: StudioFormState = {
        ...form,
        subject: data.subject || form.subject,
        environment: data.environment || form.environment,
        action: data.action || form.action,
        extra: data.extra || form.extra,
        cameraMovement: data.cameraMovement || form.cameraMovement,
        cameraLens: data.lens || form.cameraLens,
        light: data.lightingMood || form.light,
        style: data.style || form.style,
      };
      setForm(nextForm);
      const compiled = compileEnginePrompt(nextForm);
      setOutputText(compiled.fullPrompt);
      onSaveEntry(compiled.fullPrompt, compiled.positiveOnly, compiled.negativeOnly);
      setNextSceneIdea("");
      onNotify("Next continuous scene generated.", "ok");
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      onNotify(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Next scene failed.", "err");
    } finally {
      setGeneratingNextScene(false);
    }
  };

  const handleMiniMaxSubmit = async () => {
    try {
      setMinimaxLaunch({ status: "Submitting to MiniMax Hailuo 02..." });
      const res = await fetch("/api/minimax/generate", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          prompt: compiledPreview.positiveOnly || displayedOutput,
          duration: form.duration === 10 ? 10 : 6,
          resolution: form.minimaxResolution,
          promptOptimizer: form.minimaxPromptOptimizer,
          firstFrameDataUrl: refImage?.dataUrl || "",
          lastFrameDataUrl: endImage?.dataUrl || "",
        }),
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      if (!res.ok) throw new Error(data?.error || "MiniMax launch failed.");
      if (data.mode === "handoff") {
        await navigator.clipboard.writeText(compiledPreview.positiveOnly || displayedOutput).catch(() => {});
        setMinimaxLaunch({
          status: "Prompt copied to clipboard. Ready to paste in Hailuo AI.",
          launchUrl: data.launchUrl,
        });
      } else {
        setMinimaxLaunch({
          status: `Task queued (${data.taskId}) — check MiniMax status.`,
        });
      }
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      setMinimaxLaunch({
        status: err instanceof Error ? err.message : "MiniMax request failed.",
      });
    }
  };

  const handleDownloadFile = (filename: string, content: string, mime: string) => {
    const blob = new Blob([content], { type: mime });
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const a = document.createElement("a");
    a.href = url;
    a.download = filename;
    a.click();
    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
  };

  const togglePhysics = (id: PhysicsHintId) => {
    setForm((prev) => ({
      ...prev,
      physicsHints: prev.physicsHints.includes(id)
        ? prev.physicsHints.filter((x) => x !== id)
        : [...prev.physicsHints, id],
    }));
  };

  const toggleAudioTag = (id: AudioTagId) => {
    setForm((prev) => ({
      ...prev,
      audioTags: prev.audioTags.includes(id)
        ? prev.audioTags.filter((x) => x !== id)
        : [...prev.audioTags, id],
    }));
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      {/* AI Commercial Director Bar */}
      <section className="rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 bg-white p-4 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900/70">
        <div className="mb-2 flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-medium text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300">
          <Clapperboard className="h-4 w-4 text-amber-500" />
          <span>{copy.studioDirectorTitle}</span>
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-2 sm:flex-row">
          <input
            type="text"
            value={directorBrief}
            onChange={(e) => setDirectorBrief(e.target.value)}
            placeholder={copy.studioDirectorPlaceholder}
            className="flex-1 rounded-lg border border-zinc-200 bg-zinc-50 px-3.5 py-2 text-sm text-zinc-900 focus:border-amber-500 focus:outline-none dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-100"
          />
          <button
            type="button"
            disabled={directing || !directorBrief.trim()}
            onClick={handleDirector}
            className="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 whitespace-nowrap rounded-lg bg-amber-500 px-4 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-950 transition-colors hover:bg-amber-400 disabled:opacity-50"
          >
            {directing ? (
              <Loader2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5 animate-spin" />
            ) : (
              <Wand2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
            )}
            <span>{copy.studioDirectorBtn}</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* Engine Selector */}
      <section className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
        {ENGINE_ORDER.map((id) => {
          const spec = ENGINES[id];
          const active = form.engine === id;
          return (
            <button
              key={id}
              type="button"
              onClick={() => handleEngineSelect(id)}
              className={`whitespace-nowrap rounded-lg border px-3.5 py-2 text-xs font-medium transition-colors ${
                active
                  ? "border-amber-500 bg-amber-500/10 text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-50"
                  : "border-zinc-200 bg-white text-zinc-600 hover:border-zinc-300 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-400 dark:hover:border-zinc-700"
              }`}
            >
              {spec.name}
            </button>
          );
        })}
      </section>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-6 lg:grid-cols-12">
        {/* Left Workbench Controls */}
        <div className="space-y-5 lg:col-span-7">
          {/* Core Parameters */}
          <div className="space-y-4 rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 bg-white p-5 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900/70">
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-3">
              <div>
                <label className="mb-1 block text-xs font-medium text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400">
                  Mode
                </label>
                <select
                  value={form.mode}
                  onChange={(e) => updateField("mode", e.target.value as GenerationMode)}
                  className="w-full rounded-lg border border-zinc-200 bg-zinc-50 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-zinc-900 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-100"
                >
                  {activeModes.map((m) => (
                    <option key={m} value={m}>
                      {m.toUpperCase()}
                    </option>
                  ))}
                </select>
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="mb-1 block text-xs font-medium text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400">
                  Aspect Ratio
                </label>
                <select
                  value={form.aspect}
                  onChange={(e) => updateField("aspect", e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full rounded-lg border border-zinc-200 bg-zinc-50 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-zinc-900 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-100"
                >
                  {activeAspects.map((a) => (
                    <option key={a} value={a}>
                      {a}
                    </option>
                  ))}
                </select>
              </div>

              {activeDurations.length > 0 && (
                <div>
                  <label className="mb-1 block text-xs font-medium text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400">
                    Duration
                  </label>
                  <select
                    value={form.duration}
                    onChange={(e) => updateField("duration", Number(e.target.value))}
                    className="w-full rounded-lg border border-zinc-200 bg-zinc-50 px-3 py-2 font-mono text-xs text-zinc-900 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-100"
                  >
                    {activeDurations.map((d) => (
                      <option key={d.value} value={d.value}>
                        {d.label}
                      </option>
                    ))}
                  </select>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>

            {form.engine === "wan22" && (
              <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 pt-1">
                {(["a14b", "ti2v_5b"] as WanVariant[]).map((v) => (
                  <button
                    key={v}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => handleVariantChange(v)}
                    className={`rounded-md border px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium ${
                      form.wanVariant === v
                        ? "border-amber-500 bg-amber-500/10 text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100"
                        : "border-zinc-200 text-zinc-500 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-400"
                    }`}
                  >
                    {WAN_VARIANTS[v].label}
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
            )}

            {/* Format & SmartSceneGuard */}
            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 border-t border-zinc-200 pt-3 dark:border-zinc-800">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                {(["official", "structured"] as const).map((fmt) => (
                  <button
                    key={fmt}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => updateField("promptFormat", fmt)}
                    className={`rounded-md border px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-medium capitalize ${
                      form.promptFormat === fmt
                        ? "border-amber-500 bg-amber-500/10 text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100"
                        : "border-zinc-200 text-zinc-500 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-400"
                    }`}
                  >
                    {fmt}
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>

              {engineSpec.kind === "video" && (
                <label className="flex cursor-pointer items-center gap-2 text-xs text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-300">
                  <input
                    type="checkbox"
                    checked={form.smartSceneGuard}
                    onChange={(e) => updateField("smartSceneGuard", e.target.checked)}
                    className="h-3.5 w-3.5 accent-amber-500"
                  />
                  <ShieldCheck className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-emerald-500" />
                  <span>Smart Scene Guard v2.0</span>
                </label>
              )}
            </div>

            {/* Category */}
            <div>
              <label className="mb-1 block text-xs font-medium text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400">
                Category
              </label>
              <select
                value={form.category}
                onChange={(e) => updateField("category", e.target.value)}
                className="w-full rounded-lg border border-zinc-200 bg-zinc-50 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-zinc-900 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-100"
              >
                <option value="">General Cinema</option>
                {CATEGORIES.map((c) => (
                  <option key={c} value={c}>
                    {c}
                  </option>
                ))}
              </select>
            </div>

            {/* Subject & Environment */}
            <div className="space-y-3">
              <div>
                <label className="mb-1 block text-xs font-medium text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300">
                  Subject & Materials
                </label>
                <textarea
                  rows={2}
                  value={form.subject}
                  onChange={(e) => updateField("subject", e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="Describe the hero subject, wardrobe, textures, or product..."
                  className="w-full rounded-lg border border-zinc-200 bg-zinc-50 px-3 py-2 text-sm text-zinc-900 focus:border-amber-500 focus:outline-none dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-100"
                />
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="mb-1 block text-xs font-medium text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300">
                  Environment & Spatial Depth
                </label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={form.environment}
                  onChange={(e) => updateField("environment", e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="Location, weather, background architecture..."
                  className="w-full rounded-lg border border-zinc-200 bg-zinc-50 px-3 py-2 text-sm text-zinc-900 focus:border-amber-500 focus:outline-none dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-100"
                />
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="mb-1 block text-xs font-medium text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300">
                  Primary Motion / Pose
                </label>
                <textarea
                  rows={2}
                  value={form.action}
                  onChange={(e) => updateField("action", e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="Continuous physical action, wind/fluid reaction, gesture..."
                  className="w-full rounded-lg border border-zinc-200 bg-zinc-50 px-3 py-2 text-sm text-zinc-900 focus:border-amber-500 focus:outline-none dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-100"
                />
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Reference Image Vision Upload */}
          {(form.mode === "i2v" ||
            form.mode === "flf2v" ||
            form.mode === "img2img" ||
            form.mode === "s2v") && (
            <div className="space-y-3 rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 bg-white p-5 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900/70">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                <span className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100">
                  <ImagePlus className="h-4 w-4 text-amber-500" />
                  Reference Frame Vision Analysis
                </span>
                {analyzingImg && <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin text-amber-500" />}
              </div>

              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
                <label className="flex cursor-pointer flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-lg border border-dashed border-zinc-300 p-4 text-center text-xs text-zinc-500 hover:border-amber-500 dark:border-zinc-700">
                  <input
                    type="file"
                    accept="image/*"
                    className="hidden"
                    onChange={(e) => {
                      const f = e.target.files?.[0];
                      if (f) void handleAnalyzeImage(f, "first");
                    }}
                  />
                  {refImage ? (
                    <img
                      src={refImage.dataUrl}
                      alt={refImage.name}
                      className="h-24 w-full rounded object-cover"
                    />
                  ) : (
                    <span>Upload First / Reference Frame</span>
                  )}
                </label>

                {form.mode === "flf2v" && (
                  <label className="flex cursor-pointer flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-lg border border-dashed border-zinc-300 p-4 text-center text-xs text-zinc-500 hover:border-amber-500 dark:border-zinc-700">
                    <input
                      type="file"
                      accept="image/*"
                      className="hidden"
                      onChange={(e) => {
                        const f = e.target.files?.[0];
                        if (f) void handleAnalyzeImage(f, "last");
                      }}
                    />
                    {endImage ? (
                      <img
                        src={endImage.dataUrl}
                        alt={endImage.name}
                        className="h-24 w-full rounded object-cover"
                      />
                    ) : (
                      <span>Upload Last Frame (FLF2V)</span>
                    )}
                  </label>
                )}
              </div>

              {form.mode === "flf2v" && refImage && endImage && (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={handleAnalyzeTransition}
                  className="rounded-lg bg-zinc-900 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-white dark:bg-zinc-100 dark:text-zinc-900"
                >
                  Analyze Transition Between Frames
                </button>
              )}
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Camera, Lighting, Style & Physics */}
          <div className="space-y-4 rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 bg-white p-5 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900/70">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100">
              <Camera className="h-4 w-4 text-amber-500" />
              <span>Cinematography, Lighting & Style</span>
            </div>

            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
              <div>
                <label className="mb-1 block text-xs text-zinc-500">Camera Movement</label>
                <select
                  value={form.cameraMovement}
                  onChange={(e) => updateField("cameraMovement", e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full rounded-lg border border-zinc-200 bg-zinc-50 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-zinc-900 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-100"
                >
                  <option value="">Auto</option>
                  {CAMERA_MOVEMENTS.map((m) => (
                    <option key={m.label} value={m.label}>
                      {m.label}
                    </option>
                  ))}
                </select>
              </div>
              <div>
                <label className="mb-1 block text-xs text-zinc-500">Lens</label>
                <select
                  value={form.cameraLens}
                  onChange={(e) => updateField("cameraLens", e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full rounded-lg border border-zinc-200 bg-zinc-50 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-zinc-900 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-100"
                >
                  <option value="">Auto</option>
                  {CAMERA_LENSES.map((l) => (
                    <option key={l.label} value={l.label}>
                      {l.label}
                    </option>
                  ))}
                </select>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
              <div>
                <label className="mb-1 block text-xs text-zinc-500">Lighting Palette</label>
                <select
                  value={form.light}
                  onChange={(e) => updateField("light", e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full rounded-lg border border-zinc-200 bg-zinc-50 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-zinc-900 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-100"
                >
                  <option value="">Select lighting...</option>
                  {LIGHTING_PALETTES.map((l) => (
                    <option key={l} value={l}>
                      {l}
                    </option>
                  ))}
                </select>
              </div>
              <div>
                <label className="mb-1 block text-xs text-zinc-500">Visual Grade / Film Stock</label>
                <select
                  value={form.style}
                  onChange={(e) => updateField("style", e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full rounded-lg border border-zinc-200 bg-zinc-50 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-zinc-900 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-100"
                >
                  <option value="">Select grade...</option>
                  {STYLE_OPTIONS.map((s) => (
                    <option key={s} value={s}>
                      {s}
                    </option>
                  ))}
                </select>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Micro-Physics */}
            <div className="space-y-1.5">
              <span className="block text-xs text-zinc-500">Physics & Atmospheric Hints</span>
              <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
                {PHYSICS_HINTS.map((p) => {
                  const active = form.physicsHints.includes(p.id);
                  return (
                    <button
                      key={p.id}
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => togglePhysics(p.id)}
                      className={`rounded-md border px-2.5 py-1 text-xs transition-colors ${
                        active
                          ? "border-amber-500 bg-amber-500/10 text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100"
                          : "border-zinc-200 text-zinc-500 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-400"
                      }`}
                    >
                      {p.label}
                    </button>
                  );
                })}
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Extra Micro-Details + Smart Keywords */}
            <div>
              <label className="mb-1 block text-xs text-zinc-500">
                Micro-Details & Lens Optics
              </label>
              <input
                type="text"
                value={form.extra}
                onChange={(e) => updateField("extra", e.target.value)}
                placeholder="Dust motes, cloth texture, glass refraction..."
                className="w-full rounded-lg border border-zinc-200 bg-zinc-50 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-zinc-900 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-100"
              />
              <div className="mt-2 flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
                {SMART_KEYWORDS.map((kw) => (
                  <button
                    key={kw}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() =>
                      updateField(
                        "extra",
                        form.extra.includes(kw)
                          ? form.extra
                          : [form.extra.trim(), kw].filter(Boolean).join(", ")
                      )
                    }
                    className="rounded border border-zinc-200 px-2 py-0.5 text-[11px] text-zinc-500 hover:border-amber-500 hover:text-zinc-900 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:hover:text-zinc-200"
                  >
                    + {kw}
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Engine-Specific: MiniMax Hailuo 02 */}
          {form.engine === "minimax" && (
            <div className="space-y-3 rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 bg-white p-5 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900/70">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100">
                  MiniMax Hailuo 02 Controls
                </span>
                <select
                  value={form.minimaxResolution}
                  onChange={(e) =>
                    updateField("minimaxResolution", e.target.value as "1080P" | "768P")
                  }
                  className="rounded border border-zinc-200 bg-zinc-50 px-2 py-1 font-mono text-xs dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-950"
                >
                  <option value="1080P">1080P</option>
                  <option value="768P">768P</option>
                </select>
              </div>
              <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
                {HAILUO_OFFICIAL_COMMANDS.map((cmd) => {
                  const active = form.minimaxCameraCommands.includes(cmd);
                  return (
                    <button
                      key={cmd}
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() =>
                        updateField(
                          "minimaxCameraCommands",
                          active
                            ? form.minimaxCameraCommands.filter((c) => c !== cmd)
                            : [...form.minimaxCameraCommands, cmd].slice(-2)
                        )
                      }
                      className={`rounded-md border px-2 py-1 text-xs ${
                        active
                          ? "border-amber-500 bg-amber-500/10 text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100"
                          : "border-zinc-200 text-zinc-500 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-400"
                      }`}
                    >
                      [{cmd}]
                    </button>
                  );
                })}
              </div>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handleMiniMaxSubmit}
                className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-lg bg-zinc-900 px-3.5 py-2 text-xs font-medium text-white dark:bg-zinc-100 dark:text-zinc-900"
              >
                <Play className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                <span>Launch / Send to MiniMax Hailuo 02</span>
              </button>
              {minimaxLaunch && (
                <div className="text-xs text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-300">
                  {minimaxLaunch.status}
                  {minimaxLaunch.launchUrl && (
                    <a
                      href={minimaxLaunch.launchUrl}
                      target="_blank"
                      rel="noreferrer"
                      className="ms-2 underline text-amber-500"
                    >
                      Open Hailuo AI
                    </a>
                  )}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Engine-Specific: Google Veo 3.1 */}
          {form.engine === "veo" && (
            <div className="space-y-3 rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 bg-white p-5 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900/70">
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-3">
                <select
                  value={form.veoModel}
                  onChange={(e) => updateField("veoModel", e.target.value as GoogleVideoModel)}
                  className="rounded-lg border border-zinc-200 bg-zinc-50 px-3 py-2 text-xs dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-950"
                >
                  <option value="veo-3.1">Veo 3.1</option>
                  <option value="veo-3.1-fast">Veo 3.1 Fast</option>
                </select>
                <select
                  value={form.veoResolution}
                  onChange={(e) =>
                    updateField("veoResolution", e.target.value as "720p" | "1080p")
                  }
                  className="rounded-lg border border-zinc-200 bg-zinc-50 px-3 py-2 text-xs dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-950"
                >
                  <option value="1080p">1080p</option>
                  <option value="720p">720p</option>
                </select>
                <select
                  value={form.veoLanguage}
                  onChange={(e) =>
                    updateField("veoLanguage", e.target.value as GoogleVideoLanguage)
                  }
                  className="rounded-lg border border-zinc-200 bg-zinc-50 px-3 py-2 text-xs dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-950"
                >
                  {GOOGLE_VEO_LANGUAGES.map((l) => (
                    <option key={l} value={l}>
                      {l}
                    </option>
                  ))}
                </select>
              </div>
              <input
                type="text"
                value={form.veoDialogue}
                onChange={(e) => updateField("veoDialogue", e.target.value)}
                placeholder="Spoken dialogue line (optional)..."
                className="w-full rounded-lg border border-zinc-200 bg-zinc-50 px-3 py-2 text-xs dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-950"
              />
              <input
                type="text"
                value={form.veoSoundEffects}
                onChange={(e) => updateField("veoSoundEffects", e.target.value)}
                placeholder="Ambient & foley SFX cues..."
                className="w-full rounded-lg border border-zinc-200 bg-zinc-50 px-3 py-2 text-xs dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-950"
              />
              <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
                {AUDIO_DESIGN_TAGS.map((tag) => (
                  <button
                    key={tag.id}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => toggleAudioTag(tag.id)}
                    className={`rounded-md border px-2 py-1 text-xs ${
                      form.audioTags.includes(tag.id)
                        ? "border-amber-500 bg-amber-500/10"
                        : "border-zinc-200 text-zinc-500 dark:border-zinc-800"
                    }`}
                  >
                    {tag.label}
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Storyboard Shots + Negative + Sampler */}
          {engineSpec.supportsStoryboard && (
            <div className="space-y-3 rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 bg-white p-5 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900/70">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                <span className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-semibold">
                  <Film className="h-4 w-4 text-amber-500" />
                  Storyboard Shots
                </span>
                <select
                  value={form.shotsCount}
                  onChange={(e) => {
                    const next = Number(e.target.value);
                    setForm((prev) => ({
                      ...prev,
                      shotsCount: next,
                      shots: buildDefaultShots(next, prev.shots),
                    }));
                  }}
                  className="rounded border border-zinc-200 bg-zinc-50 px-2 py-1 font-mono text-xs dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-950"
                >
                  {[1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 8, 10].map((n) => (
                    <option key={n} value={n}>
                      {n} shot{n > 1 ? "s" : ""}
                    </option>
                  ))}
                </select>
              </div>
              <div className="space-y-2">
                {form.shots.slice(0, form.shotsCount).map((shot, idx) => (
                  <div key={shot.id} className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-2 sm:grid-cols-3">
                    <input
                      type="text"
                      value={shot.camera}
                      onChange={(e) => {
                        const updated = [...form.shots];
                        updated[idx] = { ...shot, camera: e.target.value };
                        updateField("shots", updated);
                      }}
                      placeholder={`Shot ${idx + 1} camera`}
                      className="rounded-lg border border-zinc-200 bg-zinc-50 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-950"
                    />
                    <input
                      type="text"
                      value={shot.description}
                      onChange={(e) => {
                        const updated = [...form.shots];
                        updated[idx] = { ...shot, description: e.target.value };
                        updateField("shots", updated);
                      }}
                      placeholder={`Shot ${idx + 1} action beat...`}
                      className="rounded-lg border border-zinc-200 bg-zinc-50 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs sm:col-span-2 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-950"
                    />
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {engineSpec.supportsNegative && (
            <div className="space-y-2 rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 bg-white p-5 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900/70">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                <span className="text-xs font-semibold">Negative Prompt</span>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() =>
                    setForm((prev) => ({
                      ...prev,
                      negativeEdited: false,
                      negative: buildSmartNegative(prev.engine, prev.mode, prev.category),
                    }))
                  }
                  className="text-xs text-amber-500 hover:underline"
                >
                  Auto Smart Negative
                </button>
              </div>
              <textarea
                rows={2}
                value={form.negative}
                onChange={(e) =>
                  setForm((prev) => ({
                    ...prev,
                    negativeEdited: true,
                    negative: e.target.value,
                  }))
                }
                placeholder="Artifacts to avoid..."
                className="w-full rounded-lg border border-zinc-200 bg-zinc-50 px-3 py-2 font-mono text-xs dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-950"
              />
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Action buttons */}
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handleEnhance}
              disabled={enhancing}
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-lg border border-zinc-200 bg-white px-4 py-2.5 text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-900 hover:bg-zinc-100 disabled:opacity-50 dark:border-zinc-700 dark:bg-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800"
            >
              {enhancing ? (
                <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" />
              ) : (
                <Sparkles className="h-4 w-4 text-amber-500" />
              )}
              <span>{copy.studioEnhanceBtn}</span>
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handleCompileAndSave}
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-lg bg-amber-500 px-5 py-2.5 text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-950 hover:bg-amber-400"
            >
              <Check className="h-4 w-4" />
              <span>{copy.studioCompileBtn}</span>
            </button>

            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-zinc-500">
              <span className="font-mono tabular-nums">Seed: {form.seed}</span>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => updateField("seedLocked", !form.seedLocked)}
                aria-label="Lock seed"
                className="rounded p-1 hover:bg-zinc-200 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800"
              >
                {form.seedLocked ? (
                  <Lock className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-amber-500" />
                ) : (
                  <Unlock className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                )}
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() =>
                  updateField("seed", Math.floor(100_000 + Math.random() * 8_999_999_999))
                }
                aria-label="Randomize seed"
                className="rounded p-1 hover:bg-zinc-200 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800"
              >
                <Dices className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Right Output & Calibration Column */}
        <div className="space-y-4 lg:col-span-5">
          <PromptOutputPanel
            lang={lang}
            engineName={engineSpec.name}
            compiled={compiledPreview}
            outputText={displayedOutput}
            onChangeOutput={setOutputText}
            onUnslop={() => {
              const res = cleanPromptWithUnslop(displayedOutput);
              if (res.changed) {
                setOutputText(res.cleaned);
                onNotify(`Removed ${res.wordsSaved} filler words.`, "ok");
              } else {
                onNotify("Prompt already clean.", "ok");
              }
            }}
            onFitLength={handleFitLength}
            fitting={fitting}
            onConvertFormat={handleConvertFormat}
            converting={converting}
            onExportComfy={() =>
              handleDownloadFile(
                `videoprompts-${form.engine}.json`,
                JSON.stringify(
                  buildComfyExportPayload(
                    form,
                    compiledPreview.positiveOnly || displayedOutput,
                    compiledPreview.negativeOnly
                  ),
                  null,
                  2
                ),
                "application/json"
              )
            }
            onExportPython={() =>
              handleDownloadFile(
                `videoprompts-${form.engine}.py`,
                buildPythonPipelineScript(
                  form,
                  compiledPreview.positiveOnly || displayedOutput,
                  compiledPreview.negativeOnly
                ),
                "text/x-python"
              )
            }
            onNotify={onNotify}
          />

          {/* Prompt Completeness Checklist */}
          <div className="rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 bg-white p-4 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900/70">
            <div className="mb-2 flex items-center justify-between text-xs">
              <span className="font-medium text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300">
                {lang === "ar" ? "اكتمال عناصر البرومبت" : "Prompt Structure Checklist"}
              </span>
              <span className="font-mono tabular-nums text-zinc-500">
                {qualityReport.passedCount}/{qualityReport.totalCount}
              </span>
            </div>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-1.5 text-xs">
              {qualityReport.checks.map((chk) => (
                <div
                  key={chk.id}
                  className={
                    chk.passed
                      ? "text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400"
                      : "text-zinc-400 dark:text-zinc-500"
                  }
                >
                  {chk.passed ? "✓" : "○"} {lang === "ar" ? chk.labelAr : chk.labelEn}
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Continuous Next Scene */}
          <div className="space-y-2 rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 bg-white p-4 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900/70">
            <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300">
              {lang === "ar" ? "توليد المشهد التالي المتصل" : "Generate Next Continuous Scene"}
            </label>
            <div className="flex gap-2">
              <input
                type="text"
                value={nextSceneIdea}
                onChange={(e) => setNextSceneIdea(e.target.value)}
                placeholder={
                  lang === "ar"
                    ? "ماذا يحدث في اللقطة التالية؟"
                    : "What happens in the next shot?"
                }
                className="flex-1 rounded-lg border border-zinc-200 bg-zinc-50 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-950"
              />
              <button
                type="button"
                disabled={generatingNextScene || !nextSceneIdea.trim()}
                onClick={handleNextScene}
                className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-lg bg-zinc-900 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-white disabled:opacity-50 dark:bg-zinc-100 dark:text-zinc-900"
              >
                {generatingNextScene ? (
                  <Loader2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5 animate-spin" />
                ) : (
                  <Plus className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                )}
                <span>{lang === "ar" ? "التالي" : "Next"}</span>
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import { ENGINE_ORDER, ENGINES } from "../data/engines";
import { I18N } from "../data/i18n";
import type { EngineId, Language } from "../types/prompt";

interface EngineGuideViewProps {
  lang: Language;
  onUseEngine: (engineId: EngineId) => void;
}

export function EngineGuideView({ lang, onUseEngine }: EngineGuideViewProps) {
  const copy = I18N[lang];

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <div className="space-y-1 border-b border-zinc-200 pb-5 dark:border-zinc-800">
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-semibold text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-50">
          {copy.guideTitle}
        </h1>
        <p className="text-sm text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400">
          {copy.guideSubtitle}
        </p>
      </div>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-5 md:grid-cols-2">
        {ENGINE_ORDER.map((id) => {
          const spec = ENGINES[id];
          return (
            <section
              key={spec.id}
              className="flex flex-col justify-between space-y-4 rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 bg-white p-5 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900/70"
            >
              <div className="space-y-3">
                <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-4">
                  <div>
                    <div className="text-xs text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400">
                      {spec.vendor} · {spec.encoder}
                    </div>
                    <h2 className="mt-0.5 text-lg font-semibold text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100">
                      {spec.name}
                    </h2>
                  </div>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => onUseEngine(spec.id)}
                    className="whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 rounded-lg border border-zinc-200 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-zinc-800 transition-colors hover:bg-zinc-100 dark:border-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-200 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800"
                  >
                    {copy.portalSecondaryBtn}
                  </button>
                </div>

                <div
                  dir="ltr"
                  className="rounded-lg border border-zinc-200 bg-zinc-50 px-3 py-2 font-mono text-xs text-zinc-800 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-200"
                >
                  {spec.promptStructure}
                </div>

                <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 text-xs text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400">
                  <span>Modes: {spec.modes.join(", ").toUpperCase()}</span>
                  <span aria-hidden="true">·</span>
                  <span>Aspects: {spec.aspectOptions.join(", ")}</span>
                  {spec.durationOptions.length > 0 && (
                    <>
                      <span aria-hidden="true">·</span>
                      <span className="tabular-nums">
                        Durations: {spec.durationOptions.map((d) => d.label).join(", ")}
                      </span>
                    </>
                  )}
                </div>

                <ul className="space-y-1.5 border-t border-zinc-200 pt-3 text-xs leading-relaxed text-zinc-600 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-300">
                  {spec.bestPractices.map((tip, idx) => (
                    <li key={idx}>• {tip[lang]}</li>
                  ))}
                </ul>
              </div>
            </section>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import { useState } from "react";
import {
  ArrowLeft,
  ArrowRight,
  Film,
  Sparkles,
  Wand2,
} from "lucide-react";
import { CURATED_PRESETS } from "../data/engines";
import { I18N } from "../data/i18n";
import type { EngineId, Language, PresetItem } from "../types/prompt";

interface PortalViewProps {
  lang: Language;
  onOpenGenerator: () => void;
  onOpenDirector: () => void;
  onOpenStudio: () => void;
  onLaunchEngine: (engine: EngineId) => void;
  onLoadPreset: (preset: PresetItem) => void;
}

function FourPointStar({ className = "h-3.5 w-3.5" }: { className?: string }) {
  return (
    <svg
      viewBox="0 0 24 24"
      fill="currentColor"
      aria-hidden="true"
      className={className}
    >
      <path d="M12 2L14.6 9.4L22 12L14.6 14.6L12 22L9.4 14.6L2 12L9.4 9.4L12 2Z" />
    </svg>
  );
}

export function PortalView({
  lang,
  onOpenGenerator,
  onOpenDirector,
  onOpenStudio,
  onLaunchEngine,
  onLoadPreset,
}: PortalViewProps) {
  const copy = I18N[lang];
  const [failedImages, setFailedImages] = useState<Record<string, boolean>>({});
  const isRtl = lang === "ar";
  const DirectionalArrow = isRtl ? ArrowLeft : ArrowRight;

  return (
    <div className="space-y-16">
      {/* Luxury Obsidian & Metallic Gold Portal Hero */}
      <section className="relative overflow-hidden rounded-3xl border border-zinc-200/80 bg-gradient-to-b from-white via-zinc-50 to-amber-50/40 px-5 py-12 text-center dark:border-[#C9A353]/20 dark:from-[#0E1015] dark:via-[#090A0D] dark:to-[#090A0D] sm:px-10 sm:py-16">
        {/* Subtle ambient stage lighting */}
        <div
          aria-hidden="true"
          className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-0 bg-[radial-gradient(ellipse_at_top,rgba(212,169,84,0.12),transparent_58%),radial-gradient(ellipse_at_bottom_right,rgba(34,211,238,0.06),transparent_50%)]"
        />

        <div className="relative mx-auto max-w-3xl space-y-6">
          <h1 className="mx-auto max-w-2xl bg-gradient-to-b from-amber-800 via-amber-700 to-amber-900 bg-clip-text text-3xl font-bold leading-tight tracking-tight text-transparent text-balance dark:from-[#F4DA92] dark:via-[#DFB963] dark:to-[#B88B36] sm:text-5xl sm:leading-[1.18]">
            {copy.portalHeadline}
          </h1>

          <p className="mx-auto max-w-2xl text-sm leading-relaxed text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-300/90 sm:text-base">
            {copy.portalLead}
          </p>

          {/* Central ARABIPROMPT Studio Gateway Card */}
          <div className="mx-auto mt-8 max-w-md rounded-[26px] border border-amber-500/30 bg-white/95 p-6 shadow-sm dark:border-[#C9A353]/35 dark:bg-[#121318]/95 sm:max-w-lg sm:p-8">
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-3 text-zinc-900 dark:text-[#F5F2EB]">
                <FourPointStar className="h-4 w-4 text-amber-600 dark:text-[#DEC07A]" />
                <span className="font-mono text-lg font-bold tracking-[0.26em] sm:text-xl">
                  ARABIPROMPT
                </span>
                <FourPointStar className="h-4 w-4 text-amber-600 dark:text-[#DEC07A]" />
              </div>
              <p className="text-xs text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400 sm:text-sm">
                {copy.portalStudioTagline}
              </p>
            </div>

            {/* Primary Metallic Gold CTA */}
            <div className="mt-6 flex justify-center">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={onOpenStudio}
                className="inline-flex min-w-[220px] items-center justify-center gap-2.5 whitespace-nowrap rounded-full bg-gradient-to-r from-[#E6C577] via-[#D8B25D] to-[#C2963D] px-8 py-3 text-sm font-bold text-zinc-950 transition-transform duration-150 hover:scale-[1.01] hover:brightness-105 focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-amber-500"
              >
                <span>{copy.portalEnterStudio}</span>
                <DirectionalArrow className="h-4 w-4" />
              </button>
            </div>

            {/* Stacked Engine Quick-Launch Buttons */}
            <div className="mt-6 space-y-2.5">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => onLaunchEngine("wan22")}
                className="flex w-full items-center justify-center gap-2.5 whitespace-nowrap rounded-xl border border-amber-600/30 bg-amber-50/70 px-4 py-3 text-sm font-medium text-zinc-900 transition-colors hover:border-amber-500/60 hover:bg-amber-100/70 dark:border-[#C9A353]/30 dark:bg-[#1E1A13] dark:text-[#F4EFE4] dark:hover:border-[#E2BC6B]/60 dark:hover:bg-[#262016]"
              >
                <Wand2 className="h-4 w-4 shrink-0 text-amber-600 dark:text-[#E2BC6B]" />
                <span>{copy.portalLaunchWan}</span>
              </button>

              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => onLaunchEngine("minimax")}
                className="flex w-full items-center justify-center gap-2.5 whitespace-nowrap rounded-xl border border-cyan-600/35 bg-cyan-50/70 px-4 py-3 text-sm font-medium text-cyan-800 transition-colors hover:border-cyan-500/60 hover:bg-cyan-100/70 dark:border-cyan-400/35 dark:bg-[#0D2127] dark:text-cyan-300 dark:hover:border-cyan-400/60 dark:hover:bg-[#102930]"
              >
                <Film className="h-4 w-4 shrink-0 text-cyan-600 dark:text-cyan-300" />
                <span>{copy.portalLaunchMinimax}</span>
              </button>

              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => onLaunchEngine("veo")}
                className="flex w-full items-center justify-center gap-2.5 whitespace-nowrap rounded-xl border border-zinc-300 bg-zinc-100/80 px-4 py-3 text-sm font-medium text-zinc-900 transition-colors hover:border-zinc-400 hover:bg-zinc-200/70 dark:border-zinc-700/80 dark:bg-[#1A1C22] dark:text-zinc-100 dark:hover:border-zinc-500 dark:hover:bg-[#21242C]"
              >
                <Sparkles className="h-4 w-4 shrink-0 text-cyan-600 dark:text-cyan-400" />
                <span>{copy.portalLaunchVeo}</span>
              </button>
            </div>

            {/* Direct Generator v1.0 & Director v2.0 Shortcuts */}
            <div className="mt-4 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-center gap-x-4 gap-y-2">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={onOpenGenerator}
                className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-amber-700 underline-offset-4 hover:underline dark:text-[#E2BC6B]"
              >
                <Film className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                <span>{copy.portalPrimaryBtn}</span>
              </button>
              <span aria-hidden="true" className="text-zinc-300 dark:text-zinc-700">
                ·
              </span>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={onOpenDirector}
                className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-cyan-700 underline-offset-4 hover:underline dark:text-cyan-400"
              >
                <Sparkles className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                <span>{copy.portalDirectorBtn}</span>
              </button>
            </div>

            {/* Unboxed Engine Directory Links */}
            <div className="mt-6 space-y-2 border-t border-zinc-200 pt-5 text-xs dark:border-zinc-800/80">
              <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-center gap-x-2.5 gap-y-1">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => onLaunchEngine("minimax")}
                  className="font-medium text-cyan-700 transition-colors hover:underline dark:text-cyan-400"
                >
                  MiniMax Hailuo 02
                </button>
                <span aria-hidden="true" className="text-zinc-400 dark:text-zinc-600">
                  ·
                </span>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => onLaunchEngine("wan22")}
                  className="font-medium text-zinc-600 transition-colors hover:text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-400 dark:hover:text-zinc-100"
                >
                  WAN 2.2 A14B / TI2V-5B
                </button>
              </div>

              <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-center gap-x-2.5 gap-y-1">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => onLaunchEngine("flux1")}
                  className="font-medium text-zinc-600 transition-colors hover:text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-400 dark:hover:text-zinc-100"
                >
                  FLUX.1
                </button>
                <span aria-hidden="true" className="text-zinc-400 dark:text-zinc-600">
                  ·
                </span>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => onLaunchEngine("hunyuan")}
                  className="font-medium text-zinc-600 transition-colors hover:text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-400 dark:hover:text-zinc-100"
                >
                  HunyuanVideo
                </button>
                <span aria-hidden="true" className="text-zinc-400 dark:text-zinc-600">
                  ·
                </span>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => onLaunchEngine("veo")}
                  className="font-medium text-cyan-700 transition-colors hover:underline dark:text-cyan-400"
                >
                  Google Veo 3.1
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* Curated Reference Cinema Presets */}
      <section className="space-y-5">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-zinc-200 pb-3 dark:border-zinc-800/80">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100">
            {copy.portalPresetsHeading}
          </h2>
        </div>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-6 md:grid-cols-3">
          {CURATED_PRESETS.map((preset) => (
            <article
              key={preset.id}
              className="flex flex-col overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 bg-white dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-[#121318]/90"
            >
              <div className="aspect-video w-full overflow-hidden bg-zinc-900">
                {preset.image && !failedImages[preset.id] ? (
                  <img
                    src={preset.image}
                    alt={preset.title[lang]}
                    referrerPolicy="no-referrer"
                    onError={() =>
                      setFailedImages((prev) => ({ ...prev, [preset.id]: true }))
                    }
                    className="h-full w-full object-cover"
                  />
                ) : (
                  <div className="flex h-full w-full items-center justify-center bg-gradient-to-br from-zinc-800 to-zinc-950 text-xs text-zinc-400">
                    {preset.title[lang]}
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>

              <div className="flex flex-1 flex-col justify-between space-y-4 p-4">
                <div className="space-y-1.5">
                  <div className="text-xs text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400">
                    {preset.subtitle[lang]}
                  </div>
                  <h3 className="text-base font-semibold text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100">
                    {preset.title[lang]}
                  </h3>
                  <p className="line-clamp-2 text-xs leading-relaxed text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400">
                    {preset.subject} — {preset.environment}
                  </p>
                </div>

                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => onLoadPreset(preset)}
                  className="inline-flex w-full items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-lg border border-zinc-200 px-3 py-2 text-xs font-medium text-zinc-800 transition-colors hover:bg-zinc-100 dark:border-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-200 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800"
                >
                  <span>{copy.portalLoadPreset}</span>
                  <DirectionalArrow className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                </button>
              </div>
            </article>
          ))}
        </div>
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}


####################################################################################################
FILE: src/components/HistoryView.tsx
####################################################################################################

import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import {
  Check,
  ChevronDown,
  ChevronUp,
  Copy,
  Download,
  Film,
  Search,
  SlidersHorizontal,
  Star,
  Trash2,
} from "lucide-react";
import { ENGINES } from "../data/engines";
import { I18N } from "../data/i18n";
import {
  ArabiCinematicDirector,
  type DirectorEngineId,
} from "../utils/arabiCinematicDirector";
import type {
  EngineId,
  HistoryEntry,
  Language,
  SavedSequenceEntry,
} from "../types/prompt";

interface HistoryViewProps {
  lang: Language;
  history: HistoryEntry[];
  savedSequences: SavedSequenceEntry[];
  onToggleFavorite: (id: string) => void;
  onDeleteEntry: (id: string) => void;
  onClearAll: () => void;
  onLoadEntry: (entry: HistoryEntry) => void;
  onToggleFavoriteSequence: (id: string) => void;
  onDeleteSequence: (id: string) => void;
  onClearSequences: () => void;
  onLoadSequence: (seq: SavedSequenceEntry) => void;
  onApplySequenceProps: (seq: SavedSequenceEntry) => void;
  onApplyEntryProps: (entry: HistoryEntry) => void;
}

interface PromptPropertyItem {
  label: string;
  value: string;
  numeric?: boolean;
}

function formatFirstScenePreview(rawPrompt: string): string {
  return rawPrompt
    .replace(/\n?→.*?(Wan 2\.2).*$/gm, "")
    .replace(
      /\s*\(consistent character design,\s*same color palette,\s*same lens,\s*35mm film grain,\s*cinematic grading\)\s*/gi,
      ""
    )
    .trim();
}

function countWords(text: string): number {
  const cleaned = text.trim();
  if (!cleaned) return 0;
  return cleaned.split(/\s+/).length;
}

export function HistoryView({
  lang,
  history,
  savedSequences,
  onToggleFavorite,
  onDeleteEntry,
  onClearAll,
  onLoadEntry,
  onToggleFavoriteSequence,
  onDeleteSequence,
  onClearSequences,
  onLoadSequence,
  onApplySequenceProps,
  onApplyEntryProps,
}: HistoryViewProps) {
  const copy = I18N[lang];
  const [subTab, setSubTab] = useState<"sequences" | "all">("sequences");
  const [favoritesOnly, setFavoritesOnly] = useState(false);
  const [query, setQuery] = useState("");
  const [engineFilter, setEngineFilter] = useState<EngineId | "all">("all");
  const [copiedId, setCopiedId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [expandedIds, setExpandedIds] = useState<Record<string, boolean>>({});

  const filteredHistory = useMemo(() => {
    const q = query.trim().toLowerCase();
    return history.filter((item) => {
      if (favoritesOnly && !item.isFavorite) return false;
      if (engineFilter !== "all" && item.engine !== engineFilter) return false;
      if (!q) return true;
      return (
        item.title.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        item.prompt.toLowerCase().includes(q)
      );
    });
  }, [engineFilter, favoritesOnly, history, query]);

  const filteredSequences = useMemo(() => {
    const q = query.trim().toLowerCase();
    return savedSequences.filter((seq) => {
      if (favoritesOnly && !seq.isFavorite) return false;
      if (!q) return true;
      return (
        seq.title.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        seq.idea.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        (seq.scenes[0]?.prompt || "").toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        seq.formattedOutput.toLowerCase().includes(q)
      );
    });
  }, [favoritesOnly, query, savedSequences]);

  const handleCopy = async (text: string, id: string) => {
    await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);
    setCopiedId(id);
    setTimeout(() => setCopiedId((prev) => (prev === id ? null : prev)), 1500);
  };

  const toggleExpanded = (id: string) => {
    setExpandedIds((prev) => ({ ...prev, [id]: !prev[id] }));
  };

  const handleExportSequence = (seq: SavedSequenceEntry) => {
    const blob = new Blob([JSON.stringify(seq, null, 2)], {
      type: "application/json",
    });
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const a = document.createElement("a");
    a.href = url;
    a.download = `sequence-${seq.model}-${seq.scenes.length}scenes.json`;
    a.click();
    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
  };

  const getSequenceProperties = (seq: SavedSequenceEntry): PromptPropertyItem[] => {
    const modelNames: Record<SavedSequenceEntry["model"], string> = {
      wan: "Wan 2.2",
      minimax: "MiniMax Hailuo",
      google: "Google Veo",
    };
    const totalWords = seq.scenes.reduce(
      (sum, sc) => sum + countWords(formatFirstScenePreview(sc.prompt)),
      0
    );
    const items: PromptPropertyItem[] = [
      { label: copy.propModel, value: modelNames[seq.model] || seq.model },
      {
        label: copy.propMode,
        value: seq.mode === "film_10" ? "Film 10" : seq.mode.toUpperCase(),
      },
      {
        label: copy.propScenes,
        value: `${seq.scenes.length}`,
        numeric: true,
      },
      {
        label: copy.propDuration,
        value: `${seq.shotLen}s × ${seq.scenes.length}`,
        numeric: true,
      },
      { label: copy.propStyle, value: seq.style || "-" },
      { label: copy.propLanguage, value: seq.lang.toUpperCase() },
      { label: copy.propWords, value: `${totalWords}`, numeric: true },
    ];
    if (seq.consistencyNotes?.lens) {
      items.push({ label: copy.propCamera, value: seq.consistencyNotes.lens });
    }
    return items;
  };

  const getEntryProperties = (entry: HistoryEntry): PromptPropertyItem[] => {
    const engineName = ENGINES[entry.engine]?.name || entry.engine;
    const words = countWords(entry.positiveOnly || entry.prompt);
    const snap = entry.stateSnapshot;
    const gen = entry.generatorSnapshot?.result;
    const dir = entry.directorSnapshot?.options;

    if (dir) {
      const dirEngineName =
        ArabiCinematicDirector.engines[
          String(dir.engine || "generic").toLowerCase() as DirectorEngineId
        ] || String(dir.engine || engineName);
      const items: PromptPropertyItem[] = [
        { label: copy.propModel, value: dirEngineName },
        {
          label: copy.propDuration,
          value: `${dir.duration ?? 15}s`,
          numeric: true,
        },
        {
          label: copy.propFps,
          value: `${dir.fps ?? 24} FPS`,
          numeric: true,
        },
        {
          label: copy.propAspect,
          value: dir.aspectRatio || "16:9",
          numeric: true,
        },
        {
          label: copy.propLanguage,
          value: (dir.language || "en").toUpperCase(),
        },
        { label: copy.propWords, value: `${words}`, numeric: true },
      ];
      if (dir.genre) {
        items.push({
          label: copy.propGenre,
          value:
            ArabiCinematicDirector.genres[dir.genre]?.name || dir.genre,
        });
      }
      if (dir.shotType) {
        items.push({ label: copy.propShotType, value: dir.shotType });
      }
      if (dir.mood) {
        items.push({ label: copy.propMood, value: dir.mood });
      }
      if (dir.grading) {
        items.push({ label: copy.propGrading, value: dir.grading });
      }
      return items;
    }

    if (gen) {
      const items: PromptPropertyItem[] = [
        { label: copy.propModel, value: engineName },
        {
          label: copy.propMode,
          value: gen.mode === "film_10" ? "Film 10" : gen.mode.toUpperCase(),
        },
        { label: copy.propStyle, value: gen.style || "-" },
        { label: copy.propDuration, value: `${gen.shotLen}s`, numeric: true },
        { label: copy.propLanguage, value: gen.lang.toUpperCase() },
        { label: copy.propWords, value: `${words}`, numeric: true },
      ];
      if (gen.scenes.length > 0) {
        items.push({
          label: copy.propScenes,
          value: `${gen.scenes.length}`,
          numeric: true,
        });
      }
      if (gen.consistencyNotes?.lens) {
        items.push({ label: copy.propCamera, value: gen.consistencyNotes.lens });
      }
      return items;
    }

    if (snap) {
      const cam =
