        [snap.cameraMovement, snap.cameraLens].filter(Boolean).join(" · ") ||
        snap.camera ||
        "-";
      const items: PromptPropertyItem[] = [
        { label: copy.propModel, value: engineName },
        { label: copy.propMode, value: entry.mode.toUpperCase() },
        { label: copy.propAspect, value: snap.aspect || "16:9", numeric: true },
        {
          label: copy.propDuration,
          value: snap.duration ? `${snap.duration}s` : "-",
          numeric: true,
        },
        { label: copy.propStyle, value: snap.style || entry.category || "-" },
        { label: copy.propCamera, value: cam },
        { label: copy.propLighting, value: snap.light || "-" },
        { label: copy.propWords, value: `${words}`, numeric: true },
      ];
      return items;
    }

    return [
      { label: copy.propModel, value: engineName },
      { label: copy.propMode, value: entry.mode.toUpperCase() },
      { label: copy.propStyle, value: entry.category || "-" },
      { label: copy.propWords, value: `${words}`, numeric: true },
    ];
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 border-b border-zinc-200 pb-5 dark:border-zinc-800 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
        <div className="space-y-2">
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-semibold text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-50">
            {copy.historyTitle}
          </h1>

          <div className="inline-flex rounded-lg bg-zinc-100 p-1 dark:bg-zinc-900">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setSubTab("sequences")}
              className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-md px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium transition-colors whitespace-nowrap ${
                subTab === "sequences"
                  ? "bg-white text-zinc-900 shadow-xs dark:bg-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-50"
                  : "text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400"
              }`}
            >
              <Film className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
              <span>{copy.historyTabSequences}</span>
              <span className="font-mono tabular-nums text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400">
                ({savedSequences.length})
              </span>
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setSubTab("all")}
              className={`rounded-md px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium transition-colors whitespace-nowrap ${
                subTab === "all"
                  ? "bg-white text-zinc-900 shadow-xs dark:bg-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-50"
                  : "text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400"
              }`}
            >
              {copy.historyTabAll} ({history.length})
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
          <div className="relative">
            <Search className="pointer-events-none absolute top-2.5 start-3 h-4 w-4 text-zinc-400" />
            <input
              type="text"
              value={query}
              onChange={(e) => setQuery(e.target.value)}
              placeholder={copy.historySearchPlaceholder}
              className="rounded-lg border border-zinc-200 bg-white py-2 pe-3 ps-9 text-xs text-zinc-900 focus:border-amber-500 focus:outline-none dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100"
            />
          </div>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setFavoritesOnly((prev) => !prev)}
            className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-lg border px-3 py-2 text-xs font-medium transition-colors whitespace-nowrap ${
              favoritesOnly
                ? "border-amber-500 bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400"
                : "border-zinc-200 bg-white text-zinc-700 hover:bg-zinc-100 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-200 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800"
            }`}
          >
            <Star className={`h-3.5 w-3.5 ${favoritesOnly ? "fill-amber-400 text-amber-400" : ""}`} />
            <span>{copy.historyFavoritesOnly}</span>
          </button>

          {subTab === "all" && (
            <select
              value={engineFilter}
              onChange={(e) => setEngineFilter(e.target.value as EngineId | "all")}
              className="rounded-lg border border-zinc-200 bg-white px-3 py-2 text-xs text-zinc-900 focus:border-amber-500 focus:outline-none dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100"
            >
              <option value="all">All Engines</option>
              {Object.values(ENGINES).map((e) => (
                <option key={e.id} value={e.id}>
                  {e.name}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          )}

          {subTab === "sequences" && savedSequences.length > 0 && (
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onClearSequences}
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-lg border border-zinc-200 px-3 py-2 text-xs font-medium text-red-600 transition-colors hover:bg-red-50 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:text-red-400 dark:hover:bg-red-950/30"
            >
              <Trash2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
              <span>{copy.historyClearAll}</span>
            </button>
          )}

          {subTab === "all" && history.length > 0 && (
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onClearAll}
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-lg border border-zinc-200 px-3 py-2 text-xs font-medium text-red-600 transition-colors hover:bg-red-50 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:text-red-400 dark:hover:bg-red-950/30"
            >
              <Trash2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
              <span>{copy.historyClearAll}</span>
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {subTab === "sequences" ? (
        filteredSequences.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="rounded-xl border border-dashed border-zinc-300 bg-white/60 p-10 text-center text-sm text-zinc-500 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900/40 dark:text-zinc-400">
            {copy.genSavedSequencesEmpty}
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="space-y-4">
            {filteredSequences.map((seq) => {
              const firstScene = seq.scenes[0];
              const firstSceneText = firstScene
                ? formatFirstScenePreview(firstScene.prompt)
                : "";
              const isExpanded = Boolean(expandedIds[seq.id]);
              const properties = getSequenceProperties(seq);

              return (
                <article
                  key={seq.id}
                  className="rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 bg-white p-4 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900/70"
                >
                  <div className="flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-3">
                    <div className="space-y-1">
                      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 text-xs text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400">
                        <span className="font-mono uppercase">{seq.model}</span>
                        <span aria-hidden="true">·</span>
                        <span className="font-mono tabular-nums">
                          {seq.scenes.length} scenes
                        </span>
                        <span aria-hidden="true">·</span>
                        <span className="font-mono tabular-nums">{seq.shotLen}s</span>
                        <span aria-hidden="true">·</span>
                        <span>{seq.style}</span>
                        <span aria-hidden="true">·</span>
                        <span className="font-mono tabular-nums">
                          {new Date(seq.createdAt).toLocaleDateString()}
                        </span>
                      </div>
                      <h2 className="text-base font-semibold text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100">
                        {seq.title}
                      </h2>
                    </div>

                    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5">
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => onToggleFavoriteSequence(seq.id)}
                        aria-label="Favorite sequence"
                        className="rounded-md border border-zinc-200 p-1.5 text-zinc-500 hover:bg-zinc-100 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800"
                      >
                        <Star
                          className={`h-3.5 w-3.5 ${
                            seq.isFavorite
                              ? "fill-amber-400 text-amber-400"
                              : "text-zinc-400"
                          }`}
                        />
                      </button>
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => handleCopy(seq.formattedOutput, seq.id)}
                        className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 whitespace-nowrap rounded-md border border-zinc-200 px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-zinc-700 hover:bg-zinc-100 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-200 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800"
                      >
                        {copiedId === seq.id ? (
                          <Check className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-emerald-500" />
                        ) : (
                          <Copy className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                        )}
                        <span>{copiedId === seq.id ? copy.genCopied : copy.genCopyAll}</span>
                      </button>
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => handleExportSequence(seq)}
                        className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 whitespace-nowrap rounded-md border border-zinc-200 px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-zinc-700 hover:bg-zinc-100 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-200 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800"
                      >
                        <Download className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                        <span>{copy.genExportSequence}</span>
                      </button>
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => onApplySequenceProps(seq)}
                        className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 whitespace-nowrap rounded-md border border-zinc-200 px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-zinc-700 hover:bg-zinc-100 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-200 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800"
                      >
                        <SlidersHorizontal className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                        <span>{copy.historyApplyProps}</span>
                      </button>
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => onLoadSequence(seq)}
                        className="whitespace-nowrap rounded-md bg-amber-500 px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-950 hover:bg-amber-400"
                      >
                        {copy.genLoadSequence}
                      </button>
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => onDeleteSequence(seq.id)}
                        aria-label="Delete sequence"
                        className="rounded-md border border-zinc-200 p-1.5 text-zinc-500 hover:text-red-500 dark:border-zinc-800"
                      >
                        <Trash2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  {/* Prompt Properties Specification Strip */}
                  <div className="mt-3 border-t border-zinc-200/80 pt-3 dark:border-zinc-800">
                    <div className="mb-2 text-xs font-medium text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400">
                      {copy.historyPromptPropsTitle}
                    </div>
                    <dl className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-x-4 gap-y-2 text-xs sm:grid-cols-4">
                      {properties.map((prop) => (
                        <div key={prop.label} className="min-w-0">
                          <dt className="text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400">{prop.label}</dt>
                          <dd
                            className={`truncate font-medium text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100 ${
                              prop.numeric ? "font-mono tabular-nums" : ""
                            }`}
                          >
                            {prop.value}
                          </dd>
                        </div>
                      ))}
                    </dl>
                  </div>

                  {/* Quick Visual Preview of the First Scene */}
                  {firstScene && (
                    <div className="mt-3 space-y-2.5 border-t border-zinc-200/80 pt-3 dark:border-zinc-800">
                      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
                        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 text-xs">
                          <span className="font-medium text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-200">
                            {copy.historyFirstScenePreview}
                          </span>
                          <span aria-hidden="true" className="text-zinc-400">
                            ·
                          </span>
                          <span className="font-mono font-semibold tabular-nums text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400">
                            SCENE 1/{seq.scenes.length}
                          </span>
                          {firstScene.narrativeBeat && (
                            <>
                              <span aria-hidden="true" className="text-zinc-400">
                                ·
                              </span>
                              <span className="text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400">
                                {firstScene.narrativeBeat}
                              </span>
                            </>
                          )}
                        </div>

                        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                          <button
                            type="button"
                            onClick={() =>
                              handleCopy(firstSceneText, `${seq.id}-scene1`)
                            }
                            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 whitespace-nowrap text-xs font-medium text-zinc-500 hover:text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-400 dark:hover:text-zinc-100"
                          >
                            {copiedId === `${seq.id}-scene1` ? (
                              <Check className="h-3 w-3 text-emerald-500" />
                            ) : (
                              <Copy className="h-3 w-3" />
                            )}
                            <span>
                              {copiedId === `${seq.id}-scene1`
                                ? copy.genCopied
                                : copy.genCopyScene}
                            </span>
                          </button>
                          <span aria-hidden="true" className="text-zinc-300 dark:text-zinc-700">
                            |
                          </span>
                          <button
                            type="button"
                            onClick={() => toggleExpanded(seq.id)}
                            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 whitespace-nowrap text-xs font-medium text-amber-600 hover:underline dark:text-amber-400"
                          >
                            <span>
                              {isExpanded
                                ? copy.historyHideFullOutput
                                : copy.historyShowFullOutput}
                            </span>
                            {isExpanded ? (
                              <ChevronUp className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                            ) : (
                              <ChevronDown className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                            )}
                          </button>
                        </div>
                      </div>

                      <p
                        dir="ltr"
                        className="whitespace-pre-wrap text-left font-mono text-xs leading-relaxed text-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-200"
                      >
                        {firstSceneText}
                      </p>

                      {seq.consistencyNotes &&
                        (seq.consistencyNotes.character ||
                          seq.consistencyNotes.palette ||
                          seq.consistencyNotes.lens) && (
                          <div
                            dir="ltr"
                            className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-2 border-t border-zinc-100 pt-2.5 text-xs text-zinc-500 dark:border-zinc-800/70 dark:text-zinc-400 sm:grid-cols-3"
                          >
                            <div className="truncate">
                              <span className="font-medium text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300">
                                Character:{" "}
                              </span>
                              <span>{seq.consistencyNotes.character}</span>
                            </div>
                            <div className="truncate">
                              <span className="font-medium text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300">
                                Palette:{" "}
                              </span>
                              <span>{seq.consistencyNotes.palette}</span>
                            </div>
                            <div className="truncate">
                              <span className="font-medium text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300">
                                Lens:{" "}
                              </span>
                              <span>{seq.consistencyNotes.lens}</span>
                            </div>
                          </div>
                        )}
                    </div>
                  )}

                  {(isExpanded || !firstScene) && (
                    <pre
                      dir="ltr"
                      className="mt-3 max-h-60 overflow-y-auto whitespace-pre-wrap border-t border-zinc-200 pt-3 text-left font-mono text-xs leading-relaxed text-zinc-600 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-400"
                    >
                      {seq.formattedOutput}
                    </pre>
                  )}
                </article>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        )
      ) : filteredHistory.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="rounded-xl border border-dashed border-zinc-300 bg-white/60 p-10 text-center text-sm text-zinc-500 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900/40 dark:text-zinc-400">
          {copy.historyEmpty}
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="space-y-4">
          {filteredHistory.map((entry) => {
            const genScenes = entry.generatorSnapshot?.result?.scenes || [];
            const firstScene = genScenes[0];
            const firstSceneText = firstScene
              ? formatFirstScenePreview(firstScene.prompt)
              : "";
            const isExpanded = Boolean(expandedIds[entry.id]);
            const properties = getEntryProperties(entry);

            return (
              <article
                key={entry.id}
                className="rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 bg-white p-4 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900/70"
              >
                <div className="flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-3">
                  <div className="space-y-1">
                    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 text-xs text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400">
                      <span>{ENGINES[entry.engine]?.name || entry.engine}</span>
                      <span aria-hidden="true">·</span>
                      <span className="uppercase">{entry.mode}</span>
                      {genScenes.length > 0 && (
                        <>
                          <span aria-hidden="true">·</span>
                          <span className="font-mono tabular-nums">
                            {genScenes.length} scenes
                          </span>
                        </>
                      )}
                      <span aria-hidden="true">·</span>
                      <span className="font-mono tabular-nums">{entry.time}</span>
                    </div>
                    <h2 className="text-base font-semibold text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100">
                      {entry.title}
                    </h2>
                  </div>

                  <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5">
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => onToggleFavorite(entry.id)}
                      aria-label="Favorite"
                      className="rounded-md border border-zinc-200 p-1.5 text-zinc-500 hover:bg-zinc-100 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800"
                    >
                      <Star
                        className={`h-3.5 w-3.5 ${
                          entry.isFavorite
                            ? "fill-amber-400 text-amber-400"
                            : "text-zinc-400"
                        }`}
                      />
                    </button>
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => handleCopy(entry.prompt, entry.id)}
                      className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 whitespace-nowrap rounded-md border border-zinc-200 px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-zinc-700 hover:bg-zinc-100 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-200 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800"
                    >
                      {copiedId === entry.id ? (
                        <Check className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-emerald-500" />
                      ) : (
                        <Copy className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                      )}
                      <span>{copiedId === entry.id ? copy.genCopied : copy.genCopyScene}</span>
                    </button>
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => onApplyEntryProps(entry)}
                      className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 whitespace-nowrap rounded-md border border-zinc-200 px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-zinc-700 hover:bg-zinc-100 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-200 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800"
                    >
                      <SlidersHorizontal className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                      <span>{copy.historyApplyProps}</span>
                    </button>
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => onLoadEntry(entry)}
                      className="whitespace-nowrap rounded-md bg-amber-500 px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-950 hover:bg-amber-400"
                    >
                      {copy.genLoadPrompt}
                    </button>
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => onDeleteEntry(entry.id)}
                      aria-label="Delete"
                      className="rounded-md border border-zinc-200 p-1.5 text-zinc-500 hover:text-red-500 dark:border-zinc-800"
                    >
                      <Trash2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </div>

                {/* Prompt Properties Specification Strip */}
                <div className="mt-3 border-t border-zinc-200/80 pt-3 dark:border-zinc-800">
                  <div className="mb-2 text-xs font-medium text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400">
                    {copy.historyPromptPropsTitle}
                  </div>
                  <dl className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-x-4 gap-y-2 text-xs sm:grid-cols-4">
                    {properties.map((prop) => (
                      <div key={prop.label} className="min-w-0">
                        <dt className="text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400">{prop.label}</dt>
                        <dd
                          className={`truncate font-medium text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100 ${
                            prop.numeric ? "font-mono tabular-nums" : ""
                          }`}
                        >
                          {prop.value}
                        </dd>
                      </div>
                    ))}
                  </dl>
                </div>

                {firstScene ? (
                  <div className="mt-3 space-y-2 border-t border-zinc-200/80 pt-3 dark:border-zinc-800">
                    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
                      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 text-xs">
                        <span className="font-medium text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-200">
                          {copy.historyFirstScenePreview}
                        </span>
                        <span aria-hidden="true" className="text-zinc-400">
                          ·
                        </span>
                        <span className="font-mono font-semibold tabular-nums text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400">
                          SCENE 1/{genScenes.length}
                        </span>
                        {firstScene.narrativeBeat && (
                          <>
                            <span aria-hidden="true" className="text-zinc-400">
                              ·
                            </span>
                            <span className="text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400">
                              {firstScene.narrativeBeat}
                            </span>
                          </>
                        )}
                      </div>
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => toggleExpanded(entry.id)}
                        className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 whitespace-nowrap text-xs font-medium text-amber-600 hover:underline dark:text-amber-400"
                      >
                        <span>
                          {isExpanded
                            ? copy.historyHideFullOutput
                            : copy.historyShowFullOutput}
                        </span>
                        {isExpanded ? (
                          <ChevronUp className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                        ) : (
                          <ChevronDown className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                        )}
                      </button>
                    </div>

                    <p
                      dir="ltr"
                      className="whitespace-pre-wrap text-left font-mono text-xs leading-relaxed text-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-200"
                    >
                      {firstSceneText}
                    </p>

                    {isExpanded && (
                      <pre
                        dir="ltr"
                        className="mt-2 max-h-60 overflow-y-auto whitespace-pre-wrap border-t border-zinc-200 pt-3 text-left font-mono text-xs leading-relaxed text-zinc-600 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-400"
                      >
                        {entry.prompt}
                      </pre>
                    )}
                  </div>
                ) : (
                  <pre
                    dir="ltr"
                    className="mt-3 max-h-40 overflow-y-auto whitespace-pre-wrap border-t border-zinc-200/80 pt-3 text-left font-mono text-xs leading-relaxed text-zinc-700 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-300"
                  >
                    {entry.positiveOnly || entry.prompt}
                  </pre>
                )}
              </article>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}


####################################################################################################
FILE: src/components/DirectorEngineView.tsx
####################################################################################################

import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import {
  AlertCircle,
  BookmarkCheck,
  Check,
  Clapperboard,
  Copy,
  Download,
  Film,
  Plus,
  RotateCcw,
  ShieldCheck,
  SlidersHorizontal,
  Trash2,
} from "lucide-react";
import type { Language } from "../types/prompt";
import { SmartSceneGuard } from "../utils/smartSceneGuard";
import {
  ArabiCinematicDirector,
  DEFAULT_DIRECTOR_NEGATIVES,
  DEFAULT_TIMELINE_BEATS,
  DIRECTOR_UI_LABELS,
  type DirectorCameraMovementId,
  type DirectorEngineId,
  type DirectorEnhanceOptions,
  type DirectorGenreId,
  type DirectorGradingId,
  type DirectorLanguageId,
  type DirectorMoodId,
  type DirectorOutputFormat,
  type DirectorShotTypeId,
  type TimelineBeat,
} from "../utils/arabiCinematicDirector";

interface DirectorEngineViewProps {
  lang: Language;
  activeSnapshot?: {
    options: DirectorEnhanceOptions;
    output: string;
  } | null;
  appliedProps?: (Partial<DirectorEnhanceOptions> & { nonce: number }) | null;
  onSaveToHistory: (options: DirectorEnhanceOptions, output: string) => void;
  onNotify: (msg: string, type: "ok" | "err") => void;
}

const LENS_SUGGESTIONS = [
  "35mm Anamorphic f/2.0",
  "50mm Prime f/1.4",
  "24mm Wide Cinema",
  "85mm Telephoto f/1.8",
  "100mm Macro",
];

function createDefaultOptions(lang: Language): DirectorEnhanceOptions {
  return {
    prompt: "",
    duration: ArabiCinematicDirector.config.defaultDuration,
    engine: "veo",
    aspectRatio: ArabiCinematicDirector.config.defaultAspectRatio,
    fps: ArabiCinematicDirector.config.defaultFPS,
    genre: "cinematic",
    shotType: "wide",
    cameraMovements: ["push_in"],
    lens: "",
    mood: "epic",
    grading: "teal_orange",
    lightingStyle: "",
    audioStyle: "",
    camera: true,
    physics: true,
    lighting: true,
    continuity: true,
    sound: true,
    timeline: true,
    beats: null,
    negatives: [],
    references: [],
    language: lang === "ar" ? "ar" : lang === "fr" ? "fr" : "en",
    format: "markdown",
  };
}

export function DirectorEngineView({
  lang,
  activeSnapshot,
  appliedProps,
  onSaveToHistory,
  onNotify,
}: DirectorEngineViewProps) {
  const isAr = lang === "ar";
  const isFr = lang === "fr";

  const [options, setOptions] = useState<DirectorEnhanceOptions>(() =>
    activeSnapshot?.options ? { ...activeSnapshot.options } : createDefaultOptions(lang)
  );
  const [generatedOutput, setGeneratedOutput] = useState<string>(
    activeSnapshot?.output || ""
  );
  const [validationErrors, setValidationErrors] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [customBeats, setCustomBeats] = useState<TimelineBeat[]>(() =>
    activeSnapshot?.options.beats?.length
      ? activeSnapshot.options.beats
      : DEFAULT_TIMELINE_BEATS.map((b) => ({ ...b }))
  );
  const [useCustomBeats, setUseCustomBeats] = useState<boolean>(
    Boolean(activeSnapshot?.options.beats?.length)
  );
  const [negativeInput, setNegativeInput] = useState("");
  const [referenceInput, setReferenceInput] = useState("");
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);
  const [justSaved, setJustSaved] = useState(false);
  const [showAdvancedTimeline, setShowAdvancedTimeline] = useState(false);
  const [includeSceneGuard, setIncludeSceneGuard] = useState(false);

  // Sync when loading a saved prompt from HistoryView
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!activeSnapshot) return;
    setOptions({ ...activeSnapshot.options });
    setGeneratedOutput(activeSnapshot.output);
    setValidationErrors([]);
    setJustSaved(false);
    if (activeSnapshot.options.beats?.length) {
      setCustomBeats(activeSnapshot.options.beats.map((b) => ({ ...b })));
      setUseCustomBeats(true);
    } else {
      setCustomBeats(DEFAULT_TIMELINE_BEATS.map((b) => ({ ...b })));
      setUseCustomBeats(false);
    }
  }, [activeSnapshot]);

  // Apply reused properties from HistoryView without overwriting current creative prompt
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!appliedProps) return;
    setOptions((prev) => ({
      ...prev,
      engine: appliedProps.engine ?? prev.engine,
      duration: appliedProps.duration ?? prev.duration,
      fps: appliedProps.fps ?? prev.fps,
      aspectRatio: appliedProps.aspectRatio ?? prev.aspectRatio,
      genre: appliedProps.genre !== undefined ? appliedProps.genre : prev.genre,
      shotType: appliedProps.shotType !== undefined ? appliedProps.shotType : prev.shotType,
      cameraMovements: appliedProps.cameraMovements ?? prev.cameraMovements,
      lens: appliedProps.lens !== undefined ? appliedProps.lens : prev.lens,
      mood: appliedProps.mood !== undefined ? appliedProps.mood : prev.mood,
      grading: appliedProps.grading !== undefined ? appliedProps.grading : prev.grading,
      lightingStyle:
        appliedProps.lightingStyle !== undefined
          ? appliedProps.lightingStyle
          : prev.lightingStyle,
      audioStyle:
        appliedProps.audioStyle !== undefined ? appliedProps.audioStyle : prev.audioStyle,
      camera: appliedProps.camera ?? prev.camera,
      physics: appliedProps.physics ?? prev.physics,
      lighting: appliedProps.lighting ?? prev.lighting,
      continuity: appliedProps.continuity ?? prev.continuity,
      sound: appliedProps.sound ?? prev.sound,
      timeline: appliedProps.timeline ?? prev.timeline,
      negatives: appliedProps.negatives ?? prev.negatives,
      language: appliedProps.language ?? prev.language,
      format: appliedProps.format ?? prev.format,
    }));
    setGeneratedOutput("");
    setJustSaved(false);
  }, [appliedProps]);

  const effectiveOptions: DirectorEnhanceOptions = useMemo(
    () => ({
      ...options,
      beats: useCustomBeats ? customBeats : null,
    }),
    [customBeats, options, useCustomBeats]
  );

  const timelinePreview = useMemo(() => {
    const safeDur = Math.min(
      ArabiCinematicDirector.config.maxDuration,
      Math.max(
        ArabiCinematicDirector.config.minDuration,
        Number(options.duration) || ArabiCinematicDirector.config.defaultDuration
      )
    );
    return ArabiCinematicDirector.buildTimeline(
      safeDur,
      useCustomBeats ? customBeats : null
    );
  }, [customBeats, options.duration, useCustomBeats]);

  const renderedOutput = useMemo(() => {
    if (!generatedOutput) return "";
    if (!includeSceneGuard) return generatedOutput;
    const provider = SmartSceneGuard.resolveProvider(options.engine || "universal");
    if ((options.format || "markdown") === "json") {
      try {
        const parsed = JSON.parse(generatedOutput) as Record<string, unknown>;
        return JSON.stringify(
          {
            ...parsed,
            sceneGuard: {
              version: SmartSceneGuard.version,
              provider,
              independent: true,
              avoid: SmartSceneGuard.negativeRules,
            },
          },
          null,
          2
        );
      } catch {
        return generatedOutput;
      }
    }
    return provider === "veo"
      ? SmartSceneGuard.applyWithinLimit(generatedOutput, provider, 2200)
      : SmartSceneGuard.apply(generatedOutput, provider);
  }, [generatedOutput, includeSceneGuard, options.engine, options.format]);

  const wordCount = useMemo(() => {
    const trimmed = SmartSceneGuard.strip(renderedOutput).trim();
    return trimmed ? trimmed.split(/\s+/).length : 0;
  }, [renderedOutput]);

  const toggleMovement = (id: DirectorCameraMovementId) => {
    setOptions((prev) => {
      const current = prev.cameraMovements || [];
      const exists = current.includes(id);
      return {
        ...prev,
        cameraMovements: exists
          ? current.filter((item) => item !== id)
          : [...current, id],
      };
    });
  };

  const handleAddNegative = () => {
    const clean = ArabiCinematicDirector.sanitize(negativeInput);
    if (!clean) return;
    setOptions((prev) => ({
      ...prev,
      negatives: [...new Set([...(prev.negatives || []), clean])],
    }));
    setNegativeInput("");
  };

  const handleRemoveNegative = (val: string) => {
    setOptions((prev) => ({
      ...prev,
      negatives: (prev.negatives || []).filter((n) => n !== val),
    }));
  };

  const handleAddReference = () => {
    const clean = ArabiCinematicDirector.sanitize(referenceInput);
    if (!clean) return;
    setOptions((prev) => ({
      ...prev,
      references: [...(prev.references || []), clean],
    }));
    setReferenceInput("");
  };

  const handleRemoveReference = (idx: number) => {
    setOptions((prev) => ({
      ...prev,
      references: (prev.references || []).filter((_, i) => i !== idx),
    }));
  };

  const handleLoadSample = (preset: "simple" | "advanced") => {
    setValidationErrors([]);
    setJustSaved(false);
    if (preset === "simple") {
      const next: DirectorEnhanceOptions = {
        ...createDefaultOptions(lang),
        prompt: "فارس يعبر صحراء ذهبية عند الغروب",
        duration: 15,
        engine: "veo",
        genre: "cinematic",
        shotType: "wide",
        cameraMovements: ["push_in"],
        mood: "epic",
        grading: "golden_hour",
        language: "ar",
        format: "markdown",
      };
      setOptions(next);
      setGeneratedOutput(ArabiCinematicDirector.enhance(next));
      return;
    }

    const advanced: DirectorEnhanceOptions = {
      prompt: "فارس يعبر صحراء ذهبية عند الغروب",
      duration: 20,
      engine: "kling",
      aspectRatio: "21:9",
      fps: 48,
      genre: "fantasy",
      shotType: "wide",
      cameraMovements: ["push_in", "orbit"],
      lens: "35mm Anamorphic f/2.0",
      mood: "epic",
      grading: "golden_hour",
      lightingStyle: "warm rim light with long shadows",
      audioStyle: "epic desert wind with distant drums",
      camera: true,
      physics: true,
      lighting: true,
      continuity: true,
      sound: true,
      timeline: true,
      negatives: ["modern buildings", "cars"],
      references: ["https://example.com/ref1.jpg"],
      language: "ar",
      format: "json",
    };
    setOptions(advanced);
    setGeneratedOutput(ArabiCinematicDirector.enhance(advanced));
  };

  const handleEnhance = (e?: React.FormEvent) => {
    if (e) e.preventDefault();
    const errs = ArabiCinematicDirector.validate(effectiveOptions);
    setValidationErrors(errs);
    if (errs.length > 0) {
      onNotify(errs[0], "err");
      return;
    }
    try {
      const result = ArabiCinematicDirector.enhance(effectiveOptions);
      setGeneratedOutput(result);
      setJustSaved(false);
    } catch (err) {
      const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Failed to enhance prompt.";
      setValidationErrors([msg]);
      onNotify(msg, "err");
    }
  };

  const handleFormatSwitch = (nextFormat: DirectorOutputFormat) => {
    const nextOptions: DirectorEnhanceOptions = {
      ...effectiveOptions,
      format: nextFormat,
    };
    setOptions((prev) => ({ ...prev, format: nextFormat }));
    if (generatedOutput && ArabiCinematicDirector.validate(nextOptions).length === 0) {
      setGeneratedOutput(ArabiCinematicDirector.enhance(nextOptions));
    }
  };

  const handleCopy = async () => {
    if (!renderedOutput) return;
    await navigator.clipboard.writeText(renderedOutput);
    setCopied(true);
    setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 1600);
  };

  const handleDownload = () => {
    if (!renderedOutput) return;
    const ext =
      options.format === "json" ? "json" : options.format === "plain" ? "txt" : "md";
    const mime =
      options.format === "json" ? "application/json" : "text/plain;charset=utf-8";
    const blob = new Blob([renderedOutput], { type: mime });
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const a = document.createElement("a");
    a.href = url;
    a.download = `arabiprompt-director-${options.engine || "generic"}.${ext}`;
    a.click();
    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
  };

  const handleSave = () => {
    if (!renderedOutput) return;
    onSaveToHistory(effectiveOptions, renderedOutput);
    setJustSaved(true);
    onNotify(
      isAr
        ? "تم حفظ البرومبت المخرج في السجل."
        : isFr
          ? "Prompt réalisateur enregistré dans l'historique."
          : "Director prompt saved to history.",
      "ok"
    );
  };

  const handleReset = () => {
    setOptions(createDefaultOptions(lang));
    setCustomBeats(DEFAULT_TIMELINE_BEATS.map((b) => ({ ...b })));
    setUseCustomBeats(false);
    setValidationErrors([]);
    setGeneratedOutput("");
    setJustSaved(false);
  };

  const uiTitle = isAr
    ? "محرك المخرج السينمائي v2.0"
    : isFr
      ? "Moteur Réalisateur Cinéma v2.0"
      : "Cinematic Director Engine v2.0";
  const uiSubtitle = isAr
    ? "تحكم سينمائي متكامل لـ 10 محركات فيديو: الأنواع السينمائية، اللقطات، حركات الكاميرا، التلوين، الإيقاع الزمني، الإطارات المرجعية، والتصدير المباشر."
    : isFr
      ? "Direction cinématique complète pour 10 moteurs vidéo : genres, types de plans, mouvements caméra, étalonnage, timeline, références et export."
      : "Complete deterministic cinematic directing for 10 video engines with genres, shot types, multi-camera movements, grading, custom timeline beats, references, and JSON/Markdown export.";

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      {/* Header Strip */}
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 border-b border-zinc-200 pb-5 dark:border-zinc-800 lg:flex-row lg:items-end lg:justify-between">
        <div className="space-y-1.5">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-amber-700 dark:text-amber-400">
            <span>ARABIPROMPT Engine v2.0</span>
            <span aria-hidden="true">·</span>
            <span className="font-mono tabular-nums">10 Engines</span>
            <span aria-hidden="true">·</span>
            <span className="font-mono tabular-nums">9 Genres</span>
            <span aria-hidden="true">·</span>
            <span className="font-mono tabular-nums">14 Shots</span>
          </div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-semibold text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-50">
            {uiTitle}
          </h1>
          <p className="max-w-3xl text-sm text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400">
            {uiSubtitle}
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => handleLoadSample("simple")}
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 whitespace-nowrap rounded-lg border border-zinc-200 bg-white px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-zinc-700 transition-colors hover:bg-zinc-100 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-300 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800"
          >
            <Film className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
            <span>{isAr ? "مثال بسيط (Veo 15s)" : "Sample: Veo 15s"}</span>
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => handleLoadSample("advanced")}
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 whitespace-nowrap rounded-lg border border-amber-500/30 bg-amber-50/70 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-amber-800 transition-colors hover:bg-amber-100 dark:border-amber-500/30 dark:bg-amber-500/10 dark:text-amber-300 dark:hover:bg-amber-500/20"
          >
            <Clapperboard className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
            <span>{isAr ? "مثال متقدم (Kling 21:9 · JSON)" : "Sample: Kling 21:9 JSON"}</span>
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={handleReset}
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 whitespace-nowrap rounded-lg border border-zinc-200 bg-white px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-zinc-600 transition-colors hover:bg-zinc-100 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-400 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800"
          >
            <RotateCcw className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
            <span>{isAr ? "إعادة ضبط" : isFr ? "Réinitialiser" : "Reset"}</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Main 2-Column Workspace */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-6 lg:grid-cols-12">
        {/* Left Controls Column */}
        <form onSubmit={handleEnhance} className="space-y-5 lg:col-span-7">
          {/* Creative Idea + Validation */}
          <div className="space-y-4 rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 bg-white p-5 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-[#121318]">
            <div className="space-y-1.5">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
                <label
                  htmlFor="director-prompt-input"
                  className="text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-200"
                >
                  {isAr ? "الفكرة الإبداعية للمشهد *" : isFr ? "Idée créative *" : "Creative Prompt Idea *"}
                </label>
                <span className="text-[11px] text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400">
                  {isAr ? "يُنظَّف تلقائياً من الرموز غير الصالحة" : "Auto-sanitized"}
                </span>
              </div>
              <textarea
                id="director-prompt-input"
                rows={3}
                value={options.prompt}
                onChange={(e) => {
                  setOptions((prev) => ({ ...prev, prompt: e.target.value }));
                  if (validationErrors.length) setValidationErrors([]);
                }}
                placeholder={
                  isAr
                    ? "مثال: فارس يعبر صحراء ذهبية عند الغروب..."
                    : "Example: A lone rider crossing a golden desert at sunset..."
                }
                className="w-full rounded-lg border border-zinc-200 bg-zinc-50 px-3.5 py-2.5 text-sm text-zinc-900 placeholder:text-zinc-400 focus:border-amber-500 focus:outline-none dark:border-zinc-700 dark:bg-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100"
              />
            </div>

            {/* Engine & Format & Language Row */}
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-3">
              <div className="space-y-1">
                <label
                  htmlFor="director-engine-select"
                  className="text-xs font-medium text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300"
                >
                  {isAr ? "محرك الفيديو" : isFr ? "Moteur vidéo" : "Video Engine"}
                </label>
                <select
                  id="director-engine-select"
                  value={String(options.engine || "veo")}
                  onChange={(e) =>
                    setOptions((prev) => ({
                      ...prev,
                      engine: e.target.value as DirectorEngineId,
                    }))
                  }
                  className="w-full rounded-lg border border-zinc-200 bg-zinc-50 px-3 py-2 text-xs font-medium text-zinc-900 focus:border-amber-500 focus:outline-none dark:border-zinc-700 dark:bg-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100"
                >
                  {(Object.keys(ArabiCinematicDirector.engines) as DirectorEngineId[]).map(
                    (id) => (
                      <option key={id} value={id}>
                        {ArabiCinematicDirector.engines[id]}
                      </option>
                    )
                  )}
                </select>
              </div>

              <div className="space-y-1">
                <label
                  htmlFor="director-language-select"
                  className="text-xs font-medium text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300"
                >
                  {isAr ? "لغة الإخراج" : isFr ? "Langue" : "Output Language"}
                </label>
                <select
                  id="director-language-select"
                  value={options.language || "ar"}
                  onChange={(e) =>
                    setOptions((prev) => ({
                      ...prev,
                      language: e.target.value as DirectorLanguageId,
                    }))
                  }
                  className="w-full rounded-lg border border-zinc-200 bg-zinc-50 px-3 py-2 text-xs font-medium text-zinc-900 focus:border-amber-500 focus:outline-none dark:border-zinc-700 dark:bg-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100"
                >
                  {(
                    Object.keys(ArabiCinematicDirector.languages) as DirectorLanguageId[]
                  ).map((l) => (
                    <option key={l} value={l}>
                      {ArabiCinematicDirector.languages[l].name} ({l.toUpperCase()})
                    </option>
                  ))}
                </select>
              </div>

              <div className="space-y-1">
                <label
                  htmlFor="director-format-select"
                  className="text-xs font-medium text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300"
                >
                  {isAr ? "تنسيق التصدير" : isFr ? "Format" : "Export Format"}
                </label>
                <select
                  id="director-format-select"
                  value={options.format || "markdown"}
                  onChange={(e) =>
                    handleFormatSwitch(e.target.value as DirectorOutputFormat)
                  }
                  className="w-full rounded-lg border border-zinc-200 bg-zinc-50 px-3 py-2 text-xs font-medium text-zinc-900 focus:border-amber-500 focus:outline-none dark:border-zinc-700 dark:bg-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100"
                >
                  <option value="markdown">Markdown (.md)</option>
                  <option value="json">JSON (.json)</option>
                  <option value="plain">Plain Text (.txt)</option>
                </select>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Technical Specs: Duration, FPS, Aspect Ratio */}
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 border-t border-zinc-100 pt-4 dark:border-zinc-800/80 sm:grid-cols-3">
              <div className="space-y-1">
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs">
                  <label
                    htmlFor="director-duration-input"
                    className="font-medium text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300"
                  >
                    {isAr ? "المدة (1–120 ثانية)" : "Duration (1–120s)"}
                  </label>
                  <span className="font-mono tabular-nums text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400">
                    {options.duration}s
                  </span>
                </div>
                <input
                  id="director-duration-input"
                  type="number"
                  min={1}
                  max={120}
                  value={options.duration ?? 15}
                  onChange={(e) =>
                    setOptions((prev) => ({
                      ...prev,
                      duration: Number(e.target.value),
                    }))
                  }
                  className="w-full rounded-lg border border-zinc-200 bg-zinc-50 px-3 py-2 font-mono text-xs tabular-nums text-zinc-900 focus:border-amber-500 focus:outline-none dark:border-zinc-700 dark:bg-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100"
                />
              </div>

              <div className="space-y-1">
                <label
                  htmlFor="director-fps-select"
                  className="text-xs font-medium text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300"
                >
                  {isAr ? "معدل الإطارات (FPS)" : "Frame Rate (FPS)"}
                </label>
                <select
                  id="director-fps-select"
                  value={options.fps ?? 24}
                  onChange={(e) =>
                    setOptions((prev) => ({ ...prev, fps: Number(e.target.value) }))
                  }
                  className="w-full rounded-lg border border-zinc-200 bg-zinc-50 px-3 py-2 font-mono text-xs tabular-nums text-zinc-900 focus:border-amber-500 focus:outline-none dark:border-zinc-700 dark:bg-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100"
                >
                  {ArabiCinematicDirector.config.supportedFPS.map((fpsVal) => (
                    <option key={fpsVal} value={fpsVal}>
                      {fpsVal} FPS
                    </option>
                  ))}
                </select>
              </div>

              <div className="space-y-1">
                <label
                  htmlFor="director-aspect-select"
                  className="text-xs font-medium text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300"
                >
                  {isAr ? "نسبة الأبعاد" : "Aspect Ratio"}
                </label>
                <select
                  id="director-aspect-select"
                  value={options.aspectRatio || "16:9"}
                  onChange={(e) =>
                    setOptions((prev) => ({ ...prev, aspectRatio: e.target.value }))
                  }
                  className="w-full rounded-lg border border-zinc-200 bg-zinc-50 px-3 py-2 font-mono text-xs tabular-nums text-zinc-900 focus:border-amber-500 focus:outline-none dark:border-zinc-700 dark:bg-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100"
                >
                  {ArabiCinematicDirector.config.supportedAspectRatios.map((ratio) => (
                    <option key={ratio} value={ratio}>
                      {ratio}
                    </option>
                  ))}
                </select>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Cinematic Direction: Genre, Shot Type, Mood, Grading */}
          <div className="space-y-4 rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 bg-white p-5 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-[#121318]">
            <h2 className="text-sm font-semibold text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100">
              {isAr
                ? "التوجيه السينمائي والمزاج والألوان"
                : isFr
                  ? "Genre, Plan, Ambiance & Étalonnage"
                  : "Cinematic Genre, Shot, Mood & Grading"}
            </h2>

            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
              <div className="space-y-1">
                <label
                  htmlFor="director-genre-select"
                  className="text-xs font-medium text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300"
                >
                  {isAr ? "النوع السينمائي (9 Genres)" : "Genre Preset (9)"}
                </label>
                <select
                  id="director-genre-select"
                  value={options.genre || ""}
                  onChange={(e) =>
                    setOptions((prev) => ({
                      ...prev,
                      genre: (e.target.value || null) as DirectorGenreId | null,
                    }))
                  }
                  className="w-full rounded-lg border border-zinc-200 bg-zinc-50 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-zinc-900 focus:border-amber-500 focus:outline-none dark:border-zinc-700 dark:bg-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100"
                >
                  <option value="">{isAr ? "بدون تحديد" : "None"}</option>
                  {(
                    Object.keys(ArabiCinematicDirector.genres) as DirectorGenreId[]
                  ).map((g) => (
                    <option key={g} value={g}>
                      {isAr
                        ? `${ArabiCinematicDirector.genres[g].arName} (${ArabiCinematicDirector.genres[g].name})`
                        : ArabiCinematicDirector.genres[g].name}
                    </option>
                  ))}
                </select>
              </div>

              <div className="space-y-1">
                <label
                  htmlFor="director-shot-select"
                  className="text-xs font-medium text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300"
                >
                  {isAr ? "نوع اللقطة (14 Shot Types)" : "Shot Type (14)"}
                </label>
                <select
                  id="director-shot-select"
                  value={options.shotType || ""}
                  onChange={(e) =>
                    setOptions((prev) => ({
                      ...prev,
                      shotType: (e.target.value || null) as DirectorShotTypeId | null,
                    }))
                  }
                  className="w-full rounded-lg border border-zinc-200 bg-zinc-50 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-zinc-900 focus:border-amber-500 focus:outline-none dark:border-zinc-700 dark:bg-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100"
                >
                  <option value="">{isAr ? "بدون تحديد" : "None"}</option>
                  {(
                    Object.keys(ArabiCinematicDirector.shotTypes) as DirectorShotTypeId[]
                  ).map((s) => (
                    <option key={s} value={s}>
                      {isAr
                        ? DIRECTOR_UI_LABELS.shotTypes[s].ar
                        : DIRECTOR_UI_LABELS.shotTypes[s].en}
                    </option>
                  ))}
                </select>
              </div>

              <div className="space-y-1">
                <label
                  htmlFor="director-mood-select"
                  className="text-xs font-medium text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300"
                >
                  {isAr ? "الحالة المزاجية (10 Moods)" : "Mood & Atmosphere (10)"}
                </label>
                <select
                  id="director-mood-select"
                  value={options.mood || ""}
                  onChange={(e) =>
                    setOptions((prev) => ({
                      ...prev,
                      mood: (e.target.value || null) as DirectorMoodId | null,
                    }))
                  }
                  className="w-full rounded-lg border border-zinc-200 bg-zinc-50 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-zinc-900 focus:border-amber-500 focus:outline-none dark:border-zinc-700 dark:bg-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100"
                >
                  <option value="">{isAr ? "بدون تحديد" : "None"}</option>
                  {(Object.keys(ArabiCinematicDirector.moods) as DirectorMoodId[]).map(
                    (m) => (
                      <option key={m} value={m}>
                        {isAr
                          ? DIRECTOR_UI_LABELS.moods[m].ar
                          : DIRECTOR_UI_LABELS.moods[m].en}
                      </option>
                    )
                  )}
                </select>
              </div>

              <div className="space-y-1">
                <label
                  htmlFor="director-grading-select"
                  className="text-xs font-medium text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300"
                >
                  {isAr ? "نمط التلوين (10 Grading)" : "Color Grading (10)"}
                </label>
                <select
                  id="director-grading-select"
                  value={options.grading || ""}
                  onChange={(e) =>
                    setOptions((prev) => ({
                      ...prev,
                      grading: (e.target.value || null) as DirectorGradingId | null,
                    }))
                  }
                  className="w-full rounded-lg border border-zinc-200 bg-zinc-50 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-zinc-900 focus:border-amber-500 focus:outline-none dark:border-zinc-700 dark:bg-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100"
                >
                  <option value="">{isAr ? "بدون تحديد" : "None"}</option>
                  {(
                    Object.keys(
                      ArabiCinematicDirector.gradingPresets
                    ) as DirectorGradingId[]
                  ).map((g) => (
                    <option key={g} value={g}>
                      {isAr
                        ? DIRECTOR_UI_LABELS.grading[g].ar
                        : DIRECTOR_UI_LABELS.grading[g].en}
                    </option>
                  ))}
                </select>
              </div>
            </div>

            {options.genre && ArabiCinematicDirector.genres[options.genre] && (
              <p className="text-xs leading-relaxed text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400">
                {ArabiCinematicDirector.genres[options.genre].instructions} ·{" "}
                {ArabiCinematicDirector.genres[options.genre].grading}
              </p>
            )}

            {/* Multi-select Camera Movements */}
            <div className="space-y-2 border-t border-zinc-100 pt-4 dark:border-zinc-800/80">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                <span className="text-xs font-medium text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300">
                  {isAr
                    ? "حركات الكاميرا (متعددة الاختيار · 14 حركة)"
                    : "Camera Movements (Multi-Select · 14)"}
                </span>
                <span className="font-mono text-[11px] tabular-nums text-zinc-500">
                  {(options.cameraMovements || []).length} selected
                </span>
              </div>
              <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
                {(
                  Object.keys(
                    ArabiCinematicDirector.cameraMovements
                  ) as DirectorCameraMovementId[]
                ).map((moveId) => {
                  const active = (options.cameraMovements || []).includes(moveId);
                  return (
                    <button
                      key={moveId}
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => toggleMovement(moveId)}
                      className={`rounded-md border px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-medium transition-colors whitespace-nowrap ${
                        active
                          ? "border-amber-500 bg-amber-50 text-amber-800 dark:border-amber-400/60 dark:bg-amber-500/15 dark:text-amber-300"
                          : "border-zinc-200 bg-zinc-50 text-zinc-600 hover:bg-zinc-100 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-400 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800"
                      }`}
                    >
                      {isAr
                        ? DIRECTOR_UI_LABELS.cameraMovements[moveId].ar
                        : DIRECTOR_UI_LABELS.cameraMovements[moveId].en}
                    </button>
                  );
                })}
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Lens, Lighting Style, Audio Style */}
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 border-t border-zinc-100 pt-4 dark:border-zinc-800/80 sm:grid-cols-3">
              <div className="space-y-1">
                <label
                  htmlFor="director-lens-input"
                  className="text-xs font-medium text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300"
                >
                  {isAr ? "العدسة (اختياري)" : "Lens (optional)"}
                </label>
                <input
                  id="director-lens-input"
                  type="text"
                  list="director-lens-list"
                  value={options.lens || ""}
                  onChange={(e) =>
                    setOptions((prev) => ({ ...prev, lens: e.target.value }))
                  }
                  placeholder="35mm Anamorphic f/2.0"
                  className="w-full rounded-lg border border-zinc-200 bg-zinc-50 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-zinc-900 focus:border-amber-500 focus:outline-none dark:border-zinc-700 dark:bg-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100"
                />
                <datalist id="director-lens-list">
                  {LENS_SUGGESTIONS.map((l) => (
                    <option key={l} value={l} />
                  ))}
                </datalist>
              </div>

              <div className="space-y-1">
                <label
                  htmlFor="director-lighting-input"
                  className="text-xs font-medium text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300"
                >
                  {isAr ? "أسلوب الإضاءة (اختياري)" : "Lighting Style"}
                </label>
                <input
                  id="director-lighting-input"
                  type="text"
                  value={options.lightingStyle || ""}
                  onChange={(e) =>
                    setOptions((prev) => ({ ...prev, lightingStyle: e.target.value }))
                  }
                  placeholder="warm rim light with long shadows"
                  className="w-full rounded-lg border border-zinc-200 bg-zinc-50 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-zinc-900 focus:border-amber-500 focus:outline-none dark:border-zinc-700 dark:bg-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100"
                />
              </div>

              <div className="space-y-1">
                <label
                  htmlFor="director-audio-input"
                  className="text-xs font-medium text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300"
                >
                  {isAr ? "أسلوب الصوت (اختياري)" : "Audio Style"}
                </label>
                <input
                  id="director-audio-input"
                  type="text"
                  value={options.audioStyle || ""}
                  onChange={(e) =>
                    setOptions((prev) => ({ ...prev, audioStyle: e.target.value }))
                  }
                  placeholder="epic desert wind with distant drums"
                  className="w-full rounded-lg border border-zinc-200 bg-zinc-50 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-zinc-900 focus:border-amber-500 focus:outline-none dark:border-zinc-700 dark:bg-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100"
                />
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Modules, Timeline Beats, Negatives, Reference Frames */}
          <div className="space-y-4 rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 bg-white p-5 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-[#121318]">
            {/* Module Toggles */}
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-200">
                  {isAr ? "وحدات الإخراج المفعّلة" : "Director Modules"}
                </span>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setShowAdvancedTimeline((v) => !v)}
                  className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-xs font-medium text-amber-700 hover:underline dark:text-amber-400"
                >
                  <SlidersHorizontal className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                  <span>
                    {showAdvancedTimeline
                      ? isAr
                        ? "إخفاء الإيقاع الزمني"
                        : "Hide Timeline Beats"
                      : isAr
                        ? "تخصيص الإيقاع الزمني (Beats)"
                        : "Customize Timeline Beats"}
                  </span>
                </button>
              </div>

              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 sm:grid-cols-3">
                {(
                  [
                    { key: "camera", ar: "الكاميرا", en: "Camera" },
                    { key: "physics", ar: "الفيزياء", en: "Physics" },
                    { key: "lighting", ar: "الإضاءة", en: "Lighting" },
                    { key: "continuity", ar: "الاتساق", en: "Continuity" },
                    { key: "sound", ar: "الصوت", en: "Audio" },
                    { key: "timeline", ar: "الجدول الزمني", en: "Timeline" },
                  ] as const
                ).map((mod) => {
                  const checked = options[mod.key] !== false;
                  return (
                    <label
                      key={mod.key}
                      className="flex cursor-pointer items-center gap-2 rounded-lg border border-zinc-200 bg-zinc-50 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-zinc-700 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-300"
                    >
                      <input
                        type="checkbox"
                        checked={checked}
                        onChange={(e) =>
                          setOptions((prev) => ({
                            ...prev,
                            [mod.key]: e.target.checked,
                          }))
                        }
                        className="accent-amber-500"
                      />
                      <span>{isAr ? mod.ar : mod.en}</span>
                    </label>
                  );
                })}
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Custom Timeline Beats Editor */}
            {showAdvancedTimeline && (
              <div className="space-y-3 border-t border-zinc-100 pt-4 dark:border-zinc-800/80">
                <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
                  <label className="flex cursor-pointer items-center gap-2 text-xs font-medium text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300">
                    <input
                      type="checkbox"
                      checked={useCustomBeats}
                      onChange={(e) => setUseCustomBeats(e.target.checked)}
                      className="accent-amber-500"
                    />
                    <span>
                      {isAr
                        ? "استخدام إيقاعات زمنية مخصصة (beats)"
                        : "Use custom timeline beats"}
                    </span>
                  </label>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => {
                        setUseCustomBeats(true);
                        setCustomBeats((prev) => [
                          ...prev,
                          { start: 0.75, end: 1, role: "Final visual beat" },
                        ]);
                      }}
                      className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded border border-zinc-200 px-2 py-1 text-[11px] font-medium text-zinc-700 hover:bg-zinc-100 dark:border-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800"
                    >
                      <Plus className="h-3 w-3" />
                      <span>{isAr ? "إضافة" : "Add Beat"}</span>
                    </button>
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => {
                        setCustomBeats(DEFAULT_TIMELINE_BEATS.map((b) => ({ ...b })));
                        setUseCustomBeats(false);
                      }}
                      className="text-[11px] font-medium text-zinc-500 hover:underline dark:text-zinc-400"
                    >
                      {isAr ? "الافتراضي" : "Default"}
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </div>

                {useCustomBeats && (
                  <div className="space-y-2">
                    {customBeats.map((beat, idx) => (
                      <div
                        key={idx}
                        className="grid grid-cols-12 items-center gap-2"
                      >
                        <input
                          type="number"
                          step="0.05"
                          min={0}
                          max={1}
                          value={beat.start}
                          onChange={(e) =>
                            setCustomBeats((prev) =>
                              prev.map((b, i) =>
                                i === idx ? { ...b, start: Number(e.target.value) } : b
                              )
                            )
                          }
                          aria-label={`Beat ${idx + 1} start`}
                          className="col-span-2 rounded border border-zinc-200 bg-zinc-50 px-2 py-1.5 font-mono text-xs tabular-nums dark:border-zinc-700 dark:bg-zinc-900"
                        />
                        <input
                          type="number"
                          step="0.05"
                          min={0}
                          max={1}
                          value={beat.end}
                          onChange={(e) =>
                            setCustomBeats((prev) =>
                              prev.map((b, i) =>
                                i === idx ? { ...b, end: Number(e.target.value) } : b
                              )
                            )
                          }
                          aria-label={`Beat ${idx + 1} end`}
                          className="col-span-2 rounded border border-zinc-200 bg-zinc-50 px-2 py-1.5 font-mono text-xs tabular-nums dark:border-zinc-700 dark:bg-zinc-900"
                        />
                        <input
                          type="text"
                          value={beat.role}
                          onChange={(e) =>
                            setCustomBeats((prev) =>
                              prev.map((b, i) =>
                                i === idx ? { ...b, role: e.target.value } : b
                              )
                            )
                          }
                          aria-label={`Beat ${idx + 1} role`}
                          className="col-span-7 rounded border border-zinc-200 bg-zinc-50 px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs dark:border-zinc-700 dark:bg-zinc-900"
                        />
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() =>
                            setCustomBeats((prev) => prev.filter((_, i) => i !== idx))
                          }
                          disabled={customBeats.length <= 1}
                          aria-label="Remove beat"
                          className="col-span-1 flex justify-center text-zinc-400 hover:text-red-500 disabled:opacity-40"
                        >
                          <Trash2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                        </button>
                      </div>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                )}

                <pre className="whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-lg bg-zinc-50 p-3 font-mono text-[11px] leading-relaxed tabular-nums text-zinc-600 dark:bg-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-400">
                  {timelinePreview}
                </pre>
              </div>
            )}

            {/* Negative Prompts & Reference Frames */}
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 border-t border-zinc-100 pt-4 dark:border-zinc-800/80 sm:grid-cols-2">
              <div className="space-y-2">
                <label
                  htmlFor="director-negative-input"
                  className="text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-200"
                >
                  {isAr ? "تجنب العناصر (Negative Prompts)" : "Negative Prompts"}
                </label>
                <div className="flex gap-1.5">
                  <input
                    id="director-negative-input"
                    type="text"
                    value={negativeInput}
                    onChange={(e) => setNegativeInput(e.target.value)}
                    onKeyDown={(e) => {
                      if (e.key === "Enter") {
                        e.preventDefault();
                        handleAddNegative();
                      }
                    }}
                    placeholder={isAr ? "مثال: modern buildings" : "e.g. modern buildings"}
                    className="flex-1 rounded-lg border border-zinc-200 bg-zinc-50 px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs text-zinc-900 focus:border-amber-500 focus:outline-none dark:border-zinc-700 dark:bg-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100"
                  />
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={handleAddNegative}
                    className="rounded-lg border border-zinc-200 bg-zinc-100 px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-zinc-700 hover:bg-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-700 dark:bg-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-200"
                  >
                    <Plus className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                  </button>
                </div>
                <p className="text-[11px] text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400">
                  {isAr
                    ? `يتضمن تلقائياً ${DEFAULT_DIRECTOR_NEGATIVES.length} قيود جودة افتراضية.`
                    : `Includes ${DEFAULT_DIRECTOR_NEGATIVES.length} built-in defaults automatically.`}
                </p>
                {(options.negatives || []).length > 0 && (
                  <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
                    {(options.negatives || []).map((neg) => (
                      <span
                        key={neg}
                        className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded border border-red-500/20 bg-red-500/10 px-2 py-0.5 text-[11px] text-red-700 dark:text-red-300"
                      >
                        <span>{neg}</span>
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => handleRemoveNegative(neg)}
                          aria-label={`Remove ${neg}`}
                          className="hover:text-red-500"
                        >
                          ×
                        </button>
                      </span>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>

              <div className="space-y-2">
                <label
                  htmlFor="director-ref-input"
                  className="text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-200"
                >
                  {isAr ? "الإطارات المرجعية (Reference Frames)" : "Reference Frames"}
                </label>
                <div className="flex gap-1.5">
                  <input
                    id="director-ref-input"
                    type="text"
                    value={referenceInput}
                    onChange={(e) => setReferenceInput(e.target.value)}
                    onKeyDown={(e) => {
                      if (e.key === "Enter") {
                        e.preventDefault();
                        handleAddReference();
                      }
                    }}
                    placeholder="https://example.com/ref1.jpg"
                    className="flex-1 rounded-lg border border-zinc-200 bg-zinc-50 px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs text-zinc-900 focus:border-amber-500 focus:outline-none dark:border-zinc-700 dark:bg-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100"
                  />
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={handleAddReference}
                    className="rounded-lg border border-zinc-200 bg-zinc-100 px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-zinc-700 hover:bg-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-700 dark:bg-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-200"
                  >
                    <Plus className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                  </button>
                </div>
                {(options.references || []).length > 0 && (
                  <ul className="space-y-1 text-[11px] text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400">
                    {(options.references || []).map((ref, idx) => (
                      <li
                        key={`${ref}-${idx}`}
                        className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 rounded bg-zinc-50 px-2 py-1 dark:bg-zinc-900"
                      >
                        <span className="truncate font-mono">
                          Ref {idx + 1}: {ref}
                        </span>
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => handleRemoveReference(idx)}
                          aria-label={`Remove reference ${idx + 1}`}
                          className="text-zinc-400 hover:text-red-500"
                        >
                          <Trash2 className="h-3 w-3" />
                        </button>
                      </li>
                    ))}
                  </ul>
                )}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {validationErrors.length > 0 && (
            <div
              role="alert"
              className="flex items-start gap-2.5 rounded-xl border border-red-500/30 bg-red-500/10 p-3.5 text-xs text-red-700 dark:text-red-300"
            >
              <AlertCircle className="mt-0.5 h-4 w-4 shrink-0" />
              <ul className="space-y-1">
