                {validationErrors.map((err, i) => (
                  <li key={i}>{err}</li>
                ))}
              </ul>
            </div>
          )}

          <button
            type="submit"
            className="inline-flex w-full items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-amber-500 px-5 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-zinc-950 transition-colors hover:bg-amber-400 focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-amber-500"
          >
            <Clapperboard className="h-4 w-4" />
            <span>
              {isAr
                ? "تحسين وتوليد البرومبت السينمائي (v2.0)"
                : isFr
                  ? "Générer le prompt réalisateur v2.0"
                  : "Enhance & Generate Director v2.0 Prompt"}
            </span>
          </button>
        </form>

        {/* Right Output Column */}
        <div className="space-y-4 lg:col-span-5">
          <div className="sticky top-6 space-y-4 rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 bg-white p-5 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-[#121318]">
            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 border-b border-zinc-100 pb-3 dark:border-zinc-800/80">
              <div className="space-y-0.5">
                <h2 className="text-sm font-semibold text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100">
                  {isAr ? "مخرجات المخرج السينمائي" : "Director v2.0 Output"}
                </h2>
                <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 font-mono text-[11px] tabular-nums text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400">
                  <span>{ArabiCinematicDirector.engines[(options.engine || "veo") as DirectorEngineId] || options.engine}</span>
                  <span aria-hidden="true">·</span>
                  <span>{options.duration}s</span>
                  <span aria-hidden="true">·</span>
                  <span>{options.fps} FPS</span>
                  <span aria-hidden="true">·</span>
                  <span>{options.aspectRatio}</span>
                  {generatedOutput && (
                    <>
                      <span aria-hidden="true">·</span>
                      <span>{wordCount} words</span>
                    </>
                  )}
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="inline-flex rounded-lg bg-zinc-100 p-0.5 dark:bg-zinc-900">
                {(["markdown", "json", "plain"] as DirectorOutputFormat[]).map((fmt) => (
                  <button
                    key={fmt}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => handleFormatSwitch(fmt)}
                    className={`rounded-md px-2.5 py-1 font-mono text-[11px] font-medium uppercase transition-colors ${
                      (options.format || "markdown") === fmt
                        ? "bg-white text-zinc-900 shadow-xs dark:bg-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-100"
                        : "text-zinc-500 hover:text-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-400 dark:hover:text-zinc-200"
                    }`}
                  >
                    {fmt}
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Optional Smart Scene Guard v2.0 Attachment */}
            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 rounded-lg border border-zinc-200 bg-zinc-50 px-3 py-2 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-950">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300">
                <ShieldCheck
                  className={`h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0 ${
                    includeSceneGuard ? "text-amber-500" : "text-zinc-400"
                  }`}
                />
                <span>
                  {isAr
                    ? "إرفاق قواعد الإخراج وحارس المشهد v2.0"
                    : "Attach Smart Scene Guard & Rules v2.0"}
                </span>
              </div>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setIncludeSceneGuard((v) => !v)}
                className={`whitespace-nowrap rounded-md border px-2.5 py-1 text-[11px] font-semibold transition-colors ${
                  includeSceneGuard
                    ? "border-amber-500 bg-amber-500/15 text-amber-800 dark:text-amber-300"
                    : "border-zinc-200 bg-white text-zinc-600 dark:border-zinc-700 dark:bg-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-400"
                }`}
              >
                {includeSceneGuard ? (isAr ? "مفعّل" : "On") : isAr ? "معطّل" : "Off"}
              </button>
            </div>

            {renderedOutput ? (
              <>
                <pre
                  dir="ltr"
                  className="max-h-[540px] overflow-auto whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-lg border border-zinc-200 bg-zinc-50 p-3.5 font-mono text-xs leading-relaxed text-zinc-800 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-200"
                >
                  {renderedOutput}
                </pre>

                <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={handleCopy}
                    className="inline-flex flex-1 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 whitespace-nowrap rounded-lg bg-zinc-900 px-3 py-2 text-xs font-medium text-white transition-colors hover:bg-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-100 dark:text-zinc-900 dark:hover:bg-zinc-200"
                  >
                    {copied ? (
                      <>
                        <Check className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                        <span>{isAr ? "تم النسخ" : "Copied"}</span>
                      </>
                    ) : (
                      <>
                        <Copy className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                        <span>{isAr ? "نسخ المخرجات" : "Copy Output"}</span>
                      </>
                    )}
                  </button>

                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={handleSave}
                    className="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 whitespace-nowrap rounded-lg border border-amber-500/30 bg-amber-50/70 px-3 py-2 text-xs font-medium text-amber-800 transition-colors hover:bg-amber-100 dark:border-amber-500/30 dark:bg-amber-500/10 dark:text-amber-300 dark:hover:bg-amber-500/20"
                  >
                    <BookmarkCheck className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                    <span>
                      {justSaved
                        ? isAr
                          ? "تم الحفظ"
                          : "Saved"
                        : isAr
                          ? "حفظ في السجل"
                          : "Save to History"}
                    </span>
                  </button>

                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={handleDownload}
                    className="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 whitespace-nowrap rounded-lg border border-zinc-200 px-3 py-2 text-xs font-medium text-zinc-700 transition-colors hover:bg-zinc-100 dark:border-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800"
                  >
                    <Download className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                    <span>.{options.format === "json" ? "json" : options.format === "plain" ? "txt" : "md"}</span>
                  </button>
                </div>
              </>
            ) : (
              <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center space-y-2 rounded-lg border border-dashed border-zinc-200 px-4 py-14 text-center dark:border-zinc-800">
                <Clapperboard className="h-7 w-7 text-zinc-400" />
                <p className="text-sm font-medium text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300">
                  {isAr
                    ? "جاهز لإخراج البرومبت السينمائي v2.0"
                    : "Ready for Cinematic Director v2.0"}
                </p>
                <p className="max-w-xs text-xs text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400">
                  {isAr
                    ? "أدخل فكرتك وحدد النوع واللقطة والتلوين، ثم اضغط توليد أو جرّب أحد الأمثلة الجاهزة."
                    : "Enter your concept, select genre/shot/grading presets, and generate in Markdown, JSON, or Plain Text."}
                </p>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


####################################################################################################
FILE: src/types/prompt.ts
####################################################################################################

import type { DirectorEnhanceOptions } from "../utils/arabiCinematicDirector";

export type Language = "ar" | "fr" | "en";
export type ThemeMode = "dark" | "light";
export type WorkspaceTab = "generator" | "portal" | "director" | "studio" | "guide" | "history";

export type EngineId =
  | "wan22"
  | "minimax"
  | "veo"
  | "hunyuan"
  | "ltx"
  | "flux1"
  | "sdxl";

export type WanVariant = "a14b" | "ti2v_5b";
export type GenerationMode = "t2v" | "i2v" | "flf2v" | "s2v" | "film" | "t2i" | "img2img";
export type PromptFormat = "official" | "structured" | "compact";

export type PhysicsHintId =
  | "gravity"
  | "fluid"
  | "volumetric"
  | "subsurface"
  | "inertia"
  | "caustics";

export type AudioTagId =
  | "bass"
  | "whoosh"
  | "clicks"

  | "foley";

export type GoogleVideoModel = "veo-3.1" | "veo-3.1-fast";
export type GoogleVideoDetail = "standard" | "professional" | "cinema";

export interface ShotItem {
  id: number;
  role?: string;
  camera: string;
  description: string;
}

export type StoryboardShot = ShotItem;

  engine: EngineId;
  wanVariant: WanVariant;
  promptFormat: PromptFormat;
  mode: GenerationMode;
  category: string;
  subject: string;
  environment: string;
  action: string;

  motionScale: number;
  shotsCount: number;
  shots: ShotItem[];
  cfgScale: number;
  steps: number;
  loraTags: string;
  seed: number;
  seedLocked: boolean;
  physicsHints: PhysicsHintId[];
  includeAudio: boolean;
  audioTags: AudioTagId[];
  audioDescription: string;
  negative: string;
  negativeEdited: boolean;
  minimaxResolution: "1080P" | "768P";
  minimaxCameraCommands: string[];
  minimaxCustomDirective: string;
  minimaxPromptOptimizer: boolean;
  veoModel: GoogleVideoModel;
  veoResolution: "720p" | "1080p";
  veoDialogue: string;
  veoSoundEffects: string;
  veoDetail: GoogleVideoDetail;
  veoLanguage: GoogleVideoLanguage;
  smartSceneGuard: boolean;
}

export interface HistoryEntry {
  id: string;
  engine: EngineId;
  wanVariant?: WanVariant;
  mode: GenerationMode;
  category: string;
  shotsCount: number;
  title: string;
  time: string;
  timestamp: number;
  isFavorite: boolean;
  prompt: string;
  positiveOnly: string;
  negativeOnly: string;

    result: VideoGeneratorResult;
    idea: string;
  };
  directorSnapshot?: {
    options: DirectorEnhanceOptions;
    output: string;
  };
}

  name: string;
}

export type VideoGeneratorModel = "wan" | "minimax" | "google";
export type VideoGeneratorMode = "normal" | "film" | "film_10";
export type VideoGeneratorLang = "ar" | "en";

export interface VideoGeneratorRequest {
  idea: string;
  model: VideoGeneratorModel;
  mode: VideoGeneratorMode;
  scenes: number;
  shotLen: number;
  style: string;
  lang: VideoGeneratorLang;
  allowMinimaxMerge?: boolean;
}

  sceneNumber: number;
  narrativeBeat: string;
  prompt: string;
}

  character: string;
  palette: string;
  lens: string;
}

  version: string;
  model: VideoGeneratorModel;
  mode: VideoGeneratorMode;
  style: string;
  lang: VideoGeneratorLang;
  shotLen: number;
  singlePrompt: string;
  scenes: VideoGeneratorScene[];
  consistencyNotes: VideoGeneratorConsistencyNotes | null;
  formattedOutput: string;
}

export interface SavedSequenceEntry {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  idea: string;
  model: VideoGeneratorModel;
  mode: VideoGeneratorMode;
  style: string;
  lang: VideoGeneratorLang;
  shotLen: number;
  scenes: VideoGeneratorScene[];
  consistencyNotes: VideoGeneratorConsistencyNotes | null;
  formattedOutput: string;
  createdAt: number;
  isFavorite: boolean;
}

  engine: EngineId;
  style: string;
  character: string;
  environment: string;
  camera: string;
  lighting: string;
  seed: number;
}

  negativeOnly: string;
  engine: EngineId;
  createdAt: number;
  sceneId: string;
  jobId?: string;
  continuityState?: string;
}

export interface SceneChainState {
  baseContext: SceneChainContext | null;
  scenes: SceneChainItem[];
}

  AudioTagId,
  EngineId,
  GenerationMode,
  PhysicsHintId,
  PresetItem,
  WanVariant,
} from "../types/prompt";

export interface EngineSpec {
  id: EngineId;
  name: string;
  vendor: string;
  kind: "video" | "image";
  encoder: string;
  defaultMode: GenerationMode;
  modes: GenerationMode[];
  aspectOptions: string[];
  durationOptions: { value: number; label: string; frames?: number }[];
  defaultDuration: number;
  defaultCfg: number;
  defaultSteps: number;
  supportsNegative: boolean;
  supportsStoryboard: boolean;
  supportsAudio: boolean;
  promptStructure: string;
  bestPractices: { ar: string; fr: string; en: string }[];
}

FILE: src/utils/cinematicPromptRules.ts

import { EngineId } from "../types/prompt";

export type CinematicProvider = "wan" | "minimax" | "veo" | "universal";

const RULE_MARKER = "[CINEMATIC PRODUCTION RULES]";

/**
 * Engine-aware directing rules. These are prompt instructions, not claims that
 * a model supports a feature. Keep each rule concise and avoid forcing cuts,
 * audio, lenses, or timelines that the user did not request.
 */
const PROVIDER_RULES: Record<CinematicProvider, string> = {
  wan: `MODEL PROFILE — WAN 2.2:
- Prefer concrete visual descriptions and one clearly readable primary action.
- Describe motion as a simple cause-and-effect sequence; avoid stacking many simultaneous actions.
- For a single-shot request, keep one continuous camera move and one coherent environment.
- For storyboard requests, make each shot visually distinct and specify only the transition needed.
- Keep prompts signal-rich and concise; avoid contradictory camera, motion, and lighting instructions.
- Do not assume native audio generation; include sound direction only when the selected workflow supports audio.`,
  minimax: `MODEL PROFILE — MINIMAX / HAILUO:
- Prioritize the main subject, its physical action, and the camera movement.
- Use direct, observable motion verbs and a clear beginning, development, and end.
- Avoid excessive micro-actions, multiple competing camera commands, and unnecessary technical jargon.
- Keep one coherent shot unless the user explicitly requests a sequence of shots.
- Do not assume native audio generation; include sound direction only when the selected workflow supports audio.`,
  veo: `MODEL PROFILE — GOOGLE VEO:
- Use natural-language cinematic direction with clear subject, action, setting, and camera intent.
- Describe physical interactions and synchronized sound only when requested and supported by the selected Veo model.
- Keep dialogue exact and assign it to the correct speaker when dialogue is provided.
- For fast generation, prioritize one subject, one primary action, and a simple camera instruction.
- Respect the selected model's duration, aspect ratio, resolution, and audio capabilities; never imply unsupported settings.`,
  universal: `MODEL PROFILE — UNIVERSAL VIDEO:
- Make the subject, setting, primary action, and camera intent unambiguous.
- Prefer observable details over abstract adjectives.
- Keep actions physically plausible and temporally ordered.
- Do not invent dialogue, sound, props, characters, or scene changes that the user did not request.`,
};

export const CinematicPromptRules = {
  version: "1.0.0",

  resolveProvider(engine: EngineId | string): CinematicProvider {
    const value = String(engine || "").toLowerCase();
    if (value.includes("wan")) return "wan";
    if (value.includes("minimax") || value.includes("hailuo")) return "minimax";
    if (value.includes("veo") || value.includes("google")) return "veo";
    return "universal";
  },

  build(engine: EngineId | string): string {
    const provider = this.resolveProvider(engine);
    return `${RULE_MARKER}\n\n${PROVIDER_RULES[provider]}\n\nGLOBAL FILM-DIRECTING RULES:\n- SUBJECT: establish the primary subject and preserve its visible identity and defining attributes.\n- ENVIRONMENT: establish a coherent location, spatial layout, foreground, background, weather, and important objects only as relevant.\n- ACTION: describe one primary action with a readable progression; secondary actions must support, not compete with, it.\n- CAMERA: specify framing and movement only when useful; do not combine incompatible movements or angles.\n- LIGHT: keep light direction, color, exposure, and reflections coherent throughout the shot.\n- PHYSICS: preserve believable gravity, momentum, collisions, fluid motion, cloth, hair, smoke, and environmental interaction when present.\n- TEMPORAL ORDER: events must happen in a clear order; use timestamps only when the user requests timed beats or a storyboard needs them.\n- CONTINUITY: preserve identity, wardrobe, props, geography, screen direction, and lighting within a continuous shot.\n- EDITING: one continuous take by default; use cuts or transitions only when multiple shots are explicitly requested.\n- ENDING: finish the requested action naturally; do not append an unrelated event or a new scene.\n- FIDELITY: preserve the user's idea and do not add decorative details that change its meaning.\n\nPROMPT CONFLICT CHECK:\nResolve contradictions by prioritizing the user's explicit creative instructions, then the selected model's supported capabilities, then these general production rules.\n\n[END CINEMATIC PRODUCTION RULES]`;
  },

  seriesBibleRules: `SERIES / FILM CONTINUITY BIBLE:\n- Treat the supplied base context as the canonical visual bible for the project.\n- Preserve recurring character identity, age appearance, face, hairstyle, wardrobe, accessories, and distinctive traits unless the script explicitly changes them.\n- Preserve established locations, architecture, geography, color palette, lens language, lighting mood, and recurring props.\n- Track each scene's starting state and ending state: character position, pose, gaze, prop placement, screen direction, time of day, and unresolved action.\n- The next scene must follow the requested story logic. Continue an action seamlessly only when it is the same moment; otherwise use a motivated time or location transition.\n- Do not repeat the previous scene's action, reset character positions without reason, or carry transient effects into a new scene.\n- Keep each generated scene independently renderable while preserving the series-level visual identity.\n- Do not change established facts or invent backstory, dialogue, characters, or props that conflict with the user's material.`,

  strip(prompt: string): string {
    if (!prompt || typeof prompt !== "string") return "";
    return prompt
      .replace(/\n*\[CINEMATIC PRODUCTION RULES\][\s\S]*?\[END CINEMATIC PRODUCTION RULES\]/g, "")
      .replace(/\n{3,}/g, "\n\n")
      .trim();
  },
};

==========================================================================================
FILE: src/utils/smartSceneGuard.ts
==========================================================================================

import { EngineId } from "../types/prompt";
import { CinematicPromptRules } from "./cinematicPromptRules";

export type SmartSceneGuardProvider = "wan" | "minimax" | "veo" | "universal";

export interface IsolatedSceneItem {
  sceneId: string;
  jobId: string;
  prompt: string;
  independent: true;
}

const ISOLATION_MARKER = "[SMART SCENE ISOLATION — STRICT MODE]";

export const SmartSceneGuard = {
  version: "2.0",

  isolationRules: `[SMART SCENE ISOLATION — STRICT MODE]

SCENE INDEPENDENCE:
Treat this generation as an independent cinematic scene.
Never import, reuse, or blend unrelated previous scenes.
Do not carry over previous backgrounds, characters, objects,
lighting setups, or camera compositions.

ENVIRONMENT LOCK:
Maintain one coherent environment throughout this shot.
No unexpected location changes or background replacement.

CHARACTER LOCK:
Preserve the identity, face, clothing, body proportions,
and appearance of every reference character.
No duplication, replacement, or identity drift.

TEMPORAL STABILITY:
No ghosting, residual frames, double exposure,
frame blending, flickering, visual trails, or unwanted morphing.

CAMERA CONTINUITY:
Follow only the camera movements requested by the user.
Maintain realistic camera physics and spatial continuity.
No unintended cuts or sudden camera jumps.

OBJECT CONSISTENCY:
Keep objects in their correct positions.
Do not introduce unrelated objects or duplicate existing objects.

SCENE BOUNDARIES:
One coherent shot unless multiple scenes are explicitly requested.
Keep intentional camera movements and transitions.
Do not blend unrelated scenes or environments.

FINAL FRAME:
End the requested scene naturally.
Do not introduce another scene at the end.

PRIORITY:
Preserve the user's original creative idea.
These instructions must not override the requested action.

[END SMART SCENE ISOLATION]`,

  negativeRules: `Avoid: scene blending, previous scene residue,
unrelated backgrounds, ghosting, double exposure,
duplicated characters, duplicated objects, flickering,
temporal artifacts, unwanted transitions, visual trails,
identity drift, environment morphing, camera jumps,
random scene changes, residual frames.`,

  providerRules: {
    wan: `[WAN TEMPORAL CONTROL]
Maintain stable frame-to-frame composition.
Preserve reference image identity.
Use smooth continuous motion.
Avoid unintended temporal blending.`,

    minimax: `[MINIMAX SHOT CONTROL]
Maintain one coherent cinematic shot.
Preserve subject and environment consistency.
No unexpected scene transitions.
Maintain natural camera and object motion.`,

    veo: `[VEO SCENE CONTROL]
Use clear natural-language visual direction.
Preserve subject, environment, and temporal consistency.
Use native audio only when enabled and supported by the selected model.`,

    universal: `[UNIVERSAL VIDEO CONSISTENCY]
Maintain visual and temporal consistency.`,
  } satisfies Record<SmartSceneGuardProvider, string>,

  resolveProvider(engine: EngineId | string): SmartSceneGuardProvider {
    const lower = String(engine || "").toLowerCase();
    if (lower.includes("wan")) return "wan";
    if (lower.includes("minimax") || lower.includes("hailuo")) return "minimax";
    if (lower.includes("veo") || lower.includes("google")) return "veo";
    return "universal";
  },

  apply(prompt: string, provider: string = "universal"): string {
    if (!prompt || typeof prompt !== "string" || !prompt.trim()) {
      throw new Error("A valid video prompt is required.");
    }

    if (prompt.includes(ISOLATION_MARKER) || prompt.includes("[SMART SCENE COMPACT CONTINUITY —")) {
      return prompt;
    }

    const providerKey = this.resolveProvider(provider);

    return [
      prompt.trim(),
      this.providerRules[providerKey],
      CinematicPromptRules.build(provider),
      this.isolationRules,
      this.negativeRules,
    ].join("\n\n");
  },

  applyWithinLimit(
    prompt: string,
    provider: string = "veo",
    maxChars: number
  ): string {
    if (!prompt || typeof prompt !== "string" || !prompt.trim()) {
      throw new Error("A valid video prompt is required.");
    }
    if (!Number.isFinite(maxChars) || maxChars <= 0) {
      return this.apply(prompt, provider);
    }
    const full = this.apply(prompt, provider);
    if (full.length <= maxChars) return full;

    const providerKey = this.resolveProvider(provider).toUpperCase();
    const compactRules = `[SMART SCENE COMPACT CONTINUITY — ${providerKey}]\nPreserve the user's original idea, subject identity, wardrobe, props, and one coherent environment. Keep motion, camera, lighting, and physics consistent. No ghosting, flicker, duplication, morphing, residual frames, or unintended cuts. Use only features supported by the selected model.`;
    const availablePromptChars = Math.max(0, maxChars - compactRules.length - 2);
    if (availablePromptChars < 40) {
      return prompt.trim().slice(0, maxChars);
    }
    const base = prompt.trim().length > availablePromptChars
      ? `${prompt.trim().slice(0, Math.max(1, availablePromptChars - 1)).trimEnd()}…`
      : prompt.trim();
    return `${base}\n\n${compactRules}`;
  },

  strip(prompt: string): string {
    if (!prompt || typeof prompt !== "string") return "";
    return prompt
      .replace(
        /\n*\[(?:WAN TEMPORAL CONTROL|MINIMAX SHOT CONTROL|VEO SCENE CONTROL|UNIVERSAL VIDEO CONSISTENCY)\][\s\S]*?(?=\n+\[CINEMATIC PRODUCTION RULES\]|\n+\[SMART SCENE ISOLATION — STRICT MODE\]|\n+AUDIO & SOUND DESIGN:|\n+NEGATIVE PROMPT:|$)/g,
        ""
      )
      .replace(
        /\n*\[CINEMATIC PRODUCTION RULES\][\s\S]*?\[END CINEMATIC PRODUCTION RULES\]/g,
        ""
      )
      .replace(/\n*\[SMART SCENE COMPACT CONTINUITY — [^\]]+\][\s\S]*$/g, "")
      .replace(
        /\n*\[SMART SCENE ISOLATION — STRICT MODE\][\s\S]*?\[END SMART SCENE ISOLATION\](?:\s*\n+Avoid:\s*scene blending,[\s\S]*?residual frames\.)?/g,
        ""
      )
      .replace(/\n*\[SMART SCENE ISOLATION — STRICT MODE\][\s\S]*$/g, "")
      .replace(/\n*Avoid:\s*scene blending,[\s\S]*?residual frames\./g, "")
      .replace(/\n{3,}/g, "\n\n")
      .trim();
  },

  separateScenes(
    scenes: string[],
    provider: string = "universal"
  ): IsolatedSceneItem[] {
    if (!Array.isArray(scenes)) {
      throw new Error("Scenes must be an array.");
    }

    const baseTs = Date.now();
    return scenes.map((scene, index) => ({
      sceneId: `scene_${index + 1}`,
      jobId: `sg_${baseTs}_${index + 1}_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 7)}`,
      prompt: this.apply(scene, provider),
      independent: true,
    }));
  },
};

==========================================================================================
FILE: src/utils/sceneChainParser.ts
==========================================================================================

import {
  EngineId,
  SceneChainContext,
  SceneChainItem,
  SceneChainState,
  StudioFormState,
} from "../types/prompt";

const HEADER_REGEX =
  /^\s*(?:={2,}\s*)?(?:المشهد|مشهد|Scene|Scène|Shot|SHOT)\s*(\d+)\s*(?:[:\-—–]\s*(.+?))?(?:\s*={2,})?\s*$/i;

function cleanTrailingMarkers(title: string): {
  idea: string;
  engineHint?: string;
} {
  let clean = title.replace(/={2,}\s*$/g, "").trim();
  let engineHint: string | undefined;
  const parenMatch = clean.match(/\(([^()]+)\)\s*$/);
  if (parenMatch) {
    engineHint = parenMatch[1].trim();
    clean = clean.slice(0, parenMatch.index).trim();
  }
  return { idea: clean, engineHint };
}

function summarizeIdea(promptText: string, order: number): string {
  const firstLine =
    promptText
      .split(/\r?\n/)
      .map((l) => l.trim())
      .find((l) => l.length > 0 && !l.startsWith("[")) || "";
  const cleaned = firstLine
    .replace(/^(SUBJECT|CINEMATOGRAPHY|STYLE|ACTION|SHOT\s*\d+)\s*:\s*/i, "")
    .trim();
  if (!cleaned) return `#${order}`;
  const words = cleaned.split(/\s+/).slice(0, 7).join(" ");
  return words.length < cleaned.length ? `${words}…` : words;
}

function splitPositiveNegative(prompt: string): {
  positiveOnly: string;
  negativeOnly: string;
} {
  const negMatch = prompt.match(/\n?NEGATIVE(?:\s+PROMPT|\s+CONSTRAINTS)?\s*:\s*([\s\S]*)$/i);
  if (!negMatch || negMatch.index === undefined) {
    return { positiveOnly: prompt.trim(), negativeOnly: "" };
  }
  return {
    positiveOnly: prompt.slice(0, negMatch.index).trim(),
    negativeOnly: negMatch[1].trim(),
  };
}

function extractField(text: string, labelPattern: RegExp): string {
  const lines = text.split(/\r?\n/);
  for (const line of lines) {
    const m = line.match(labelPattern);
    if (m && m[1]?.trim()) {
      return m[1].trim();
    }
  }
  return "";
}

function detectEngine(hint: string | undefined, fallback: EngineId): EngineId {
  if (!hint) return fallback;
  const lower = hint.toLowerCase();
  if (lower.includes("minimax") || lower.includes("hailuo")) return "minimax";
  if (lower.includes("veo")) return "veo";
  if (lower.includes("wan")) return "wan22";
  if (lower.includes("hunyuan")) return "hunyuan";
  if (lower.includes("ltx")) return "ltx";
  if (lower.includes("flux")) return "flux1";
  if (lower.includes("sdxl")) return "sdxl";
  return fallback;
}

export function parseSceneChainTxt(
  rawText: string,
  currentForm: StudioFormState
): SceneChainState | null {
  const normalized = rawText.replace(/\r\n/g, "\n").trim();
  if (!normalized) return null;

  const lines = normalized.split("\n");
  const parsedBlocks: Array<{
    order: number;
    idea: string;
    promptLines: string[];
    engineHint?: string;
  }> = [];

  let currentBlock: {
    order: number;
    idea: string;
    promptLines: string[];
    engineHint?: string;
  } | null = null;

  for (const line of lines) {
    const match = line.match(HEADER_REGEX);
    if (match) {
      if (currentBlock && currentBlock.promptLines.join("\n").trim()) {
        parsedBlocks.push(currentBlock);
      }
      const parsedOrder = Number(match[1]) || parsedBlocks.length + 1;
      const rawTitle = match[2] ? cleanTrailingMarkers(match[2]) : { idea: "" };
      currentBlock = {
        order: parsedOrder,
        idea: rawTitle.idea,
        promptLines: [],
        engineHint: rawTitle.engineHint,
      };
    } else if (currentBlock) {
      currentBlock.promptLines.push(line);
    }
  }

  if (currentBlock && currentBlock.promptLines.join("\n").trim()) {
    parsedBlocks.push(currentBlock);
  }

  // Fallback if no explicit scene headers were present: split on '---' or blank lines
  if (parsedBlocks.length === 0) {
    const hasDividers = /\n\s*-{3,}\s*\n/.test(normalized);
    const chunks = (
      hasDividers
        ? normalized.split(/\n\s*-{3,}\s*\n/)
        : normalized.split(/\n\s*\n+/)
    )
      .map((chunk) => chunk.trim())
      .filter(Boolean);

    if (chunks.length === 0) return null;

    chunks.forEach((chunk, idx) => {
      parsedBlocks.push({
        order: idx + 1,
        idea: summarizeIdea(chunk, idx + 1),
        promptLines: [chunk],
      });
    });
  }

  const now = Date.now();
  const scenes: SceneChainItem[] = parsedBlocks
    .map((block, idx) => {
      const prompt = block.promptLines.join("\n").trim();
      if (!prompt) return null;
      const order = idx + 1;
      const { positiveOnly, negativeOnly } = splitPositiveNegative(prompt);
      return {
        id: now + idx,
        order,
        idea: block.idea || summarizeIdea(prompt, order),
        prompt,
        positiveOnly,
        negativeOnly,
        engine: detectEngine(block.engineHint, currentForm.engine),
        createdAt: now + idx,
      };
    })
    .filter((item): item is SceneChainItem => item !== null);

  if (scenes.length === 0) return null;

  const firstPrompt = scenes[0].prompt;
  const seedMatch = firstPrompt.match(/\bseed\s*[:=]\s*(\d+)/i);
  const extractedSeed = seedMatch ? Number(seedMatch[1]) : currentForm.seed;

  const baseContext: SceneChainContext = {
    engine: scenes[0].engine,
    style:
      extractField(firstPrompt, /^(?:STYLE(?:\s*&\s*AMBIANCE)?|VISUAL STYLE)\s*:\s*(.+)$/i) ||
      currentForm.style ||
      "Cinematic photorealistic",
    character:
      extractField(firstPrompt, /^(?:MAIN\s+)?SUBJECT\s*:\s*(.+)$/i) ||
      currentForm.subject ||
      scenes[0].idea,
    environment:
      extractField(firstPrompt, /^(?:ENVIRONMENT|CONTEXT)\s*:\s*(.+)$/i) ||
      currentForm.environment,
    camera:
      extractField(firstPrompt, /^(?:CAMERA|CINEMATOGRAPHY)\s*:\s*(.+)$/i) ||
      currentForm.camera,
    lighting:
      extractField(firstPrompt, /^LIGHTING\s*:\s*(.+)$/i) || currentForm.light,
    seed: Number.isFinite(extractedSeed) ? extractedSeed : currentForm.seed,
  };

  return {
    baseContext,
    scenes,
  };
}

export function parseSceneChainInput(
  rawText: string,
  currentForm: StudioFormState
): SceneChainState | null {
  const trimmed = rawText.trim();
  if (!trimmed) return null;

  if (trimmed.startsWith("{") || trimmed.startsWith("[")) {
    try {
      const parsed = JSON.parse(trimmed) as
        | Partial<SceneChainState>
        | Partial<SceneChainItem>[];
      const rawScenes = Array.isArray(parsed)
        ? parsed
        : Array.isArray(parsed?.scenes)
          ? parsed.scenes
          : null;

      if (rawScenes && rawScenes.length > 0) {
        const now = Date.now();
        const scenes: SceneChainItem[] = rawScenes
          .map((item, idx) => {
            const prompt = typeof item?.prompt === "string" ? item.prompt.trim() : "";
            if (!prompt) return null;
            const order =
              typeof item.order === "number" && item.order > 0
                ? item.order
                : idx + 1;
            const { positiveOnly, negativeOnly } = splitPositiveNegative(prompt);
            return {
              id: typeof item.id === "number" ? item.id : now + idx,
              order,
              idea:
                typeof item.idea === "string" && item.idea.trim()
                  ? item.idea.trim()
                  : summarizeIdea(prompt, order),
              prompt,
              positiveOnly:
                typeof item.positiveOnly === "string" && item.positiveOnly.trim()
                  ? item.positiveOnly
                  : positiveOnly,
              negativeOnly:
                typeof item.negativeOnly === "string"
                  ? item.negativeOnly
                  : negativeOnly,
              engine:
                typeof item.engine === "string"
                  ? detectEngine(item.engine, currentForm.engine)
                  : currentForm.engine,
              createdAt:
                typeof item.createdAt === "number" ? item.createdAt : now + idx,
              ...(typeof item.continuityState === "string"
                ? { continuityState: item.continuityState }
                : {}),
            };
          })
          .filter((item): item is SceneChainItem => item !== null);

        if (scenes.length > 0) {
          const rawCtx = !Array.isArray(parsed) ? parsed?.baseContext : null;
          const baseContext: SceneChainContext = {
            engine: rawCtx?.engine
              ? detectEngine(rawCtx.engine, scenes[0].engine)
              : scenes[0].engine,
            style:
              typeof rawCtx?.style === "string" && rawCtx.style.trim()
                ? rawCtx.style
                : currentForm.style || "Cinematic photorealistic",
            character:
              typeof rawCtx?.character === "string" && rawCtx.character.trim()
                ? rawCtx.character
                : currentForm.subject || scenes[0].idea,
            environment:
              typeof rawCtx?.environment === "string"
                ? rawCtx.environment
                : currentForm.environment,
            camera:
              typeof rawCtx?.camera === "string"
                ? rawCtx.camera
                : currentForm.camera,
            lighting:
              typeof rawCtx?.lighting === "string"
                ? rawCtx.lighting
                : currentForm.light,
            seed:
              typeof rawCtx?.seed === "number" && Number.isFinite(rawCtx.seed)
                ? rawCtx.seed
                : currentForm.seed,
          };
          return { baseContext, scenes };
        }
      }
    } catch {
      // Fallback to TXT parsing if JSON is malformed
    }
  }

  return parseSceneChainTxt(rawText, currentForm);
}

function randomSeed(): number {
  return Math.floor(100_000 + Math.random() * 8_999_999_999);
}

async function safeReadJsonResponse<T = Record<string, unknown>>(
  res: Response
): Promise<T> {
  const text = await res.text();
  if (!text.trim()) return {} as T;
  try {
    return JSON.parse(text) as T;
  } catch {
    throw new Error(`HTTP ${res.status}: Upstream returned non-JSON response.`);
  }
}

function safeParseClientJson<T = Record<string, unknown>>(raw: string): T {
  const cleaned = String(raw || "{}")
    .replace(/```json/gi, "")
    .replace(/```/g, "")
    .trim();
  try {
    return JSON.parse(cleaned) as T;
  } catch {
    const start = cleaned.indexOf("{");
    const end = cleaned.lastIndexOf("}");
    if (start !== -1 && end > start) {
      return JSON.parse(cleaned.slice(start, end + 1)) as T;
    }
    throw new Error("Invalid JSON response returned by AI model.");
  }
}

function formatRetryWait(retryDelay: string | null | undefined, lang: Language): string {
  if (!retryDelay) return "";
  const match = String(retryDelay).match(/^(\d+)s$/);
  if (!match) return "";
  const secs = Number(match[1]);
  if (!Number.isFinite(secs) || secs <= 0) return "";
  const mins = Math.ceil(secs / 60);
  if (mins >= 60) {
    const hrs = Math.floor(mins / 60);
    const rem = mins % 60;
    if (lang === "ar") return rem > 0 ? ` (~${hrs} س ${rem} د)` : ` (~${hrs} س)`;
    if (lang === "fr") return rem > 0 ? ` (~${hrs} h ${rem} min)` : ` (~${hrs} h)`;
    return rem > 0 ? ` (~${hrs}h ${rem}m)` : ` (~${hrs}h)`;
  }
  if (lang === "ar") return ` (~${mins} دقيقة)`;
  if (lang === "fr") return ` (~${mins} min)`;
  return ` (~${mins} min)`;
}

function formatClientApiError(
  rawMsg: string,
  lang: Language,
  opts?: { httpStatus?: number; apiStatus?: string; retryDelay?: string | null }
): string {
  const text = String(rawMsg || "").trim();
  let apiStatus = opts?.apiStatus || "";
  let retryDelay = opts?.retryDelay || null;
  let httpStatus = opts?.httpStatus || 0;

  if (!apiStatus && text.includes("{")) {
    try {
      const parsed = JSON.parse(text.slice(text.indexOf("{"))) as {
        error?: { code?: number; status?: string; details?: Array<{ retryDelay?: string }> };
      };
      if (parsed?.error) {
        apiStatus = parsed.error.status || apiStatus;
        httpStatus = parsed.error.code || httpStatus;
        const rd = parsed.error.details?.find((d) => typeof d?.retryDelay === "string");
        if (rd?.retryDelay) retryDelay = rd.retryDelay;
      }
    } catch {
      // ignore JSON parse issues
    }
  }

  const isQuota =
    httpStatus === 429 ||
    apiStatus === "RESOURCE_EXHAUSTED" ||
    text.includes("RESOURCE_EXHAUSTED") ||
    text.toLowerCase().includes("quota exceeded");

  if (isQuota) {
    const waitHint = formatRetryWait(retryDelay, lang);
    if (lang === "ar") {
      return `تم تجاوز الحصة اليومية المجانية لواجهة Gemini${waitHint}. يمكنك تفعيل مفتاح بمستوى مدفوع عبر Settings > Secrets أو التبديل إلى Ollama المحلي.`;
    }
    if (lang === "fr") {
      return `Quota gratuit Gemini dépassé${waitHint}. Sélectionnez une clé avec facturation dans Settings > Secrets ou utilisez Ollama local.`;
    }
    return `Gemini Free Tier quota exceeded${waitHint}. Select a billing-enabled key in Settings > Secrets or switch to local Ollama.`;
  }

  const isKeyErr =
    httpStatus === 403 ||
    apiStatus === "PERMISSION_DENIED" ||
    apiStatus === "API_KEY_INVALID" ||
    apiStatus === "MISSING_KEY" ||
    text.includes("PERMISSION_DENIED") ||
    text.includes("API_KEY_INVALID") ||
    text.includes("GEMINI_API_KEY is not configured");

  if (isKeyErr) {
    if (lang === "ar") {
      return "مفتاح Gemini غير صالح أو غير مفعّل. يرجى التحقق من المفتاح في Settings > Secrets.";
    }
    if (lang === "fr") {
      return "Clé API Gemini invalide ou manquante. Vérifiez Settings > Secrets.";
    }
    return "Gemini API key is missing or lacks permission. Please check Settings > Secrets.";
  }

  if (/timed?\s*out|timeout|aborted/i.test(text)) {
    if (lang === "ar") {
      return "انتهت مهلة استجابة الذكاء الاصطناعي. يرجى المحاولة مرة أخرى.";
    }
    if (lang === "fr") {
      return "Le délai de réponse de l'IA a expiré. Veuillez réessayer.";
    }
    return "AI request timed out. Please try again.";
  }

  if (text.length > 220 && text.includes("{")) {
    return lang === "ar"
      ? "تعذر الاتصال بخدمة الذكاء الاصطناعي حالياً. حاول مرة أخرى بعد قليل."
      : lang === "fr"
        ? "Échec de la requête IA. Veuillez réessayer."
        : "AI request failed. Please try again shortly.";
  }

  return text;
}

function composeCameraDirective(movementLabel: string, lensLabel: string): string {
  const mov = CAMERA_MOVEMENTS.find((m) => m.label === movementLabel);
  const lens = CAMERA_LENSES.find((l) => l.label === lensLabel);
  const movPart = mov ? mov.prompt : movementLabel;
  const lensPart = lens ? lens.prompt : lensLabel;
  return [movPart, lensPart].filter(Boolean).join(", ");
}

function escapeRegExp(str: string): string {
  return str.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, "\\$&");
}

function hasSmartKeyword(text: string, keyword: string): boolean {
  const regex = new RegExp(`(^|[,،\\s])${escapeRegExp(keyword)}($|[,،\\s])`, "i");
  return regex.test(text);
}

function toggleSmartKeyword(text: string, keyword: string): string {
  const trimmed = text.trim();
  if (!trimmed) return keyword;

  if (hasSmartKeyword(trimmed, keyword)) {
    const removeRegex = new RegExp(
      `(?:^|\\s*[,،]\\s*)${escapeRegExp(keyword)}(?=\\s*[,،]|\\s*$)`,
      "gi"
    );
    return trimmed
      .replace(removeRegex, "")
      .replace(/^[\s,،]+|[\s,،]+$/g, "")
      .replace(/\s*([,،])\s*([,،])+/g, "$1 ")
      .trim();
  }

  return `${trimmed.replace(/[,،\s]+$/, "")}, ${keyword}`;
}

function createInitialFormState(): StudioFormState {
  const defaultEngine: EngineId = "wan22";
  const defaultVariant: WanVariant = "a14b";
  const spec = ENGINES[defaultEngine];
  const variantSpec = WAN_VARIANTS[defaultVariant];

  return {
    engine: defaultEngine,
    wanVariant: defaultVariant,
    promptFormat: "official",
    mode: spec.defaultMode,
    category: "",
    subject: "",
    environment: "",
    action: "",
    camera: "",
    cameraMovement: "",
    cameraLens: "",
    light: "",
    style: "",
    extra: "",
    aspect: variantSpec.aspectOptions[0],
    duration: spec.defaultDuration,
    motionScale: 6,
    shotsCount: 3,
    shots: buildDefaultShots(3),
    cfgScale: variantSpec.defaultCfg,
    steps: variantSpec.defaultSteps,
    loraTags: "",
    seed: randomSeed(),
    seedLocked: false,
    physicsHints: [],
    includeAudio: true,
    audioTags: [],
    audioDescription: "",
    negative: "",
    negativeEdited: false,
    aiProvider: "gemini",
    ollamaModel: "llama3.1",
    minimaxResolution: "1080P",
    minimaxCameraCommands: [],
    minimaxCustomDirective: "",
    minimaxPromptOptimizer: true,
    veoModel: "veo-3.1",
    veoResolution: "1080p",
    veoDialogue: "",
    veoSoundEffects: "",
    veoDetail: "professional",
    veoLanguage: "English",
    smartSceneGuard: true,
  };
}

function isValidEngineId(id: unknown): id is EngineId {
  return typeof id === "string" && Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(ENGINES, id);
}

function readStoredWorkspace(): {
  lang?: Language;
  theme?: ThemeMode;
  form?: Partial<StudioFormState>;
} | null {
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(STATE_STORAGE_KEY);
    if (!raw) return null;
    const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
    return parsed && typeof parsed === "object" ? parsed : null;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

export default function App() {
  const [lang, setLang] = useState<Language>(() => {
    const saved = readStoredWorkspace()?.lang;
    return saved === "ar" || saved === "fr" || saved === "en" ? saved : "ar";
  });
  const [theme, setTheme] = useState<ThemeMode>(() => {
    const saved = readStoredWorkspace()?.theme;
    return saved === "dark" || saved === "light" ? saved : "dark";
  });
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<WorkspaceTab>("portal");
  const [mobileMenuOpen, setMobileMenuOpen] = useState(false);

  const [form, setForm, resetPersistedForm] = usePersistentForm(
    createInitialFormState,
    FORM_STORAGE_KEY,
    STATE_STORAGE_KEY
  );

  const [history, setHistory] = useState<HistoryEntry[]>(() => {
    try {
      const raw = localStorage.getItem(HISTORY_STORAGE_KEY);
      if (raw) {
        const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
        if (Array.isArray(parsed)) {
          return parsed.filter(
            (item): item is HistoryEntry =>
              Boolean(item) &&
              typeof item === "object" &&
              typeof item.id === "string" &&
              typeof item.prompt === "string" &&
              isValidEngineId(item.engine)
          );
        }
      }
    } catch {
      // ignore
    }
    return [];
  });

  const [directing, setDirecting] = useState(false);

  const [sceneChain, setSceneChain] = useState<SceneChainState>(() => {
    try {
      const raw = localStorage.getItem(SCENE_CHAIN_STORAGE_KEY);
      if (raw) {
        const parsed = JSON.parse(raw) as Partial<SceneChainState>;
        if (parsed && Array.isArray(parsed.scenes)) {
          const validScenes = parsed.scenes.filter(
            (s): s is SceneChainItem =>
              Boolean(s) &&
              typeof s === "object" &&
              typeof s.prompt === "string" &&
              isValidEngineId(s.engine)
          );
          const safeBase =
            parsed.baseContext && typeof parsed.baseContext === "object"
              ? {
                  ...parsed.baseContext,
                  engine: isValidEngineId(parsed.baseContext.engine)
                    ? parsed.baseContext.engine
                    : "wan22",
                }
              : null;
          return {
            baseContext: safeBase,
            scenes: validScenes,
          };
        }
      }
    } catch {
      // ignore
    }
    return { baseContext: null, scenes: [] };
  });

  const [outputText, setOutputText, clearPersistedOutput] = usePersistentOutput(
    () => sceneChain.scenes[sceneChain.scenes.length - 1]?.prompt ?? ""
  );
  const [positiveText, setPositiveText] = useState(() => {
    const last = sceneChain.scenes[sceneChain.scenes.length - 1];
    if (last && outputText === last.prompt) {
      return last.positiveOnly;
    }
    return outputText ? extractOutputParts(outputText).positiveOnly : "";
  });
  const [negativeText, setNegativeText] = useState(() => {
    const last = sceneChain.scenes[sceneChain.scenes.length - 1];
    if (last && outputText === last.prompt) {
      return last.negativeOnly;
    }
    return outputText ? extractOutputParts(outputText).negativeOnly : "";
  });
  const [hasCompiledOnce, setHasCompiledOnce] = useState(
    () => outputText.trim().length > 0 || sceneChain.scenes.length > 0
  );
  const [addingNextScene, setAddingNextScene] = useState(false);
  const [chainError, setChainError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [activePresetId, setActivePresetId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [referenceImage, setReferenceImage] =
    useState<ReferenceImageState | null>(null);
  const [endReferenceImage, setEndReferenceImage] =
    useState<ReferenceImageState | null>(null);

  const [enhancing, setEnhancing] = useState(false);
  const [analyzingImage, setAnalyzingImage] = useState(false);
  const [analyzingTransition, setAnalyzingTransition] = useState(false);
  const [fittingLength, setFittingLength] = useState(false);
  const [convertingTarget, setConvertingTarget] = useState<
    "hailuo02" | "veo31" | null
  >(null);
  const [testingConnection, setTestingConnection] = useState(false);
  const [confirmNegReset, setConfirmNegReset] = useState(false);
  const [copiedType, setCopiedType] = useState<"full" | "positive" | null>(null);
  const [promptSavedRecently, setPromptSavedRecently] = useState(false);
  const [openPanels, setOpenPanels] = useState({
    sceneDetails: false,
    minimax: false,
    veo: false,
    engineOpts: false,
    camera: false,
    lighting: false,
    technical: false,
    audio: false,
    storyboard: false,
    negativeAi: false,
  });
  const [minimaxLaunchState, setMinimaxLaunchState] = useState<MiniMaxLaunchState>({
    open: false,
    submitting: false,
    polling: false,
    mode: null,
    launchUrl: "https://hailuoai.video/create",
    taskId: null,
    statusText: null,
    videoUrl: null,
    error: null,
    compiledPrompt: "",
    duration: 6,
    resolution: "1080P",
    hasFirstFrame: false,
    hasLastFrame: false,
  });
  const [status, setStatus] = useState<{ msg: string; type: "ok" | "err" | null }>({
    msg: "",
    type: null,
  });

  // Firebase Auth & Admin-only Visitor Counter state
  const [authReady, setAuthReady] = useState(false);
  const [currentUserEmail, setCurrentUserEmail] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [isAdmin, setIsAdmin] = useState(false);
  const [authLoading, setAuthLoading] = useState(false);
  const [visitorStats, setVisitorStats] = useState<AdminVisitorStats | null>(null);
  const [visitorStatsLoading, setVisitorStatsLoading] = useState(false);

  const togglePanel = (key: keyof typeof openPanels) => {
    setOpenPanels((prev) => ({ ...prev, [key]: !prev[key] }));
  };

  const anyPanelOpen = Object.values(openPanels).some(Boolean);
  const setAllPanels = (open: boolean) => {
    setOpenPanels({
      sceneDetails: open,
      minimax: open,
      veo: open,
      engineOpts: open,
      camera: open,
      lighting: open,
      technical: open,
      audio: open,
      storyboard: open,
      negativeAi: open,
    });
  };

  // Record one real persistent visit per browser session
  useEffect(() => {
    void recordSiteVisit(lang, activeTab).catch(() => {
      // Structured error already logged inside handleFirestoreError
    });
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, []);

  // Listen to Firebase Authentication and verify admin privileges
  useEffect(() => {
    const unsubscribe = onAuthStateChanged(auth, async (user) => {
      setCurrentUserEmail(user?.email ?? null);
      if (user) {
        const adminFlag = await checkUserIsAdmin(user);
        setIsAdmin(adminFlag);
      } else {
        setIsAdmin(false);
        setVisitorStats(null);
      }
      setAuthReady(true);
    });
    return () => unsubscribe();
  }, []);

  // Subscribe to live visitor analytics ONLY when authenticated as verified Admin
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!authReady || !isAdmin) {
      return;
    }
    setVisitorStatsLoading(true);
    const unsubscribe = subscribeAdminVisitorStats(
      (nextStats) => {
        setVisitorStats(nextStats);
        setVisitorStatsLoading(false);
      },
      () => {
        setVisitorStatsLoading(false);
      }
    );
    return () => unsubscribe();
  }, [authReady, isAdmin]);

  const handleAdminSignIn = async () => {
    setAuthLoading(true);
    try {
      const cred = await signInWithPopup(auth, googleProvider);
      const allowed = await checkUserIsAdmin(cred.user);
      if (!allowed) {
        showStatus(
          `${copy.adminNotAuthorized} (${cred.user.email || ""})`,
          "err"
        );
      }
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      const code =
        typeof err === "object" && err !== null && "code" in err
          ? String((err as { code?: unknown }).code || "")
          : "";
      if (
        code === "auth/popup-closed-by-user" ||
        code === "auth/cancelled-popup-request"
      ) {
        return;
      }
      const message =
        err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Admin authentication failed.";
      showStatus(message, "err");
    } finally {
      setAuthLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const handleAdminSignOut = async () => {
    setAuthLoading(true);
    try {
      await signOut(auth);
      setIsAdmin(false);
      setVisitorStats(null);
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      const message = err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Sign out failed.";
      setStatus({ msg: message, type: "err" });
    } finally {
      setAuthLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const handleRefreshVisitorStats = async () => {
    if (!isAdmin) return;
    setVisitorStatsLoading(true);
    try {
      const exact = await fetchAdminExactCounts();
      setVisitorStats((prev) =>
        prev
          ? {
              ...prev,
              totalVisits: exact.totalVisits,
              todayVisits: exact.todayVisits,
              lastUpdatedAt: Date.now(),
            }
          : {
              totalVisits: exact.totalVisits,
              todayVisits: exact.todayVisits,
              uniqueRecentVisitors: 0,
              langCounts: { ar: 0, fr: 0, en: 0 },
              recentVisits: [],
              lastUpdatedAt: Date.now(),
            }
      );
    } catch {
      // Structured error logged inside fetchAdminExactCounts
    } finally {
      setVisitorStatsLoading(false);
    }
  };

  // Cleanup any active MiniMax polling loop on unmount
  useEffect(() => {
    return () => {
      activeMinimaxPollRef.current = null;
    };
  }, []);

  // Sync HTML attributes for RTL/LTR and dark/light theme
  useEffect(() => {
    const root = document.documentElement;
    root.lang = lang;
    root.dir = lang === "ar" ? "rtl" : "ltr";
    root.classList.toggle("dark", theme === "dark");
  }, [lang, theme]);

  // Persist UI language and theme preferences
  useEffect(() => {
    try {
      localStorage.setItem(
        STATE_STORAGE_KEY,
        JSON.stringify({ lang, theme })
      );
    } catch {
      // ignore
    }
  }, [lang, theme]);

  // Persist history
  useEffect(() => {
    try {
      localStorage.setItem(HISTORY_STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(history));
    } catch {
      // ignore
    }
  }, [history]);

  // Persist sequential scene chain across page reloads
  useEffect(() => {
    try {
      if (sceneChain.scenes.length === 0 && !sceneChain.baseContext) {
        localStorage.removeItem(SCENE_CHAIN_STORAGE_KEY);
      } else {
        localStorage.setItem(SCENE_CHAIN_STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(sceneChain));
      }
    } catch {
      // ignore
    }
  }, [sceneChain]);

  const copy = I18N[lang];
  const engineSpec = ENGINES[form.engine];
  const activeWanSpec = WAN_VARIANTS[form.wanVariant];
  const activeModes =
    form.engine === "wan22" ? activeWanSpec.modes : engineSpec.modes;
  const activeAspectOptions =
    form.engine === "wan22"
      ? activeWanSpec.aspectOptions
      : engineSpec.aspectOptions;
  const activeDurationOptions =
    form.engine === "wan22"
      ? activeWanSpec.durationOptions
      : engineSpec.durationOptions;
  const needsReferenceImage =
    form.mode === "i2v" ||
    form.mode === "flf2v" ||
    form.mode === "s2v" ||
    form.mode === "img2img";

  const qualityReport = useMemo(() => {
    const targetText = outputText.trim() || compileEnginePrompt(form).fullPrompt;
    return analyzePromptQuality(form, targetText);
  }, [form, outputText]);

  const veoMeta = useMemo(() => {
    if (form.engine !== "veo") return null;
    if (!form.subject.trim() && !form.action.trim()) return null;
    try {
      return compileGoogleVeoFromStudio(form).meta;
    } catch {
      return null;
    }
  }, [form]);

  const showStatus = useCallback((msg: string, type: "ok" | "err") => {
    setStatus({ msg, type });
  }, []);

  const pendingResyncRef = useRef(false);
  const activeMinimaxPollRef = useRef<string | null>(null);

  const resyncOutputIfActive = useCallback(
    (nextForm: StudioFormState) => {
      if (!hasCompiledOnce) return;
      if (nextForm.engine === "veo") {
        if (!nextForm.subject.trim() && !nextForm.action.trim()) return;
      } else if (
        !nextForm.subject.trim() &&
        !nextForm.action.trim() &&
        !nextForm.environment.trim()
      ) {
        return;
      }
      const compiled = compileEnginePrompt(nextForm);
      setOutputText(compiled.fullPrompt);
      setPositiveText(compiled.positiveOnly);
      setNegativeText(compiled.negativeOnly);
    },
    [hasCompiledOnce]
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!pendingResyncRef.current) return;
    pendingResyncRef.current = false;
    resyncOutputIfActive(form);
  }, [form, resyncOutputIfActive]);

  const handleChangeOutput = useCallback((nextValue: string) => {
    setOutputText(nextValue);
    const { positiveOnly, negativeOnly } = extractOutputParts(nextValue);
    setPositiveText(positiveOnly);
    setNegativeText(negativeOnly);
  }, [setOutputText]);

  const handleUnslopOutput = useCallback(() => {
    if (!outputText.trim()) {
      showStatus(copy.errEmptyOutput, "err");
      return;
    }
    const result = cleanPromptWithUnslop(outputText);
    if (!result.changed) {
      showStatus(
        lang === "ar"
          ? "البرومبت نقي بالفعل ولا يحتوي على كلمات حشوية زائدة."
          : lang === "fr"
            ? "Le prompt est déjà épuré et sans remplissage superflu."
            : "Prompt is already clean with no filler jargon found.",
        "ok"
      );
      return;
    }
    handleChangeOutput(result.cleaned);
    showStatus(
      lang === "ar"
        ? `تم تنظيف البرومبت (Unslop) وإزالة ${result.wordsSaved > 0 ? `${result.wordsSaved} كلمة حشوية` : `${result.removedCount} عبارة مكررة`}.`
        : lang === "fr"
          ? `Prompt nettoyé (Unslop) · ${result.wordsSaved} mot(s) superflu(s) retiré(s).`
          : `Prompt cleaned with Unslop (${result.wordsSaved > 0 ? `${result.wordsSaved} filler words` : `${result.removedCount} phrases`} removed).`,
      "ok"
    );
  }, [copy.errEmptyOutput, handleChangeOutput, lang, outputText, showStatus]);

  const updateFormField = <K extends keyof StudioFormState>(
    key: K,
    value: StudioFormState[K],
    shouldResync = false
  ) => {
    setActivePresetId(null);
    if (shouldResync) {
      pendingResyncRef.current = true;
    }
    setForm((prev) => ({ ...prev, [key]: value }));
  };

  const handleSelectEngine = (nextEngineId: EngineId) => {
    const nextSpec = ENGINES[nextEngineId];
    setActivePresetId(null);
    setConfirmNegReset(false);
    pendingResyncRef.current = true;
    setForm((prev) => {
      const variantSpec = WAN_VARIANTS[prev.wanVariant];
      const allowedModes =
        nextEngineId === "wan22" ? variantSpec.modes : nextSpec.modes;
      const allowedAspects =
        nextEngineId === "wan22"
          ? variantSpec.aspectOptions
          : nextSpec.aspectOptions;
      const allowedDurations =
        nextEngineId === "wan22"
          ? variantSpec.durationOptions
          : nextSpec.durationOptions;

      const nextMode = allowedModes.includes(prev.mode)
        ? prev.mode
        : nextSpec.defaultMode;
      const nextAspect = allowedAspects.includes(prev.aspect)
        ? prev.aspect
        : allowedAspects[0];
      const nextDuration =
        nextSpec.kind === "video"
          ? allowedDurations.some((d) => d.value === prev.duration)
            ? prev.duration
            : nextSpec.defaultDuration
          : 0;
      const nextShots = nextSpec.supportsStoryboard ? prev.shotsCount : 1;
      const nextNegative = prev.negativeEdited
        ? prev.negative
        : prev.negative.trim()
          ? buildSmartNegative(nextEngineId, nextMode, prev.category)
          : "";

      return {
        ...prev,
        engine: nextEngineId,
        mode: nextMode,
        aspect: nextAspect,
        duration: nextDuration,
        shotsCount: nextShots,
        cfgScale:
          nextEngineId === "wan22" ? variantSpec.defaultCfg : nextSpec.defaultCfg,
        steps:
          nextEngineId === "wan22"
            ? variantSpec.defaultSteps
            : nextSpec.defaultSteps,
        minimaxResolution:
          nextEngineId === "minimax" && nextDuration === 10
            ? "768P"
            : prev.minimaxResolution || "1080P",
        negative: nextNegative,
      };
    });
  };

  const handleWanVariantChange = (nextVariant: WanVariant) => {
    const variantSpec = WAN_VARIANTS[nextVariant];
    setActivePresetId(null);
    pendingResyncRef.current = true;
    setForm((prev) => {
      const nextMode = variantSpec.modes.includes(prev.mode)
        ? prev.mode
        : "t2v";
      const nextAspect = variantSpec.aspectOptions.includes(prev.aspect)
        ? prev.aspect
        : variantSpec.aspectOptions[0];
      const nextDuration = variantSpec.durationOptions.some(
        (d) => d.value === prev.duration
      )
        ? prev.duration
        : variantSpec.durationOptions[1]?.value ||
          variantSpec.durationOptions[0].value;
      const nextNegative = prev.negativeEdited
        ? prev.negative
        : prev.negative.trim()
          ? buildSmartNegative("wan22", nextMode, prev.category)
          : "";

      return {
        ...prev,
        wanVariant: nextVariant,
        mode: nextMode,
        aspect: nextAspect,
        duration: nextDuration,
        cfgScale: variantSpec.defaultCfg,
        steps: variantSpec.defaultSteps,
        negative: nextNegative,
      };
    });
  };

  const handleModeChange = (nextMode: GenerationMode) => {
    setActivePresetId(null);
    setConfirmNegReset(false);
    pendingResyncRef.current = true;
    setForm((prev) => ({
      ...prev,
      mode: nextMode,
      negative: prev.negativeEdited
        ? prev.negative
        : prev.negative.trim()
          ? buildSmartNegative(prev.engine, nextMode, prev.category)
          : "",
    }));
    if (nextMode === "s2v") {
      showStatus(copy.tipS2vMode, "ok");
    }
  };

  const handleCategoryChange = (nextCategory: string) => {
    setActivePresetId(null);
    setConfirmNegReset(false);
    pendingResyncRef.current = true;
    setForm((prev) => ({
      ...prev,
      category: nextCategory,
      negative: prev.negativeEdited
        ? prev.negative
        : prev.negative.trim()
          ? buildSmartNegative(prev.engine, prev.mode, nextCategory)
          : "",
    }));
  };

  const handleCameraStudioChange = (
    movementLabel: string,
    lensLabel: string
  ) => {
    setActivePresetId(null);
    const combined = composeCameraDirective(movementLabel, lensLabel);
    pendingResyncRef.current = true;
    setForm((prev) => ({
      ...prev,
      cameraMovement: movementLabel,
      cameraLens: lensLabel,
      camera: combined,
    }));
  };

  const handleShotsCountChange = (nextCount: number) => {
    pendingResyncRef.current = true;
    setForm((prev) => ({
      ...prev,
      shotsCount: nextCount,
      shots: buildDefaultShots(nextCount, prev.shots),
    }));
  };

  const handleUpdateShot = (
    index: number,
    field: "camera" | "description",
    value: string
  ) => {
    pendingResyncRef.current = true;
    setForm((prev) => {
      const updated = [...prev.shots];
      if (updated[index]) {
        updated[index] = { ...updated[index], [field]: value };
      }
      return { ...prev, shots: updated };
    });
  };

  const handleTogglePhysicsHint = (hintId: PhysicsHintId) => {
    pendingResyncRef.current = true;
    setForm((prev) => {
      const exists = prev.physicsHints.includes(hintId);
      const nextHints = exists
        ? prev.physicsHints.filter((id) => id !== hintId)
        : [...prev.physicsHints, hintId];
      return { ...prev, physicsHints: nextHints };
    });
  };

  const handleToggleAudioTag = (tagId: AudioTagId) => {
    pendingResyncRef.current = true;
    setForm((prev) => {
      const exists = prev.audioTags.includes(tagId);
      const nextTags = exists
        ? prev.audioTags.filter((id) => id !== tagId)
        : [...prev.audioTags, tagId];
      return { ...prev, audioTags: nextTags };
    });
  };

  const compileAndSave = useCallback(
    (
      overrideState?: StudioFormState,
      customSuccessMsg?: string,
      options?: { preserveStatusBanner?: boolean }
    ) => {
      const base = overrideState || form;
      const target: StudioFormState = base.seedLocked
        ? base
        : { ...base, seed: randomSeed() };

      if (!base.seedLocked && !overrideState) {
        setForm(target);
      }

      const spec = ENGINES[target.engine];

      if (target.engine === "veo") {
        if (!target.subject.trim() && !target.action.trim()) {
          showStatus(copy.errSubject, "err");
          return;
        }
      } else if (
        !target.subject.trim() &&
        !target.action.trim() &&
        !target.environment.trim() &&
        !referenceImage
      ) {
        showStatus(copy.errSubject, "err");
        return;
      }

      const compiled = compileEnginePrompt(target);
      setOutputText(compiled.fullPrompt);
      setPositiveText(compiled.positiveOnly);
      setNegativeText(compiled.negativeOnly);
      setHasCompiledOnce(true);

      const titleBody = (target.subject || target.action || spec.name).slice(
        0,
        48
      );
      const prefixedTitle = `${spec.name}${target.category ? ` · ${target.category.split(" ")[0]}` : ""} — ${titleBody}`;

      const newEntry: HistoryEntry = {
        id: `${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 7)}`,
        engine: target.engine,
        wanVariant: target.engine === "wan22" ? target.wanVariant : undefined,
        mode: target.mode,
        category: target.category,
        shotsCount: spec.kind === "video" ? target.shotsCount : 1,
        title: prefixedTitle,
        time: new Date().toLocaleTimeString([], {
          hour: "2-digit",
          minute: "2-digit",
        }),
        timestamp: Date.now(),
        isFavorite: false,
        prompt: compiled.fullPrompt,
        positiveOnly: compiled.positiveOnly,
        negativeOnly: compiled.negativeOnly,
        stateSnapshot: {
          ...target,
        },
      };

      setHistory((prev) => [newEntry, ...prev].slice(0, 30));

      setSceneChain({
        baseContext: {
          engine: target.engine,
          style: target.style,
          character: target.subject,
          environment: target.environment,
          camera: target.camera,
          lighting: target.light,
          seed: target.seed,
        },
        scenes: [
          {
            id: 1,
            order: 1,
            idea: target.subject.trim() || target.action.trim() || spec.name,
            prompt: compiled.fullPrompt,
            positiveOnly: compiled.positiveOnly,
            negativeOnly: compiled.negativeOnly,
            engine: target.engine,
            createdAt: Date.now(),
            sceneId: "scene_1",
            jobId: `sg_chain_1_${Date.now()}`,
          },
        ],
      });

      if (options?.preserveStatusBanner) {
        return;
      }

      if (customSuccessMsg) {
        showStatus(customSuccessMsg, "ok");
      } else if (target.engine === "wan22" && target.promptFormat === "official") {
        const w = compiled.positiveWords;
        const lengthMsg =
          lang === "ar"
            ? w < 80
              ? `تم البناء (${w} كلمة · المثالي لـ Wan 80–120، يمكنك إضافة تفاصيل للمشهد أو الضوء).`
              : w > 120
                ? `تم البناء (${w} كلمة · فوق 120 كلمة قد يتجاهل Wan التفاصيل الأخيرة، يفضل الاختصار).`
                : `تم البناء (${w} كلمة · طول مثالي لمعيار Wan 2.2).`
            : lang === "fr"
              ? w < 80
                ? `Généré (${w} mots · idéal Wan : 80–120).`
                : w > 120
                  ? `Généré (${w} mots · au-delà de 120, Wan peut ignorer l'excédent).`
                  : `Généré (${w} mots · longueur idéale Wan 2.2).`
              : w < 80
                ? `Built (${w} words · Wan 2.2 sweet spot is 80–120 words).`
                : w > 120
                  ? `Built (${w} words · above 120 words, Wan may attenuate trailing details).`
                  : `Built (${w} words · optimal Wan 2.2 length).`;
        showStatus(lengthMsg, "ok");
      } else {
        showStatus(copy.okGenerated, "ok");
      }
    },
    [copy, form, lang, referenceImage, showStatus]
  );


  const handleToggleFavorite = (id: string) => {
    setHistory((prev) =>
      prev.map((item) =>
        item.id === id ? { ...item, isFavorite: !item.isFavorite } : item
      )
    );
  };

  const handleApplyPreset = (preset: PresetItem) => {
    const spec = ENGINES[preset.engine];
    const nextVariant: WanVariant = preset.data.wanVariant ?? form.wanVariant;
    const variantSpec = WAN_VARIANTS[nextVariant];
    const nextShotsCount =
      preset.data.shotsCount ?? (spec.supportsStoryboard ? 3 : 1);
    const nextMode = preset.data.mode ?? spec.defaultMode;
    const nextCategory = preset.data.category ?? CATEGORIES[0];
    const aspectPool =
      preset.engine === "wan22"
        ? variantSpec.aspectOptions
        : spec.aspectOptions;

    const merged: StudioFormState = {
      ...form,
      ...preset.data,
      engine: preset.engine,
      wanVariant: nextVariant,
      mode: nextMode,
      category: nextCategory,
      aspect:
        preset.data.aspect && aspectPool.includes(preset.data.aspect)
          ? preset.data.aspect
          : aspectPool[0],
      duration: preset.data.duration ?? spec.defaultDuration,
      cfgScale:
        preset.data.cfgScale ??
        (preset.engine === "wan22" ? variantSpec.defaultCfg : spec.defaultCfg),
      steps:
        preset.data.steps ??
        (preset.engine === "wan22" ? variantSpec.defaultSteps : spec.defaultSteps),
      shotsCount: nextShotsCount,
      shots: buildDefaultShots(nextShotsCount),
      seed: form.seedLocked ? form.seed : randomSeed(),
      negative:
        preset.data.negative ??
        buildSmartNegative(preset.engine, nextMode, nextCategory),
      negativeEdited: Boolean(preset.data.negative),
    };

    setConfirmNegReset(false);
    setForm(merged);
    setActivePresetId(preset.id);
    setActiveTab("studio");
    compileAndSave(merged);
  };

  const runAutoSceneDirector = useCallback(
    async (briefText: string, baseState: StudioFormState = form): Promise<StudioFormState | null> => {
      const briefToUse = briefText.trim();
      if (!briefToUse) return null;

      setDirecting(true);
      const controller = new AbortController();
      const timer = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 55_000);

      try {
        let data: {
          category?: string;
          duration?: number;
          subject?: string;
          environment?: string;
          action?: string;
          extra?: string;
          cameraMovement?: string;
          lens?: string;
          lightingMood?: string;
          style?: string;
          physicsHints?: string[];
          audioTags?: string[];
          audioDesign?: string;
          dialogue?: string;
          soundEffects?: string;
          minimaxCameraCommands?: string[];
          shots?: { camera: string; description: string }[];
          negative?: string;
        } = {};

        const baseSpec = ENGINES[baseState.engine];
        const baseDurations =
          baseState.engine === "wan22"
            ? WAN_VARIANTS[baseState.wanVariant].durationOptions
            : baseSpec.durationOptions;
        const isRefMode = baseState.mode === "i2v" || baseState.mode === "flf2v";

        if (baseState.aiProvider === "gemini") {
          const res = await fetch("/api/ai/director", {
            method: "POST",
            headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
            signal: controller.signal,
            body: JSON.stringify({
              brief: briefToUse,
              engine: baseState.engine,
              wanVariant: baseState.wanVariant,
            }),
          });
          const json = await safeReadJsonResponse<{
            error?: string;
            apiStatus?: string;
            retryDelay?: string | null;
            [key: string]: unknown;
          }>(res);
          if (!res.ok) {
            throw new Error(
              formatClientApiError(
                json.error || `HTTP ${res.status}: Auto scene direction failed.`,
                lang,
                {
                  httpStatus: res.status,
                  apiStatus: json.apiStatus,
                  retryDelay: json.retryDelay,
                }
              )
            );
          }
          data = json as typeof data;
        } else {
          const modelName = baseState.ollamaModel.trim() || "llama3.1";
          const sysPrompt = `You are an elite AI Commercial Film Director for ${baseSpec.name}. Convert the user's brief into strictly valid JSON with keys: category, duration (integer 5, 6, 8, or 10), subject, environment, action, extra, cameraMovement, lens, lightingMood, style, physicsHints (array from gravity,fluid,volumetric,subsurface,inertia,caustics), audioTags (array from bass,whoosh,clicks,music,ambient,foley), audioDesign, minimaxCameraCommands (array of 1-2 from Push in,Pull out,Orbit,Tracking shot,Pan left,Pan right,Tilt up,Tilt down,Pedestal up,Zoom in,Zoom out,Static shot), shots (array of 3 objects with camera and description), negative.`;
          const res = await fetch("http://localhost:11434/api/generate", {
            method: "POST",
            headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
            signal: controller.signal,
            body: JSON.stringify({
              model: modelName,
              prompt: `${sysPrompt}\n\nBrief: ${briefToUse}`,
              format: "json",
              stream: false,
            }),
          });
          if (!res.ok) {
            throw new Error(`Ollama error (HTTP ${res.status}) at http://localhost:11434.`);
          }
          const json = await safeReadJsonResponse<{ response?: string }>(res);
          data = safeParseClientJson<typeof data>(json.response || "{}");
        }

        const matchedCategory =
          baseState.category ||
          CATEGORIES.find((c) => c === data.category) ||
          CATEGORIES[0];

        const matchedMovement =
          baseState.cameraMovement ||
          CAMERA_MOVEMENTS.find(
            (m) =>
              (data.cameraMovement || "").toLowerCase().includes(m.id) ||
              (data.cameraMovement || "")
                .toLowerCase()
                .includes(m.label.toLowerCase().split(" ")[0])
          )?.label ||
          CAMERA_MOVEMENTS[0].label;

        const matchedLens =
          baseState.cameraLens ||
          CAMERA_LENSES.find((l) =>
            (data.lens || "").toLowerCase().includes(l.id)
          )?.label ||
          CAMERA_LENSES[1].label;

        const composedCam =
          baseState.camera.trim() ||
          composeCameraDirective(matchedMovement, matchedLens);

        const matchedStyle =
          baseState.style ||
          STYLE_OPTIONS.find((s) =>
            s.toLowerCase().includes((data.style || "").toLowerCase().slice(0, 18))
          ) ||
          data.style ||
          STYLE_OPTIONS[0];

        const validPhysicsIds = new Set<PhysicsHintId>(PHYSICS_HINTS.map((h) => h.id));
        const matchedPhysics =
          baseState.physicsHints.length > 0
            ? baseState.physicsHints
            : Array.isArray(data.physicsHints)
              ? data.physicsHints.filter((id): id is PhysicsHintId =>
                  validPhysicsIds.has(id as PhysicsHintId)
                )
              : (["gravity", "inertia"] as PhysicsHintId[]);

        const validAudioIds = new Set<AudioTagId>(AUDIO_DESIGN_TAGS.map((t) => t.id));
        const matchedAudioTags =
          baseState.audioTags.length > 0
            ? baseState.audioTags
            : baseSpec.kind === "video" && Array.isArray(data.audioTags)
              ? data.audioTags.filter((id): id is AudioTagId =>
                  validAudioIds.has(id as AudioTagId)
                )
              : baseSpec.kind === "video"
                ? (["ambient"] as AudioTagId[])
                : [];

        const validHailuoCmds = new Set(HAILUO_OFFICIAL_COMMANDS.map((c) => c.cmd));
        const matchedHailuoCmds =
          baseState.minimaxCameraCommands.length > 0
            ? baseState.minimaxCameraCommands
            : Array.isArray(data.minimaxCameraCommands)
              ? data.minimaxCameraCommands
                  .filter((c) => validHailuoCmds.has(c))
                  .slice(0, 3)
              : [];

        let nextDuration = baseState.duration;
        if (baseSpec.kind === "video" && baseDurations.length > 0) {
          const requested = Number(data.duration) || baseState.duration;
          const closest = baseDurations.reduce((best, opt) =>
            Math.abs(opt.value - requested) < Math.abs(best.value - requested)
              ? opt
              : best
          );
          nextDuration = closest.value;
        }

        const nextShotsCount = baseSpec.supportsStoryboard ? 3 : 1;
        const userEnteredShots = baseState.shots.some(
          (s) => s.camera.trim() || s.description.trim()
        );
        const mappedShots = userEnteredShots
          ? baseState.shots
          : buildDefaultShots(
              nextShotsCount,
              Array.isArray(data.shots)
                ? data.shots.map((s, idx) => ({
                    id: idx + 1,
                    role: `Shot ${idx + 1}`,
                    camera: s.camera || composedCam,
                    description: s.description || data.action || "",
                  }))
                : []
            );

        const nextForm: StudioFormState = {
          ...baseState,
          category: matchedCategory,
          duration: nextDuration,
          subject: data.subject || baseState.subject,
          environment: isRefMode
            ? baseState.environment
            : baseState.environment.trim() || data.environment || "",
          action: baseState.action.trim() || data.action || "",
          extra: baseState.extra.trim() || data.extra || "",
          cameraMovement: matchedMovement,
          cameraLens: matchedLens,
          camera: composedCam,
          light: baseState.light.trim() || data.lightingMood || "",
          style: matchedStyle,
          physicsHints: matchedPhysics,
          includeAudio: baseSpec.kind === "video",
          audioTags: matchedAudioTags,
          audioDescription:
            baseState.audioDescription.trim() || data.audioDesign || "",
          veoDialogue:
            baseState.veoDialogue.trim() || data.dialogue || "",
          veoSoundEffects:
            baseState.veoSoundEffects.trim() ||
            data.soundEffects ||
            data.audioDesign ||
            "",
          minimaxCameraCommands: matchedHailuoCmds,
          shotsCount: nextShotsCount,
          shots: mappedShots,
          negative: !baseSpec.supportsNegative
            ? ""
            : baseState.negative.trim() ||
              data.negative ||
              buildSmartNegative(baseState.engine, baseState.mode, matchedCategory),
          negativeEdited: Boolean(baseState.negativeEdited || data.negative),
        };

        setConfirmNegReset(false);
        setActivePresetId(null);
        setForm(nextForm);
        return nextForm;
      } catch (err: unknown) {
        const raw =
          err instanceof DOMException && err.name === "AbortError"
            ? "Auto scene direction timed out after 55s."
            : err instanceof Error
              ? err.message
              : "Auto scene direction failed.";
        showStatus(formatClientApiError(raw, lang), "err");
        return null;
      } finally {
        clearTimeout(timer);
        setDirecting(false);
      }
    },
    [form, lang, showStatus]
  );

  const handleSmartBuild = useCallback(async () => {
    const needsAutoDirection =
      Boolean(form.subject.trim()) &&
      (form.mode === "t2v" || form.mode === "t2i"
        ? !form.environment.trim() || !form.action.trim()
        : !form.action.trim());

    if (needsAutoDirection) {
      const directed = await runAutoSceneDirector(form.subject, form);
      if (directed) {
        compileAndSave(directed, copy.okDirectorDone);
        return;
      }
      if (form.subject.trim() || referenceImage) {
        compileAndSave(form, undefined, { preserveStatusBanner: true });
      }
      return;
    }

    compileAndSave();
